local LIGHT_BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(135, 206, 250)

local function protectGui(gui)
    if not gui then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(protect_gui) == "function" then
            protect_gui(gui)
        elseif syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(gui)
        elseif typeof(hide_in_gcoregui) == "function" then
            hide_in_gcoregui(gui)
        end
    end)
end
local HubFonts = {
    mode = "LuckyestGuys",
    labelRef = nil,
    roots = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    originalTexts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    originalTextSizes = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    pendingTextSize = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    textSizeTracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    pendingText = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    uppercased = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
    textTracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
}
function HubFonts.isTextObject(obj)
    return obj and (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox"))
end
function HubFonts.setTextInternally(obj, text)
    if obj.Text ~= text then
        HubFonts.pendingText[obj] = text
        obj.Text = text
    end
end
function HubFonts.transformText(mode, text)
    text = tostring(text or "")
    if mode == "GothamBlack" or mode == "NORMAL FONT V1" then
        return string.upper(text)
    elseif mode == "NORMAL FONT V2" then
        local lowerText = string.lower(text)
        return (lowerText:gsub("%S+", function(word)
            return word:gsub("^(%p*)(%a)", function(prefix, firstLetter)
                return prefix .. string.upper(firstLetter)
            end)
        end))
    elseif mode == "NORMAL FONT V3" then
        return string.lower(text)
    end
    return text
end
function HubFonts.trackTextObject(obj)
    if HubFonts.originalTexts[obj] == nil then
        HubFonts.originalTexts[obj] = obj.Text
    end
    if HubFonts.originalTextSizes[obj] == nil then
        HubFonts.originalTextSizes[obj] = obj.TextSize
    end
    if not HubFonts.textSizeTracked[obj] then
        HubFonts.textSizeTracked[obj] = true
        obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextSize"):Connect(function()
            local currentSize = tonumber(obj.TextSize)
            if currentSize == HubFonts.pendingTextSize[obj] then
                HubFonts.pendingTextSize[obj] = nil
                return
            end
            HubFonts.pendingTextSize[obj] = nil
            if currentSize then HubFonts.originalTextSizes[obj] = currentSize end
        end)
    end
    if HubFonts.textTracked[obj] then
        return
    end
    HubFonts.textTracked[obj] = true
    obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        local currentText = tostring(obj.Text or "")
        if HubFonts.pendingText[obj] == currentText then
            HubFonts.pendingText[obj] = nil
            return
        end
        HubFonts.pendingText[obj] = nil
        HubFonts.originalTexts[obj] = currentText
        local transformedText = HubFonts.transformText(HubFonts.mode, currentText)
        if transformedText ~= currentText then
            HubFonts.uppercased[obj] = true
            HubFonts.setTextInternally(obj, transformedText)
        else
            HubFonts.uppercased[obj] = nil
        end
    end)
end
function HubFonts.applyToObject(obj)
    if not HubFonts.isTextObject(obj) then
        return
    end
    HubFonts.trackTextObject(obj)
    local originalText = tostring(HubFonts.originalTexts[obj] or obj.Text or "")
    local transformedText = HubFonts.transformText(HubFonts.mode, originalText)
    if transformedText ~= originalText then
        if obj.Text ~= transformedText then
            HubFonts.setTextInternally(obj, transformedText)
        end
        HubFonts.uppercased[obj] = true
    else
        if HubFonts.uppercased[obj] then
            HubFonts.setTextInternally(obj, originalText)
        end
        HubFonts.uppercased[obj] = nil
    end
    local normalFontMode = HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V1" or HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V2"
        or HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V3"
    local baseTextSize = tonumber(HubFonts.originalTextSizes[obj]) or tonumber(obj.TextSize) or 12
    if normalFontMode then
        local increase = math.max(1, math.floor(baseTextSize * 0.12 + 0.5))
        local targetTextSize = baseTextSize + increase
        if tonumber(obj.TextSize) ~= targetTextSize then
            HubFonts.pendingTextSize[obj] = targetTextSize
            obj.TextSize = targetTextSize
        end
    elseif tonumber(obj.TextSize) ~= baseTextSize then
        HubFonts.pendingTextSize[obj] = baseTextSize
        obj.TextSize = baseTextSize
    end
    local chosenFont
    if HubFonts.mode == "GothamBlack" then
        chosenFont = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    elseif HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V1" or HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V2"
        or HubFonts.mode == "NORMAL FONT V3" then
        -- Perfil normal com texto maior e traços mais encorpados.
        chosenFont = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    elseif HubFonts.mode == "GOTHAM UI" then
        chosenFont = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    elseif HubFonts.mode == "Gotham" then
        if obj:IsA("TextBox") then
            chosenFont = Enum.Font.Gotham
        elseif obj:IsA("TextButton") then
            chosenFont = obj.TextSize >= 20 and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.GothamBold
        else
            local text = tostring(obj.Text or "")
            local hasLetters = text:match("%a") ~= nil
            local isHeading = obj.TextSize >= 22 or (hasLetters and text == string.upper(text))
            chosenFont = isHeading and Enum.Font.GothamBlack or Enum.Font.Gotham
        end
    else
        chosenFont = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    end
    pcall(function()
        obj.Font = chosenFont
    end)
end
function HubFonts.apply()
    for root in pairs(HubFonts.roots) do
        if root and root.Parent then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                HubFonts.applyToObject(descendant)
            end
        else
            HubFonts.roots[root] = nil
        end
    end
    if HubFonts.labelRef and HubFonts.labelRef.Parent then
        local displayMode = HubFonts.mode
        if HubFonts.mode == "GothamBlack" then
            displayMode = string.upper(displayMode)
        end
        HubFonts.setTextInternally(HubFonts.labelRef, displayMode)
    end
end
function HubFonts.register(root)
    if not root or HubFonts.roots[root] then
        return
    end
    HubFonts.roots[root] = true
    root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        if not HubFonts.isTextObject(descendant) then
            return
        end
        task.defer(function()
            if descendant.Parent and descendant:IsDescendantOf(root) then
                HubFonts.applyToObject(descendant)
            end
        end)
    end)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        HubFonts.applyToObject(descendant)
    end
end
local function parentGui(gui)
    if not gui then
        return
    end
    protectGui(gui)
    local ok = false
    if _gethui then
        ok = pcall(function()
            gui.Parent = _gethui()
        end)
    end
    if not ok or not gui.Parent then
        ok = pcall(function()
            gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)
    end
    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
            if plr then
                gui.Parent = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            end
        end)
    end
    HubFonts.register(gui)
end
local _isfile = (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile)
    or (syn and syn.isfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().isfile)
    or function(p)
        local ok, data = pcall(function()
            return readfile(p)
        end)
        return ok and data ~= nil
    end
local _readfile = (typeof(readfile) == "function" and readfile)
    or (syn and syn.readfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().readfile)
    or function()
        return nil
    end
local _writefile = (typeof(writefile) == "function" and writefile)
    or (syn and syn.writefile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().writefile)
    or function() end
isfile, readfile, writefile = _isfile, _readfile, _writefile
local function destroyOldGui(name)
    if not name then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if _gethui then
            local h = _gethui()
            local o = h and h:FindFirstChild(name)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local o = cg:FindFirstChild(name)
        if o then
            o:Destroy()
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
        if plr then
            local pg = plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                local o = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
                if o then
                    o:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function candyColor(rgb)
    return Color3.fromRGB(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3])
end
local function CandyApplyCustomSky(_mode)
    -- Sky Theme removido: não cria Sky, Atmosphere ou Clouds e não altera Lighting.
    for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if child:GetAttribute(CANDY_SKY_TAG) then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then
        for _, child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do
            if child:GetAttribute(CANDY_SKY_TAG) then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end
end
local function restoreCharacterAnimations(character)
    local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if not animate then return end
    local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end) end
    end
    animate.Disabled = true
    task.wait()
    animate.Disabled = false
end
local function applyAnimPack(name)
    animPackName = name or animPackName or "OFF"
    local char = LP and LP.Character
    local animate = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if not animate then return end
    if not animPackEnabled or animPackName == "OFF" or not animPacks[animPackName] then
        restoreCharacterAnimations(char)
        return
    end
    local pack = animPacks[animPackName]
    local map = { {"walk","WalkAnim","WalkAnim"}, {"run","RunAnim","RunAnim"}, {"jump","JumpAnim","JumpAnim"}, {"fall","FallAnim","FallAnim"}, {"climb","ClimbAnim","ClimbAnim"}, {"idle","Animation1","Animation1"}, {"idle","Animation2","Animation2"} }
    for _, item in ipairs(map) do
        local parent = animate:FindFirstChild(item[1])
        local anim = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(item[2])
        if parent and not anim then anim = Instance.new("Animation"); anim.Name=item[2]; anim.Parent=parent end
        if anim and pack[item[3]] then anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://"..tostring(pack[item[3]]) end
    end
    restoreCharacterAnimations(char)
end
local function applyStretchVision(enabled)
    stretchVisionEnabled = enabled == true
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(stretchVisionBindName) end)
    if stretchVisionEnabled then
        pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(stretchVisionBindName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function()
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if camera then camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,0,0,1,0,0,0,0.8,0,0,0,1) end
            end)
        end)
    end
end
-- Stretch Rez V2: alternate camera transform, mutually exclusive with Stretch Vision.
do
    local bindName="S2StretchRezV2"
    _G._S2SetStretchRezV2=function(on)
        _G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled=on==true
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(bindName) end)
        if _G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled then
            if stretchVisionEnabled then
                applyStretchVision(false)
                if stretchVisionSetVisual then stretchVisionSetVisual(false) end
            end
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(bindName,Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value+1,function()
                local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then cam.CFrame=cam.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,0,0,1,0,0,0,0.65,0,0,0,1) end
            end)
        end
    end
end

function getTheme()
    return GUI_THEMES[currentGuiTheme] or GUI_THEMES["Midnight"]
end
function _G._S2DarkenHubColor(color, factor)
    return Color3.new(color.R * factor, color.G * factor, color.B * factor)
end
function _G._S2LightenHubColor(color, amount)
    amount = math.clamp(tonumber(amount) or 0, 0, 1)
    return Color3.new(
        color.R + (1 - color.R) * amount,
        color.G + (1 - color.G) * amount,
        color.B + (1 - color.B) * amount
    )
end
function _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
    palette = palette or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    return _G._S2DarkenHubColor(palette.primary, 0.30)
end
function _G._S2GetToggleOnKnobColor(palette)
    palette = palette or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    return _G._S2DarkenHubColor(palette.primary, 0.64)
end
function _G._S2GetToggleOffTrackColor()
    if background2Index == 2 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 236)
    end
    return getTheme().BG
end
function _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(palette)
    palette = palette or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    local color = palette.primary
    if background2Index == 2 then
        return Color3.new(
            1 - (1 - color.R) * 0.25,
            1 - (1 - color.G) * 0.25,
            1 - (1 - color.B) * 0.25
        )
    end
    return Color3.new(color.R * 0.28, color.G * 0.28, color.B * 0.28)
end
local function applyHubColor(index)
    hubColorIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1), 1, #HUB_COLOR_PALETTES)
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex]
    if not palette then return end
    local adaptiveOutlineColor = _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(palette)
    local function isHubPrimary(color)
        if color == LIGHT_BLUE then return true end
        for _, colors in ipairs(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES) do
            if color == colors.primary then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isHubSecondary(color)
        if color == LIGHT_BLUE2 then return true end
        for _, colors in ipairs(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES) do
            if color == colors.secondary then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function recolor(root)
        if not root then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary") == true then
                        obj.TextColor3 = palette.secondary
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary") == true then
                        obj.TextColor3 = palette.primary
                    elseif isHubPrimary(obj.TextColor3) then
                        obj.TextColor3 = palette.primary
                    elseif isHubSecondary(obj.TextColor3) then
                        obj.TextColor3 = palette.secondary
                    end
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2HubTextStrokePrimary") == true then
                        obj.TextStrokeColor3 = palette.primary
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("Frame") or obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = adaptiveOutlineColor
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubToggleTrack") == true then
                        local toggleOn = obj:GetAttribute("S2ToggleOn") == true
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = toggleOn and _G._S2DarkenHubColor(palette.primary, 0.35)
                            or _G._S2GetToggleOffTrackColor()
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubToggleKnob") == true then
                        local toggleOn = obj:GetAttribute("S2ToggleOn") == true
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = toggleOn and _G._S2GetToggleOnKnobColor(palette)
                            or _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary") == true then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.primary
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary") == true then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.secondary
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2MobileButton") == true then
                        if obj:GetAttribute("S2MobileButtonOn") == true then
                            obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.primary
                        end
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2ToggleOn") == true then
                        if obj.Name == "S2ToggleDot" or obj:GetAttribute("S2ToggleDot") == true then
                            obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.secondary
                        else
                            obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.primary
                        end
                    elseif isHubPrimary(obj.BackgroundColor3) then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.primary
                    elseif isHubSecondary(obj.BackgroundColor3) then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = palette.secondary
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("UIGradient") then
                    local parent = obj.Parent
                    if parent and (parent:GetAttribute("S2HubColorText") == true or parent:IsA("TextLabel")) then
                        obj.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, palette.secondary),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, palette.primary),
                            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, palette.secondary),
                        })
                    end
                elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2HubToggleTrackStroke") == true then
                        local parent = obj.Parent
                        local toggleOn = parent and parent:GetAttribute("S2ToggleOn") == true
                        obj.Color = toggleOn and _G._S2GetToggleOnKnobColor(palette)
                            or _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true then
                        obj.Color = adaptiveOutlineColor
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary") == true then
                        obj.Color = palette.primary
                    elseif obj:GetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary") == true then
                        obj.Color = palette.secondary
                    elseif isHubPrimary(obj.Color) then
                        obj.Color = palette.primary
                    elseif isHubSecondary(obj.Color) then
                        obj.Color = palette.secondary
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    recolor(mainGuiRef)
    recolor(stealBarFrame and stealBarFrame.Parent)
    recolor(mobGuiRef)
    local introRoots = {}
    pcall(function() table.insert(introRoots, game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("S2Intro")) end)
    pcall(function() table.insert(introRoots, LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("S2Intro")) end)
    for _, introRoot in ipairs(introRoots) do recolor(introRoot) end
    if type(applySpeedIndicatorTheme) == "function" then
        pcall(applySpeedIndicatorTheme)
    end
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP and LP.Character
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            recolor(head)
        end
    end)
    if applyStealBarBackground then
        applyStealBarBackground(background2Index)
    end
end

local function loadBtnPositions()
    local data = {}
    pcall(function()
        local raw = readfile(MOB_POS_FILE)
        if raw and #raw > 2 then
            local decoded = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
            if type(decoded) == "table" then
                data = decoded
            end
        end
    end)
    if not data or next(data) == nil then
        pcall(function()
            local raw = readfile("000_duels.json")
            if raw then
                local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.btnPositions) == "table" and next(cfg.btnPositions) ~= nil then
                    data = cfg.btnPositions
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    return data or {}
end
local function saveBtnPositions()
    if not mobGuiRef then
        return
    end
    local out = loadBtnPositions() or {}
    for _, child in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") and child:GetAttribute("BtnKey") then
            local key = child:GetAttribute("BtnKey")
            out[key] = { xs = 0, xo = child.Position.X.Offset, ys = 0, yo = child.Position.Y.Offset }
        end
    end
    if next(out) == nil then
        return
    end
    if writefile then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(MOB_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(out))
        end)
    end
    if writefile and isfile and isfile("000_duels.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(readfile("000_duels.json"))
            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.btnPositions = out
                writefile("000_duels.json", HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
            end
        end)
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        pcall(saveBtnPositions)
    end
end)
local function loadGuiPositions()
    local data = {}
    pcall(function()
        local raw = readfile(GUI_POS_FILE)
        if raw and #raw > 2 then
            local decoded = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
            if type(decoded) == "table" then
                data = decoded
            end
        end
    end)
    if not data or next(data) == nil then
        pcall(function()
            local raw = readfile("000_duels.json")
            if raw then
                local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.guiPositions) == "table" and next(cfg.guiPositions) ~= nil then
                    data = cfg.guiPositions
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    if not data or next(data) == nil then
        pcall(function()
            local raw = readfile("000_duels_config.json")
            if raw then
                local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.guiPositions) == "table" and next(cfg.guiPositions) ~= nil then
                    data = cfg.guiPositions
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    return data or {}
end
local function mergeGuiPosition(key, xs, xo, ys, yo)
    if not key then
        return
    end
    local data = loadGuiPositions()
    local prev = data[key] or {}
    local xsN = tonumber(xs)
    local xoN = tonumber(xo)
    local ysN = tonumber(ys)
    local yoN = tonumber(yo)
    data[key] = {
        xs = xsN ~= nil and xsN or (prev.xs or 0),
        xo = xoN ~= nil and xoN or (prev.xo or 0),
        ys = ysN ~= nil and ysN or (prev.ys or 0),
        yo = yoN ~= nil and yoN or (prev.yo or 0),
    }
    if writefile then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(GUI_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(data))
        end)
    end
    if writefile and isfile and isfile("000_duels.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(readfile("000_duels.json"))
            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.guiPositions = data
                writefile("000_duels.json", HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
            end
        end)
    end
    if writefile and isfile and isfile("000_duels_config.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(readfile("000_duels_config.json"))
            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.guiPositions = data
                writefile("000_duels_config.json", HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function saveGuiPositions()
    if stealBarFrame then
        local p = stealBarFrame.Position
        mergeGuiPosition("_stealBar", p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
    end
    if mainGuiRef then
        local outer = mainGuiRef:FindFirstChild("OuterBorder")
        if outer and outer:GetAttribute("S2HubCollapseTweening") ~= true then
            local p = outer.Position
            local collapseCorrection = tonumber(outer:GetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection")) or 0
            mergeGuiPosition("_hub", p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + collapseCorrection)
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(3)
        pcall(saveGuiPositions)
    end
end)

local function addShimmerToLabel(lbl, color1, color2)
    local gr = Instance.new("UIGradient", lbl)
    gr.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color1 or LIGHT_BLUE),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color2 or LIGHT_BLUE),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color1 or LIGHT_BLUE),
    })
    gr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.3, 0),
    })
    return gr
end
-- S2 visual toggles: Vivid Graphics and camera Motion Blur.
do
    local cc, bloom, blur, blurConn, lastCF, blurValue
    local savedLighting
    local function setVivid(on)
        _G._S2VividGraphicsEnabled = on == true
        if on then
            if not savedLighting then
                savedLighting={Brightness=Lighting.Brightness, ExposureCompensation=Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
                    Ambient=Lighting.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient=Lighting.OutdoorAmbient}
            end
            Lighting.Brightness=math.max(Lighting.Brightness,2)
            Lighting.ExposureCompensation=math.max(Lighting.ExposureCompensation,0.05)
            Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120)
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(150,150,150)
            cc=cc and cc.Parent and cc or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2VividColor") or Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name="S2VividColor"; cc.Saturation=0.18; cc.Contrast=0.08; cc.Brightness=0.02; cc.Parent=Lighting
            bloom=bloom and bloom.Parent and bloom or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2VividBloom") or Instance.new("BloomEffect")
            bloom.Name="S2VividBloom"; bloom.Intensity=0.2; bloom.Size=24; bloom.Threshold=0.9; bloom.Parent=Lighting
        else
            cc=cc or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2VividColor")
            bloom=bloom or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2VividBloom")
            if cc then cc:Destroy(); cc=nil end
            if bloom then bloom:Destroy(); bloom=nil end
            if savedLighting then
                for k,v in pairs(savedLighting) do pcall(function() Lighting[k]=v end) end
                savedLighting=nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function setMotionBlur(on)
        _G._S2MotionBlurEnabled=on==true
        if blurConn then pcall(function() blurConn:Disconnect() end); blurConn=nil end
        if not on then
            blur=blur or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2MotionBlur")
            if blur and blur.Parent then blur.Size=0; blur.Enabled=false end
            blurValue=0; return
        end
        blur=blur and blur.Parent and blur or Lighting:FindFirstChild("S2MotionBlur") or Instance.new("BlurEffect")
        blur.Name="S2MotionBlur"; blur.Size=0; blur.Enabled=true; blur.Parent=Lighting
        local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera; lastCF=cam and cam.CFrame; blurValue=0
        blurConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            local c=workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not c then return end
            if lastCF then
                local angle=math.deg(math.acos(math.clamp(c.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(lastCF.LookVector),-1,1)))
                local target=math.min(angle*20,7)
                blurValue=blurValue+(target-blurValue)*math.min(1,(dt or 0.016)*10)
                if blur and blur.Parent then blur.Size=blurValue end
            end
            lastCF=c.CFrame
        end)
    end
    _G._S2SetVividGraphics=setVivid
    _G._S2SetMotionBlur=setMotionBlur
end

fovConn = nil
local function applyFOV()
    if fovConn then
        fovConn:Disconnect()
    end
    fovConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            cam.FieldOfView = fovValue
        end
    end)
end
applyFOV()
do
    local timerBillboard = nil
    local timerLabel = nil
    local timerActive = false
    local timerConn = nil
    local ragdollCheckConn = nil
    local lastRagdollState = false
    local function createTimerBillboard()
        if timerBillboard then
            timerBillboard:Destroy()
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            return
        end
        timerBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        timerBillboard.Name = "S2RagdollTimer"
        timerBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 50)
        timerBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5.8, 0)
        timerBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        timerBillboard.MaxDistance = 100
        timerBillboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        timerBillboard.Parent = head
        HubFonts.register(timerBillboard)
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        local bgFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        bgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        bgFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bgFrame.Parent = timerBillboard
        Instance.new("UICorner", bgFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", bgFrame)
        stroke:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
        stroke.Color = palette.primary
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Transparency = 0.3
        timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        timerLabel.Text = "3.00"
        timerLabel:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
        timerLabel.TextColor3 = palette.primary
        timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        timerLabel.TextSize = 28
        timerLabel.TextScaled = true
        timerLabel.ZIndex = 2
        timerLabel.Parent = bgFrame
        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
    end
    local function updateTimerDisplay(time, isReady)
        if not timerLabel or not timerBillboard then
            return
        end
        if isReady then
            local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
            timerLabel.Text = "READY TO STEAL!"
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = palette.secondary
            timerLabel.TextSize = 18
            timerLabel.TextScaled = false
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = palette.secondary
                    stroke.Thickness = 3
                    stroke.Transparency = 0
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 30, 10)
            end
        else
            local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
            timerLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", time)
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = palette.primary
            timerLabel.TextSize = 28
            timerLabel.TextScaled = true
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = palette.primary
                    stroke.Thickness = 2
                    stroke.Transparency = 0.3
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end
    end
    local function startRagdollTimer()
        if timerActive then
            return
        end
        if not timerBillboard or not timerBillboard.Parent then
            createTimerBillboard()
            if not timerBillboard then
                return
            end
        end
        timerActive = true
        timerBillboard.Enabled = true
        local startTime = tick()
        local duration = 3.0
        if timerConn then
            timerConn:Disconnect()
        end
        timerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not timerActive then
                timerConn:Disconnect()
                timerConn = nil
                return
            end
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then
                timerActive = false
                if timerBillboard then
                    timerBillboard.Enabled = false
                end
                if timerConn then
                    timerConn:Disconnect()
                    timerConn = nil
                end
                return
            end
            local elapsed = tick() - startTime
            local remaining = math.max(duration - elapsed, 0)
            if remaining <= 0 then
                timerActive = false
                updateTimerDisplay(0, true)
                if timerConn then
                    timerConn:Disconnect()
                    timerConn = nil
                end
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    if timerBillboard then
                        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
                    end
                    timerActive = false
                end)
                return
            end
            updateTimerDisplay(remaining, false)
        end)
    end
    local function checkRagdoll()
        if ragdollCheckConn then
            return
        end
        ragdollCheckConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then
                return
            end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then
                return
            end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            local isRagdolled = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            if isRagdolled and not lastRagdollState and not timerActive then
                if not timerBillboard or not timerBillboard.Parent then
                    createTimerBillboard()
                end
                startRagdollTimer()
            end
            lastRagdollState = isRagdolled
        end)
    end
    task.spawn(checkRagdoll)
    playIntroAnimation = function()
        if introEnabled == false then
            return
        end
        local _th = (type(getTheme) == "function" and getTheme()) or nil
        C = C or {}

            C.accentDim = _th.ACCENT2 or _th.GRAY or Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 230)
            C.topTitle = _th.WHITE or C.accent
            C.topSub = _th.GRAY or C.accentDim
        end
        local Themes = Themes or { [1] = { gradA = C.accent, gradB = C.accentDim, gradC = C.accent } }
        local currentThemeIdx = currentThemeIdx or 1
        local Players2 = game:GetService("Players")
        local TweenService2 = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local SoundService2 = game:GetService("SoundService")
        local Debris2 = game:GetService("Debris")
        local LP2 = Players2.LocalPlayer
        local guiParent = LP2:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        pcall(function()
            local old = guiParent:FindFirstChild("S2Intro")
            if old then
                old:Destroy()
            end
            local old2 = (gethui and gethui() or nil)
            if old2 then
                local o = old2:FindFirstChild("S2Intro")
                if o then
                    o:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end)
        local hubPalette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        local themeAccent = hubPalette.primary
        local themeDim = hubPalette.secondary
        local themeText = hubPalette.primary

        local themeGradB = (Themes and Themes[currentThemeIdx] and Themes[currentThemeIdx].gradC) or themeText
        local themeGradC = (Themes and Themes[currentThemeIdx] and Themes[currentThemeIdx].gradB) or themeDim
        local splashGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

                splashGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")

        task.spawn(function()

                    lastRing = t

        local orbitR = 70
        local calciumExit = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.5, 0)
        local duelsExit = UDim2.new(0.5, 160, 0.5, 0)
        local animConn = nil
        local orbitAngle = 0
        local orbitActive = false
        local function stopIntroAnim()
            orbitActive = false
            if animConn then
                pcall(function()
                    animConn:Disconnect()
                end)
                animConn = nil
            end
        end
        local function posOnOrbit(angle)
            local ox = math.cos(angle) * orbitR
            local oy = math.sin(angle) * (orbitR * 0.85)
            return UDim2.new(0.5, ox, 0.5, oy)
        end
        local function startHoldAnimations()
            stopIntroAnim()
            orbitActive = true
            orbitAngle = math.pi
            local t0 = tick()
            animConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                if introFinished or not orbitActive then
                    stopIntroAnim()
                    return
                end
                local t = tick() - t0
                orbitAngle = orbitAngle + dt * 0.85
                calciumLbl.Position = posOnOrbit(orbitAngle)
                duelsLbl.Position = posOnOrbit(orbitAngle + math.pi)
                local pulse = 26 + math.sin(t * 4) * 3
                orb.Size = UDim2.new(0, pulse, 0, pulse)
                local gPulse = 42 + math.sin(t * 4) * 6
                orbGlow.Size = UDim2.new(0, gPulse, 0, gPulse)
                orbGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78 + math.sin(t * 3) * 0.08
            end)
        end
        local introSound = nil
        local introFinished = false
        local introCompleteEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
        local INTRO_DURATION = 15
        local function playSfx(id, pitch, vol, parent, delay)
            task.delay(delay or 0, function()
                if introFinished then
                    return
                end
                local s = Instance.new("Sound")
                s.SoundId = id
                s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
                s.Volume = vol
                s.Parent = parent
                s.RollOffMaxDistance = 0
                s:Play()
                Debris2:AddItem(s, 3)
            end)
        end
        local function playWhistle()
            local WHISTLE_ID = "rbxassetid://4612414100"
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 2.2, 0.7, SoundService2, 0)
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 1.7, 0.8, SoundService2, 0.07)
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 1.2, 0.9, SoundService2, 0.15)
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 0.85, 0.9, SoundService2, 0.24)
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 0.55, 0.7, SoundService2, 0.34)
            playSfx(WHISTLE_ID, 0.3, 1.0, SoundService2, 0.5)
        end
        local function stopIntroMusic()
            pcall(function()
                if introSound then
                    introSound:Stop()
                    introSound:Destroy()
                    introSound = nil
                end
            end)
        end
        local function finishIntro()
            if introFinished then
                return
            end
            introFinished = true
            stopIntroAnim()
            pcall(function()
                if introSound then
                    TweenService2:Create(introSound, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Volume = 0 }):Play()
                    task.delay(0.45, function()
                        stopIntroMusic()
                    end)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                TweenService2:Create(
                    calciumLbl,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                    { Position = calciumExit, TextTransparency = 1 }
                ):Play()
                TweenService2:Create(
                    duelsLbl,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                    { Position = duelsExit, TextTransparency = 1 }
                ):Play()
                TweenService2
                    :Create(orb, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6) })
                    :Play()
                TweenService2:Create(orbGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                local closeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                for _, fr in ipairs({ scopeClip, housing, bevel, scopeRing2 }) do
                    TweenService2
                        :Create(fr, closeInfo, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) })
                        :Play()
                end
                TweenService2:Create(overlay, TweenInfo.new(0.55), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                TweenService2:Create(tapHint, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                TweenService2:Create(loadingLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            end)
            task.delay(0.65, function()
                pcall(function()
                    if splashGui and splashGui.Parent then
                        splashGui:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    introCompleteEvent:Fire()
                end)
            end)
        end
        skipZone.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            finishIntro()
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local intros = {
                "https://files.catbox.moe/8oogdu.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/8ch5qz.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/gm26vl.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/m5kxm3.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/zuid5n.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/z6eqnt.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/t0nlhv.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/mthg31.mp3",
                "https://files.catbox.moe/ddnbup.mp3",
            }
            local selectedMusic = math.clamp(tonumber(selectedIntroMusic) or 1, 1, #intros)
            local url = intros[selectedMusic]
            local cacheName = "S2HubIntro_" .. tostring(selectedMusic) .. ".mp3"
            local function getRequestFn()
                return (syn and syn.request)
                    or (http and http.request)
                    or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
                    or request
                    or http_request
                    or (http and http.Request)
                    or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request))
            end
            local function tryGetCustomAsset(path)
                local asset = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if getcustomasset then
                        asset = getcustomasset(path)
                    end
                end)
                if asset and asset ~= "" then
                    return asset
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if syn and syn.getcustomasset then
                        asset = syn.getcustomasset(path)
                    end
                end)
                if asset and asset ~= "" then
                    return asset
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if getcustomasset then
                        asset = getcustomasset(path, true)
                    end
                end)
                if asset and asset ~= "" then
                    return asset
                end
                return nil
            end
            local function getAssetId()
                local hasCache = false
                pcall(function()
                    if isfile(cacheName) then
                        hasCache = true
                    end
                end)
                if hasCache then
                    local asset = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
                    if asset then
                        return asset
                    end
                end
                local content = nil
                pcall(function()
                    local req = getRequestFn()
                    if req then
                        local res = req({ Url = url, Method = "GET", Headers = { ["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0" } })
                        if type(res) == "table" then
                            content = res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data
                        elseif type(res) == "string" then
                            content = res
                        end
                    end
                end)
                if not content or #content == 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        content = game:HttpGet(url)
                    end)
                end
                if not content or #content == 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        if game.HttpGetAsync then
                            content = game:HttpGetAsync(url)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if content and #content > 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        writefile(cacheName, content)
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    local asset = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
                    if asset then
                        return asset
                    end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    asset = tryGetCustomAsset(cacheName)
                    if asset then
                        return asset
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end
            local INTRO_MUSIC_ID = "rbxassetid://92674033980537"
            local INTRO_MUSIC_START = 5
            local assetId = INTRO_MUSIC_ID
            if introFinished then
                return
            end
            if introMusicEnabled ~= false and assetId and type(assetId) == "string" and #assetId > 0 then
                pcall(function()
                    introSound = Instance.new("Sound")
                    introSound.Name = "S2IntroMusic"
                    introSound.SoundId = assetId
                    introSound.PlaybackSpeed = 1
                    introSound.Volume = 1
                    introSound.Looped = false
                    local parentOk = pcall(function()
                        introSound.Parent = SoundService2
                    end)
                    if not parentOk then
                        pcall(function()
                            introSound.Parent = workspace
                        end)
                    end
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while introSound and introSound.Parent and not introSound.IsLoaded and (tick() - t0) < 3 do
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        if introFinished then
                            return
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            if introFinished then
                return
            end
            pcall(function()
                loadingLbl.Text = ""
                loadingLbl.Visible = false
                if introSound and introSound.Parent then
                    introSound.TimePosition = INTRO_MUSIC_START
                    introSound:Play()
                end
            end)
            pcall(playWhistle)
            local function zeroScope(frame)
                frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            end
            zeroScope(scopeClip)
            zeroScope(housing)
            zeroScope(bevel)
            zeroScope(scopeRing2)
            local openInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            TweenService2:Create(
                housing,
                openInfo,
                {
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, SCOPE_D + TUBE * 2, 0, SCOPE_D + TUBE * 2),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(SCOPE_D / 2 + TUBE), 0.5, -(SCOPE_D / 2 + TUBE)),
                }
            ):Play()
            TweenService2:Create(
                bevel,
                openInfo,
                {
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, SCOPE_D + 14, 0, SCOPE_D + 14),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(SCOPE_D + 14) / 2, 0.5, -(SCOPE_D + 14) / 2),
                }
            ):Play()
            TweenService2
                :Create(
                    scopeClip,
                    openInfo,
                    { Size = UDim2.new(0, SCOPE_D, 0, SCOPE_D), Position = UDim2.new(
                        0.5,
                        -SCOPE_D / 2,
                        0.5,
                        -SCOPE_D / 2
                    ) }
                )
                :Play()
            TweenService2:Create(
                scopeRing2,
                openInfo,
                {
                    Size = UDim2.new(0, SCOPE_D + 3, 0, SCOPE_D + 3),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -(SCOPE_D + 3) / 2, 0.5, -(SCOPE_D + 3) / 2),
                }
            ):Play()
            local landA = posOnOrbit(math.pi)
            local landB = posOnOrbit(0)
            TweenService2:Create(
                calciumLbl,
                TweenInfo.new(0.65, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = landA }
            ):Play()
            TweenService2:Create(
                duelsLbl,
                TweenInfo.new(0.65, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Position = landB }
            ):Play()
            task.delay(0.7, function()
                if introFinished then
                    return
                end
                startHoldAnimations()
            end)
            local lockedCalcium = UDim2.new(0.5, -58, 0.5, 0)
            local lockedDuels = UDim2.new(0.5, 54, 0.5, 0)
            local function screenShake(duration, magnitude)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local targets = { housing, bevel, scopeClip, scopeRing2 }
                    local origins = {}
                    for _, fr in ipairs(targets) do
                        origins[fr] = fr.Position
                    end
                    local t0 = tick()
                    while tick() - t0 < duration and not introFinished do
                        local decay = 1 - ((tick() - t0) / duration)
                        local ox = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * magnitude * decay
                        local oy = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * magnitude * decay
                        for _, fr in ipairs(targets) do
                            local o = origins[fr]
                            fr.Position = UDim2.new(o.X.Scale, o.X.Offset + ox, o.Y.Scale, o.Y.Offset + oy)
                        end
                        task.wait()
                    end
                    for _, fr in ipairs(targets) do
                        fr.Position = origins[fr]
                    end
                end)
            end
            local function fireSniperShot()
                if introFinished then
                    return
                end
                stopIntroAnim()
                TweenService2:Create(
                    calciumLbl,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { Position = lockedCalcium }
                ):Play()
                TweenService2:Create(
                    duelsLbl,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                    { Position = lockedDuels }
                ):Play()
                local flash = Instance.new("Frame", scopeClip)
                flash.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                flash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 240)
                flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
                flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
                flash.ZIndex = 12
                Instance.new("UICorner", flash).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
                TweenService2:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.18), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                Debris2:AddItem(flash, 0.3)
                local hit = Instance.new("Frame", scopeClip)
                hit.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 2)
                hit.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                hit.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
                hit.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
                hit.BorderSizePixel = 0
                hit.ZIndex = 13
                local hit2 = Instance.new("Frame", scopeClip)
                hit2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 14)
                hit2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                hit2.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
                hit2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
                hit2.BorderSizePixel = 0
                hit2.ZIndex = 13
                TweenService2:Create(hit, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                TweenService2:Create(hit2, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                Debris2:AddItem(hit, 0.4)
                Debris2:AddItem(hit2, 0.4)
                pcall(function()
                    local SHOT_ID = "rbxassetid://9125892370"
                    local function bang(pitch, vol, delay)
                        task.delay(delay or 0, function()
                            if introFinished then
                                return
                            end
                            local s = Instance.new("Sound")
                            s.SoundId = SHOT_ID
                            s.Volume = vol
                            s.PlaybackSpeed = pitch
                            s.Parent = SoundService2
                            s:Play()
                            Debris2:AddItem(s, 3)
                        end)
                    end
                    bang(0.85, 1.0, 0)
                    bang(0.7, 0.7, 0.03)
                end)
                screenShake(0.45, 14)
            end
            local playStart = tick()
            local shotFired = false
            local exitTriggered = false
            while not introFinished and (tick() - playStart) < INTRO_DURATION do
                task.wait(0.05)
                pcall(function()
                    if introSound and introSound.Parent and not introSound.IsPlaying and introSound.TimeLength > 0 then
                        if introSound.TimePosition < math.min(INTRO_DURATION, introSound.TimeLength) - 0.2 then
                            introSound:Play()
                        end
                    end
                end)
                local elapsed = tick() - playStart
                local remaining = INTRO_DURATION - elapsed
                if (not shotFired) and remaining <= 5 then
                    shotFired = true
                    fireSniperShot()
                end
                if (not exitTriggered) and elapsed >= (INTRO_DURATION - 1.0) then
                    exitTriggered = true
                    stopIntroAnim()
                    pcall(function()
                        TweenService2:Create(
                            calciumLbl,
                            TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                            { Position = calciumExit, TextTransparency = 1 }
                        ):Play()
                        TweenService2:Create(
                            duelsLbl,
                            TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                            { Position = duelsExit, TextTransparency = 1 }
                        ):Play()
                        TweenService2:Create(orb, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
                        local closeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                        for _, fr in ipairs({ scopeClip, housing, bevel, scopeRing2 }) do
                            TweenService2
                                :Create(
                                    fr,
                                    closeInfo,
                                    { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) }
                                )
                                :Play()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            if not introFinished then
                finishIntro()
            end
        end)
        introCompleteEvent.Event:Wait()
        pcall(function()
            introCompleteEvent:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if ragdollCheckConn then
            ragdollCheckConn:Disconnect()
            ragdollCheckConn = nil
        end
        timerActive = false
        lastRagdollState = false
        if timerConn then
            timerConn:Disconnect()
            timerConn = nil
        end
        if timerBillboard then
            timerBillboard:Destroy()
            timerBillboard = nil
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
        createTimerBillboard()
        checkRagdoll()
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        createTimerBillboard()
    end)
end
local function createRagdollBillboard(duration, labelText, color)
    if not ragdollGuiEnabled then
        return nil
    end
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    local PINK = palette.primary
    local HUB_SECONDARY = palette.secondary
    local BG = Color3.fromRGB(6, 6, 6)
    local WHITE = HUB_SECONDARY
    local W, H = 230, 72
    local guiName = "MoveeRagdollTimer_" .. labelText
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local old = cg:FindFirstChild(guiName)
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            local o = pg:FindFirstChild(guiName)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = guiName
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    sg.DisplayOrder = 25
    parentGui(sg)
    local card = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, H)
    card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -W / 2, 0, 58)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 10)
    card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.ZIndex = 30
    card.Active = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(115, 115, 125)
    stroke.Thickness = 1.25
    stroke.Transparency = 0.35
    local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 142, 0, 22)
    titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 9)
    titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLbl.Text = (
        labelText == "RAGDOLL" and "RAGDOLL TIMER" or (labelText == "STONE" and "STONE TIMER" or labelText .. " TIMER")
    )
    titleLbl:SetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary", true)
    titleLbl.TextColor3 = HUB_SECONDARY
    titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    titleLbl.TextSize = 11
    titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    titleLbl.ZIndex = 32
    local divider = Instance.new("Frame", card)
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
    divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 34)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.ZIndex = 31
    local timerLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    timerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 28)
    timerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 5)
    timerLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    timerLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", duration) .. "s"
    timerLbl:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
    timerLbl.TextColor3 = PINK
    timerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    timerLbl.TextSize = 22
    timerLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    timerLbl.ZIndex = 32
    local progressTrack = Instance.new("Frame", card)
    progressTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6)
    progressTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -16)
    progressTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)
    progressTrack.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    progressTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progressTrack.ZIndex = 31
    Instance.new("UICorner", progressTrack).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local progressFill = Instance.new("Frame", progressTrack)
    progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    progressFill:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
    progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = PINK
    progressFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progressFill.ZIndex = 32
    Instance.new("UICorner", progressFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local shimmer = addShimmerToLabel(timerLbl, PINK, WHITE)
    task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent do
            t = t + 0.04
            shimmer.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t) * 0.5, 0)
            task.wait(0.04)
        end
    end)
    local dragStart, dragStartPos, dragging = nil, nil, false
    card.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = inp.Position
            dragStartPos = card.Position
            inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if
            dragging
            and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
        then
            local d = inp.Position - dragStart
            card.Position = UDim2.new(
                dragStartPos.X.Scale,
                dragStartPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                dragStartPos.Y.Scale,
                dragStartPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    local startTime = tick()
    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local remaining = math.max(0, duration - (tick() - startTime))
        if remaining <= 0 then
            conn:Disconnect()
            pcall(function()
                sg:Destroy()
            end)
        elseif timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent then
            timerLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", remaining) .. "s"
            progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(remaining / duration, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
        end
    end)
    return sg
end
local function onHumanoidStateChanged(old, new)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then
        return
    end
    local isRag = (
        new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    )
    if isRag and not hum.PlatformStand and not activeBatBillboard then
        activeBatBillboard = createRagdollBillboard(2.5, "RAGDOLL", LIGHT_BLUE)
        task.delay(2.6, function()
            if activeBatBillboard then
                pcall(function()
                    activeBatBillboard:Destroy()
                end)
                activeBatBillboard = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function onMedusaStateChanged()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum.PlatformStand and not activeMedusaBillboard then
        activeMedusaBillboard = createRagdollBillboard(4.5, "STONE", LIGHT_BLUE)
        task.delay(4.5, function()
            if activeMedusaBillboard then
                pcall(function()
                    activeMedusaBillboard:Destroy()
                end)
                activeMedusaBillboard = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function setupRagdollTriggers()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.StateChanged:Connect(onHumanoidStateChanged)
        hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"):Connect(onMedusaStateChanged)
    end
end
local function getSpeedThemeColors()
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    local accent = palette.primary
    local accent2 = palette.secondary
    local gray = palette.secondary
    return accent, accent2, gray
end
local function applySpeedIndicatorTheme()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then
        return
    end
    local bb = head:FindFirstChild("MoveeSpeedBB")
    if not bb then
        return
    end
    local accent, accent2, gray = getSpeedThemeColors()
    for _, lbl in ipairs(bb:GetChildren()) do
        if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") then
            if string.find(lbl.Text or "", "S2HUB", 1, true) or lbl.Name == "DiscordLbl" then
                lbl.TextColor3 = gray
                local gr = lbl:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                if gr then
                    gr.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, gray),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, accent),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, gray),
                    })
                end
            else
                lbl.TextColor3 = accent
                local gr = lbl:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
                if gr then
                    gr.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, accent2),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, accent),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, accent2),
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local function setupSpeedIndicator(char)
    local head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
    if not head then
        return
    end
    if head:FindFirstChild("MoveeSpeedBB") then
        head.MoveeSpeedBB:Destroy()
    end
    local accent, accent2, gray = getSpeedThemeColors()
    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
    bb.Name = "MoveeSpeedBB"
    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 54)
    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.25, 0)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bb.LightInfluence = 0
    local discordLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
    discordLabel.Name = "DiscordLbl"
    discordLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 20)
    discordLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
    discordLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    discordLabel.Text = ".gg/HN24Xkqf8"
    discordLabel:SetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary", true)
    discordLabel.TextColor3 = gray
    discordLabel.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    discordLabel.TextSize = 13
    discordLabel.TextScaled = false
    discordLabel.TextWrapped = false
    discordLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    speedLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
    speedLabel.Name = "SpeedLbl"
    speedLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 26)
    speedLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 20)
    speedLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    speedLabel.Text = "NORMAL SPEED: " .. string.format("%.1f", NS):gsub("%.", ",")
    speedLabel.TextColor3 = accent
    speedLabel.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    speedLabel.TextSize = 15
    speedLabel.TextScaled = false
    speedLabel.TextWrapped = false
    speedLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    local gr1 = addShimmerToLabel(speedLabel, accent2, accent)
    local gr2 = addShimmerToLabel(discordLabel, gray, accent)
    gr2.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.02, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.02, 0),
    })
    task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while bb and bb.Parent do
            t = t + 0.06
            local o = Vector2.new(math.sin(t) * 1.0, 0)
            gr1.Offset = o
            gr2.Offset = o
            task.wait(0.02)
        end
    end)
end
local function getConfiguredModeSpeed()
    if laggerCarryActive then
        return "LAGGER CARRY", tonumber(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED) or 0
    elseif laggerModeEnabled then
        return "LAGGER NORMAL", tonumber(LAGGER_SPEED) or 0
    elseif carrySpeedActive then
        return "CARRY SPEED", tonumber(CS) or 0
    else
        return "NORMAL SPEED", tonumber(NS) or 0
    end
end
local function getConfiguredModeDisplay()
    local mode, value = getConfiguredModeSpeed()
    return mode .. ": " .. string.format("%.1f", value):gsub("%.", ",")
end
local function getActiveMoveSpeed()
    if laggerCarryActive then
        return LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED
    elseif laggerModeEnabled then
        return LAGGER_SPEED
    elseif carrySpeedActive then
        return CS
    else
        return NS
    end
end
local function getAutoPathSpeed()
    if laggerModeEnabled then
        return LAGGER_SPEED
    else
        return NS
    end
end
local function applyVel(part, targetVel)
    if not part or not part.Parent then
        return
    end
    local mass = part.AssemblyMass
    if mass and mass > 0 then
        local delta = targetVel - part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        part:ApplyImpulse(delta * mass)
    else
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = targetVel
    end
end
local function applyAngVel(part, targetAng)
    if not part or not part.Parent then
        return
    end
    part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = targetAng
end
local function manualJumpBoost(boost)
    if not infJumpEnabled or infJumpMode ~= "manual" then
        return
    end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then
        applyVel(root, Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, boost, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z))
    end
end
local function triggerJumpButton()
    local previousEnabled = infJumpEnabled
    local previousMode = infJumpMode
    infJumpEnabled = true
    infJumpMode = "manual"
    manualJumpBoost(45)
    infJumpEnabled = previousEnabled
    infJumpMode = previousMode
end
local function startHoldInfJump()
    if holdInfJumpConn then
        holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect()
    end
    holdInfJumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not infJumpEnabled or infJumpMode ~= "hold" then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then
            return
        end
        local isJumpHeld = UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (hum.Jump == true)
        local cv = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if isJumpHeld and cv.Y < 35 then
            applyVel(root, Vector3.new(cv.X, 55, cv.Z))
        end
        cv = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if cv.Y < -120 then
            applyVel(root, Vector3.new(cv.X, -120, cv.Z))
        end
    end)
end
local function stopHoldInfJump()
    if holdInfJumpConn then
        holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect()
        holdInfJumpConn = nil
    end
end
UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    manualJumpBoost(45)
end)
UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if
        input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
        and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space
        and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox()
    then
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                manualJumpBoost(50)
            end
        end)
    end
end)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
        manualJumpBoost(50)
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local BLACKLIST_URL = "https://pastebin.com/2zLUXv2K"
    pcall(function()
        HS.HttpEnabled = true
    end)
    while task.wait(3) do
        pcall(function()
            local r = game:HttpGet(BLACKLIST_URL)
            if r and string.find(r, tostring(LP.UserId), 1, true) then
                LP:Kick("You have been removed for cheating | CODE: BAC-1633")
            end
        end)
    end
end)
_antiKickConns = {}
enableAntiKick = function()
    antiKickEnabled = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while antiKickEnabled do
            task.wait(0.5)
            local char = LP.Character
            if char then
                for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local n = tool.Name:lower()
                        if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("skibidi") or n:find("toilet") then
                            brainrotDetected = true
                            if autoBatEnabled then
                                autoBatEnabled = false
                                if autoBatSetVisual then
                                    autoBatSetVisual(false)
                                end
                            end
                            if autoLeftEnabled then
                                autoLeftEnabled = false
                                if autoLeftSetVisual then
                                    autoLeftSetVisual(false)
                                end
                            end
                            if autoRightEnabled then
                                autoRightEnabled = false
                                if autoRightSetVisual then
                                    autoRightSetVisual(false)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            brainrotDetected = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
disableAntiKick = function()
    antiKickEnabled = false
    brainrotDetected = false
    for _, c in ipairs(_antiKickConns) do
        pcall(function()
            c:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    _antiKickConns = {}
end

    BorderColor = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90),
    GlowColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0),
    SelectorBg = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60),
    SelectorAct = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0),
    OrangeBorder = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0),
    CornerRadius = 10,
    PanelWidth = 220,
    PanelHeight = 88,
    BarHeight = 22,
}

local function SaveConfig()
    local pos = mainFrame and mainFrame.Position
    local data = {
        Nivel = nivelActual,
        Bloqueado = ventanaBloqueada,
        LaggerAtivo = laggerActive,
        PosXScale = pos and pos.X.Scale or 0.15,
        PosXOffset = pos and pos.X.Offset or 0,
        PosYScale = pos and pos.Y.Scale or 0.5,
        PosYOffset = pos and pos.Y.Offset or -(UI_CONFIG.PanelHeight / 2)
    }
    pcall(function()
        writefile(ConfigFile, HttpService:JSONEncode(data))
    end)
end

local function LoadConfig()
    if pcall(isfile, ConfigFile) and isfile(ConfigFile) then
        pcall(function()
            local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(ConfigFile))
            nivelActual = data.Nivel or "LOW"
            ventanaBloqueada = data.Bloqueado or false
            laggerActive = data.LaggerAtivo or false
            loadedPosition = {
                XScale = data.PosXScale, XOffset = data.PosXOffset,
                YScale = data.PosYScale, YOffset = data.PosYOffset
            }
        end)
    end
end

LoadConfig()

local function bomb(_poder)
    -- Modo visual: não envia tráfego nem chama RemoteEvents.
end

local toggleBall, toggleContainer, btnV1, btnV2, btnV3, btnV4, lockButton
local titleLabel, textLagger, keybindButton, toggleClick, shadowLabel, shadowGradient
