local function actualizarBotonesNivel()
    if nivelActual == "LOW" then
        btnV1.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
        btnV1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV1.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV1.BorderColor3 = UI_CONFIG.ButtonV1
    else
        btnV1.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
        btnV1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV1.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV1.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    end

    if nivelActual == "MID" then
        btnV2.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
        btnV2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV2.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV2.BorderColor3 = UI_CONFIG.ButtonV2
    else
        btnV2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
        btnV2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV2.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV2.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    end

    if nivelActual == "HIGH" then
        btnV3.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
        btnV3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV3.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV3.BorderColor3 = UI_CONFIG.ButtonV3
    else
        btnV3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
        btnV3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV3.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV3.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    end

    if nivelActual == "ULTRA" then
        btnV4.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
        btnV4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV4.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV4.BorderColor3 = UI_CONFIG.ButtonV4
    else
        btnV4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
        btnV4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        btnV4.BorderSizePixel = 1
        btnV4.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    end

    if modeTextLabel then
        modeTextLabel.Text = ""
    end
end

local function actualizarSwitch()
    if toggleContainer then
        toggleContainer.BackgroundColor3 = laggerActive and LAGGER_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 92)
    end
    if toggleBall then
        toggleBall.BackgroundColor3 = laggerActive and LAGGER_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        if laggerActive then
            toggleBall.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -9)
        else
            toggleBall.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -9)
        end
    end
    if toggleClick then
        toggleClick.Text = ""
        toggleClick.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
    end
end

local function actualizarCandado()
    lockButton.Text = ventanaBloqueada and "Lock" or "Unlock"
    lockButton.TextColor3 = ventanaBloqueada and Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 220) or Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 170)
end

local function actualizarKeybindButton()
    if keybindButton then
        local display = keybind.Name
        if display:match("Button") then
            display = display:gsub("Button", "")
        end
        keybindButton.Text = "KEY: " .. display
    end
end

local function toggleLagger()
    laggerActive = not laggerActive
    local targetPos = laggerActive and UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -9)
    local targetBg = laggerActive and LAGGER_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 92)
    local targetBall = laggerActive and LAGGER_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local toggleTween = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(toggleBall, toggleTween, { Position = targetPos, BackgroundColor3 = targetBall }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(toggleContainer, toggleTween, { BackgroundColor3 = targetBg }):Play()
    toggleClick.Text = ""
    toggleClick.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
    
    if laggerActive then
        if lagThread then
            task.cancel(lagThread)
        end
        lagThread = task.spawn(function()
            while laggerActive do
                -- O estado é apenas visual; nenhuma rotina de rede é executada.
                task.wait(0.18)
            end
        end)
    else
        if lagThread then
            task.cancel(lagThread)
            lagThread = nil
        end
    end
    SaveConfig()
end

local function showWarning(callback)
    if warningActive then return end
    warningActive = true

    local popup = Instance.new("Frame", screenGui)
    popup.Name = "WarningPopup"
    popup.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 47)
    popup.BorderSizePixel = 2
    popup.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 125, 156)
    popup.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 170)
    popup.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0.5, -110)
    popup.ZIndex = 100

    local popupCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", popup)
    popupCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", popup)
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 47)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 35))
    })
    gradient.Rotation = 45

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", popup)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 200, 255)
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Transparency = 0.7

    local warnText = Instance.new("TextLabel", popup)
    warnText.Name = "WarnText"
    warnText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    warnText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.6, -10)
    warnText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    warnText.Font = UI_CONFIG.Font
    warnText.Text = "⚠️ This mode can overheat your device if it is mid-range to high-end devices. This mode is the best to use. Medium-range devices may have problems with ping or lag."
    warnText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    warnText.TextSize = 11
    warnText.TextWrapped = true
    warnText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    warnText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    warnText.ZIndex = 101

    local textStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", warnText)
    textStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    textStroke.Thickness = 1
    textStroke.Transparency = 0.5

    local yesBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", popup)
    yesBtn.Name = "YesBtn"
    yesBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 85)
    yesBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    yesBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 26)
    yesBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -80, 1, -34)
    yesBtn.Font = UI_CONFIG.Font
    yesBtn.Text = "YES"
    yesBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    yesBtn.TextSize = 11
    yesBtn.ZIndex = 101

    local yesCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", yesBtn)
    yesCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    yesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        warningActive = false
        popup:Destroy()
        callback(true)
    end)

    yesBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        yesBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(85, 255, 136)
    end)
    yesBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        yesBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 85)
    end)

    local noBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", popup)
    noBtn.Name = "NoBtn"
    noBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 34, 68)
    noBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    noBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 26)
    noBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 10, 1, -34)
    noBtn.Font = UI_CONFIG.Font
    noBtn.Text = "NO"
    noBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    noBtn.TextSize = 11
    noBtn.ZIndex = 101

    local noCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", noBtn)
    noCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    noBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        warningActive = false
        popup:Destroy()
        callback(false)
    end)

    noBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        noBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 85, 119)
    end)
    noBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        noBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 34, 68)
    end)

    warningFrame = popup
end

local function collapseWindow()
    if not expanded then return end
    expanded = false
    centralContainer.Visible = false
    minimizeButton.Text = "+"
    local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(mainFrame, tweenInfo, { Size = UDim2.new(0, UI_CONFIG.PanelWidth, 0, UI_CONFIG.BarHeight) }):Play()
end

local function expandWindow()
    if expanded then return end
    expanded = true
    centralContainer.Visible = true
    minimizeButton.Text = "-"
    centralContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
    local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    TweenService:Create(mainFrame, tweenInfo, { Size = UDim2.new(0, UI_CONFIG.PanelWidth, 0, UI_CONFIG.PanelHeight) }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(centralContainer, tweenInfo, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -(UI_CONFIG.BarHeight + 6)) }):Play()
end

local inputConnection
inputConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if not listeningForInput then return end
    if gp then return end
    local newKey = nil
    if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
        newKey = input.KeyCode
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
        newKey = input.KeyCode
    end
    if newKey then
        keybind = newKey
        actualizarKeybindButton()
        SaveConfig()
        listeningForInput = false
        keybindButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
        keybindButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        keybindButton.TextColor3 = LAGGER_ACCENT
    end
end)

local isDragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil

mainFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if ventanaBloqueada then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        isDragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = mainFrame.Position
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if not isDragging or ventanaBloqueada then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)

mainFrame.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        isDragging = false
        SaveConfig()
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if gp then return end
    if input.KeyCode == keybind or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode == keybind) then
        toggleLagger()
    end
end)

]====]
local hubLaggerEnabled = false
local laggerHubColorIndex = 1
local hubLaggerLoaded = false
local function setHubLaggerVisual(on)
    hubLaggerEnabled = on == true
    local core = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local playerGui = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local existing = core:FindFirstChild("CleanDuels_UI") or (playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("CleanDuels_UI"))
    if hubLaggerEnabled then
        if existing then
            pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end)
            existing = nil
        end
        pcall(function()
            local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[laggerHubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
            if getgenv then getgenv()._S2LaggerHubColor = palette.primary end
        end)
        local loader = loadstring or load
        if not loader then
            warn("[S2Hub] loadstring is unavailable; HUB LAGGER could not start")
            return
        end
        local chunk, err = loader(HUB_LAGGER_SOURCE)
        if not chunk then
            warn("[S2Hub] HUB LAGGER load error: ", err)
            return
        end
        local ok, runErr = pcall(chunk)
        if not ok then
            warn("[S2Hub] HUB LAGGER runtime error: ", runErr)
            return
        end
        hubLaggerLoaded = true
    elseif existing then
        existing.Enabled = false
    end
end

local MOBILE_VISUAL_NAMES = { [1] = "BLACK", [2] = "WHITE" }
local mobileVisualButtonRef = nil
local BACKGROUND2_NAMES = { [1] = "BLACK", [2] = "WHITE" }
local applyStealBarBackground
local function applyMobileVisualButtons(index)
    mobileVisualIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1), 1, 2)
    if mobileVisualButtonRef then
        mobileVisualButtonRef.Text = MOBILE_VISUAL_NAMES[mobileVisualIndex]
    end
    if not mobGuiRef then return end
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    for _, obj in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do
        pcall(function()
            if not (obj:IsA("Frame") and obj:GetAttribute("BtnKey")) then return end
            local on = obj:GetAttribute("BtnIsOn") == true
            local textButton = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
            local stroke = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            local bg = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                or (ACTION_BTN_KEYS[obj:GetAttribute("BtnKey")] and ACTION_BTN_IDLE or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
            obj.BackgroundColor3 = on and (mobileVisualIndex == 2 and bg or palette.primary) or bg
            local textColor = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and palette.primary or Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246)
            if textButton then textButton.TextColor3 = textColor end
            if stroke then
                stroke.Color = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or (on and palette.primary or Color3.fromRGB(60, 61, 68))
            end
            local gradient = obj:FindFirstChild("S2NoBackgroundGradient")
            if gradient then gradient.Enabled = mobileVisualIndex == 1 and not on end
        end)
    end
end
local function applyBackground2(index)
    background2Index = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1), 1, 2)
    local root = mainGuiRef
    if root then
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true then return end
                    if obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeR") == nil then
                        local c = obj.Color
                        obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeR", c.R)
                        obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeG", c.G)
                        obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeB", c.B)
                    end
                    local r = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeR")
                    local g = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeG")
                    local b = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2StrokeB")
                    local original = Color3.new(r or 0, g or 0, b or 0)
                    if background2Index == 2 and original.R < 0.72 and original.G < 0.72 and original.B < 0.75 then
                        obj.Color = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 15)
                    elseif background2Index == 1 and r and g and b then
                        obj.Color = original
                    end
                    return
                end
                if not obj:IsA("Frame") and not obj:IsA("ImageLabel") and not obj:IsA("ImageButton")
                    and not obj:IsA("TextButton") and not obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                    return
                end
                if obj:GetAttribute("S2HubCardSurface") == true then
                    obj.BackgroundColor3 = background2Index == 2
                        and Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 242)
                        or Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18)
                    return
                end
                if obj:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true then return end
                if obj:GetAttribute("S2MobileButton") == true then return end
                if obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2R") == nil then
                    local c = obj.BackgroundColor3
                    obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2R", c.R)
                    obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2G", c.G)
                    obj:SetAttribute("S2Background2B", c.B)
                end
                local r = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2R")
                local g = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2G")
                local b = obj:GetAttribute("S2Background2B")
                local original = Color3.new(r or 0, g or 0, b or 0)
                if original.R < 0.22 and original.G < 0.22 and original.B < 0.25 then
                    if background2Index == 2 then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 242)
                    elseif r and g and b then
                        obj.BackgroundColor3 = original
                    end
                elseif background2Index == 1 and r and g and b then
                    obj.BackgroundColor3 = original
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    applyHubColor(hubColorIndex)
    if applyStealBarBackground then
        applyStealBarBackground(background2Index)
    end
end
local waypointMarkerFolder = nil
local waypointDefinitions = {
    { name = "WAYPOINT L1", position = AP_L1, color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 220, 110) },
    { name = "WAYPOINT L2", position = AP_L2, color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 70) },
    { name = "WAYPOINT R1", position = AP_R1, color = Color3.fromRGB(240, 75, 75) },
    { name = "WAYPOINT R2", position = AP_R2, color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 145, 255) },
}
local function clearWaypointMarkers()
    if waypointMarkerFolder then
        pcall(function()
            waypointMarkerFolder:Destroy()
        end)
        waypointMarkerFolder = nil
    end
end
local function setShowPosition(enabled)
    showPositionEnabled = enabled == true
    clearWaypointMarkers()
    if not showPositionEnabled then
        return
    end
    waypointMarkerFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    waypointMarkerFolder.Name = "S2LocalWaypointMarkers"
    waypointMarkerFolder.Parent = workspace
    for _, def in ipairs(waypointDefinitions) do
        local marker = Instance.new("Part")
        marker.Name = def.name:gsub("%s+", "_")
        marker.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        marker.Size = Vector3.new(0.42, 0.42, 0.42)
        marker.Position = def.position + Vector3.new(0, 0.28, 0)
        marker.Anchored = true
        marker.CanCollide = false
        marker.CanTouch = false
        marker.CanQuery = false
        marker.CastShadow = false
        marker.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        marker.Color = def.color
        marker.Transparency = 0.05
        marker.Parent = waypointMarkerFolder
        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "PositionLabel"
        billboard.Adornee = marker
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.LightInfluence = 0
        billboard.MaxDistance = 1000
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 34)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0.85, 0)
        billboard.Parent = marker
        HubFonts.register(billboard)
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = def.name:gsub("^WAYPOINT%s+", "")
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
        label.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        label.TextSize = 11
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.Parent = billboard
    end
end
Steal = {
    AutoStealEnabled = false,
    StealRadius = 8,
    PrimeRange = 8,
    StealDuration = 0.3,
    Mode = "V1",
    HalfFireRange = 8,
    HalfHoldMin = 1.3,
    HalfHoldMax = 2.6,
    HalfEntryDelay = 0.3,
    Data = {},
    plotCache = {},
    plotCacheTime = {},
    cachedPrompts = {},
    promptCacheTime = 0,
}
lastStealTick = 0
isStealing, stealStartTime = false, nil
Conns = { autoSteal = nil, antiRag = nil, batCounter = nil, anchor = {} }
MEDUSA_COOLDOWN = 25
batCounterDebounce = false
modeValLbl = nil
lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
MOVE_KEYS = {
    [Enum.KeyCode.W] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.A] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.S] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.D] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Up] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Left] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Down] = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Right] = true,
}
local function isRagdollState(hum)
    if not hum then
        return true
    end
    local st = hum:GetState()
    return hum.PlatformStand
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
end
local function isMyPlotByName(plotName)
    local ct = tick()
    if Steal.plotCache[plotName] ~= nil and (ct - (Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] or 0)) < 2 then
        return Steal.plotCache[plotName]
    end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
        Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
        return false
    end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then
        Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
        Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
        return false
    end
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if sign then
        local yb = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            local r = yb.Enabled == true
            Steal.plotCache[plotName] = r
            Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
            return r
        end
    end
    Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
    Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
    return false
end
local function collectPromptFromSpawn(sp)
    if not sp then
        return nil
    end
    local att = sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    if att then
        for _, child in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                return child
            end
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            return child
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function isNearPodiumWithPrompt()
    local char = LP.Character
    local hrpL = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrpL then
        return false
    end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        return false
    end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if not isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if podiums then
                for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                    local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if sp then
                        local d = (hrpL.Position - sp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= (Steal.PrimeRange or Steal.StealRadius or 60) then
                            if collectPromptFromSpawn(sp) then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
local function findNearestPrompt()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return nil
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        return nil
    end
    local nearest, dist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if plot:IsA("Model") and not isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if pods then
                for _, pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
                    local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if sp then
                        local d = (sp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= (Steal.PrimeRange or Steal.StealRadius or 60) and d < dist then
                            local found = nil
                            local att = sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                            if att then
                                for _, pr in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
                                    if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.ActionText and pr.ActionText:find("Steal") then
                                        found = pr
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if not found then
                                for _, pr in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.ActionText and pr.ActionText:find("Steal") then
                                        found = pr
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if found then
                                nearest, dist = found, d
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest
end
local function _promptDist(prompt)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return math.huge
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not root then
        return math.huge
    end
    local part = prompt.Parent
    if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then
        part = part.Parent
    end
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
    end
    local ok, cf = pcall(function()
        return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition
    end)
    if ok and cf then
        return (cf - root.Position).Magnitude
    end
    return math.huge
end
local function executeSteal(prompt)
    if isStealing then
        return
    end
    if not Steal.Data[prompt] then
        Steal.Data[prompt] = { hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true }
        if getconnections then
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                if c.Function then
                    table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].hold, c.Function)
                end
            end
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                if c.Function then
                    table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].trigger, c.Function)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local data = Steal.Data[prompt]
    if not data.ready then
        return
    end
    data.ready = false
    isStealing = true
    stealStartTime = tick()
    if Steal.Mode == "V2" or Steal.Mode == "half" then
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do
                task.spawn(fn)
            end
            task.wait(Steal.HalfHoldMin)
            local inRange = _promptDist(prompt) <= Steal.HalfFireRange
            while true do
                local el = tick() - stealStartTime
                if el > Steal.HalfHoldMax or not prompt.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if _promptDist(prompt) <= Steal.HalfFireRange then
                    if not inRange then
                        task.wait(Steal.HalfEntryDelay)
                    end
                    for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do
                        task.spawn(fn)
                    end
                    break
                end
                task.wait()
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
            data.ready = true
            isStealing = false
        end)
    else
        task.spawn(function()
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do
                task.spawn(fn)
            end
            local el = 0
            while el < Steal.StealDuration do
                el = el + task.wait()
            end
            for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do
                task.spawn(fn)
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
            data.ready = true
            isStealing = false
        end)
    end
end
local v3Steal = {conn = nil, enabled = false, progress = 0, prompt = nil, lastPulse = 0}
local function v3Release(prompt)
    if not prompt then return end
    pcall(function() if prompt.InputHoldEnd then prompt:InputHoldEnd() end end)
end
local function v3Prepare(prompt)
    if not prompt then return nil end
    if not Steal.Data[prompt] then
        Steal.Data[prompt] = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true}
        if getconnections then
            pcall(function()
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                    if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].hold, c.Function) end
                end
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                    if c.Function then table.insert(Steal.Data[prompt].trigger, c.Function) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    return Steal.Data[prompt]
end
local function v3Hold(prompt)
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return end
    pcall(function() if prompt.InputHoldBegin then prompt:InputHoldBegin() end end)
    local data = v3Prepare(prompt)
    if data then for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end end
end
local function v3Trigger(prompt)
    local data = v3Prepare(prompt)
    if data then for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end end
    v3Release(prompt)
    pcall(function() if fireproximityprompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) end end)
end
local function startV3Steal()
    if v3Steal.conn then return end
    v3Steal.enabled = true
    v3Steal.progress = 0
    v3Steal.prompt = nil
    v3Steal.lastPulse = 0
    v3Steal.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Steal.AutoStealEnabled or Steal.Mode ~= "V3" or not v3Steal.enabled then
            v3Release(v3Steal.prompt)
            v3Steal.prompt = nil
            v3Steal.progress = 0
            isStealing = false
            updateStealPct(0)
            return
        end
        local prompt = findNearestPrompt()
        local radius = tonumber(Steal.PrimeRange or Steal.StealRadius) or 8
        local dist = prompt and _promptDist(prompt) or math.huge
        if prompt and dist <= radius then
            if v3Steal.prompt ~= prompt then
                v3Release(v3Steal.prompt)
                v3Steal.prompt = prompt
                v3Steal.progress = 0
            end
            isStealing = true
            local now = tick()
            if now - v3Steal.lastPulse > 0.1 then
                v3Steal.lastPulse = now
                v3Hold(prompt)
            end
            local duration = math.max(tonumber(Steal.StealDuration) or 0.3, 0.05)
            v3Steal.progress = math.clamp(v3Steal.progress + dt / duration, 0, 1)
            updateStealPct(v3Steal.progress)
            if v3Steal.progress >= 1 then
                v3Trigger(prompt)
                v3Steal.progress = 0
                v3Steal.prompt = nil
                isStealing = false
                updateStealPct(0)
            end
        else
            v3Release(v3Steal.prompt)
            v3Steal.prompt = nil
            local decay = math.max(tonumber(Steal.HalfEntryDelay) or 0.35, 0.05)
            v3Steal.progress = math.max(0, v3Steal.progress - dt / decay)
            isStealing = v3Steal.progress > 0
            updateStealPct(v3Steal.progress)
        end
    end)
end
local function stopV3Steal()
    v3Steal.enabled = false
    v3Release(v3Steal.prompt)
    v3Steal.prompt = nil
    v3Steal.progress = 0
    isStealing = false
    updateStealPct(0)
    if v3Steal.conn then v3Steal.conn:Disconnect(); v3Steal.conn = nil end
end
startAutoSteal = function()
    if Steal.Mode == "V3" then
        startV3Steal()
        return
    end
    if Conns.autoSteal then return end
    Conns.autoSteal = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Steal.AutoStealEnabled or isStealing then return end
        local p = findNearestPrompt()
        if p then executeSteal(p) end
    end)
end
stopAutoSteal = function()
    if Conns.autoSteal then
        Conns.autoSteal:Disconnect()
        Conns.autoSteal = nil
    end
    stopV3Steal()
    isStealing = false
    updateStealPct(0)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.75) do
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LP and p.Character then
                for _, part in ipairs(p.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then
        return
    end
    if speedLabel and speedLabel.Parent then
        if os.clock() - lastSpeedLabelUpdate > 0.25 then
            speedLabel.Text = getConfiguredModeDisplay()
            lastSpeedLabelUpdate = os.clock()
        end
    end
    if isRagdollState(hum) then
        lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        return
    end
    if not autoBatEnabled and not autoLeftEnabled and not autoRightEnabled then
        local md = hum.MoveDirection
        local spd = getActiveMoveSpeed()
        if md.Magnitude > 0 then
            lastMoveDir = md
            applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(md.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, md.Z * spd))
        elseif antiRagdollEnabled and lastMoveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
            local anyHeld = false
            for key in pairs(MOVE_KEYS) do
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(key) then
                    anyHeld = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if anyHeld then
                applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(lastMoveDir.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, lastMoveDir.Z * spd))
            end
        end
    end
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    setupSpeedIndicator(char)
    setupRagdollTriggers()
    if medusaCounterEnabled then
        setupMedusa(char)
    end
    if batCounterEnabled then
        startBatCounter()
    end
    if unwalkEnabled then
        task.wait(0.5)
        startUnwalk()
    end
end)
if LP.Character then
    setupSpeedIndicator(LP.Character)
    setupRagdollTriggers()
end
alConn, arConn = nil, nil
alPhase, arPhase = 1, 1
stopAutoLeft = function()
    if alConn then
        alConn:Disconnect()
        alConn = nil
    end
    alPhase = 1
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            h:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        end
    end
    if autoLeftSetVisual then
        autoLeftSetVisual(false)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then
        mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false)
    end
end
stopAutoRight = function()
    if arConn then
        arConn:Disconnect()
        arConn = nil
    end
    arPhase = 1
    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if h then
            h:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        end
    end
    if autoRightSetVisual then
        autoRightSetVisual(false)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then
        mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false)
    end
end
startAutoLeft = function()
    if alConn then
        alConn:Disconnect()
    end
    alPhase = 1
    alConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoLeftEnabled then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then
            return
        end
        if isRagdollState(hum) then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            return
        end
        local spd = getAutoPathSpeed()
        if alPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP_L1.X, hrp.Position.Y, AP_L1.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                alPhase = 2
                local d = AP_L2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
                return
            end
            local d = AP_L1 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
        elseif alPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP_L2.X, hrp.Position.Y, AP_L2.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                applyVel(hrp, Vector3.zero)
                if not carrySpeedActive then
                    setNormalCarry()
                end
                autoLeftEnabled = false
                autoPlayEnabled = false
                if alConn then
                    alConn:Disconnect()
                    alConn = nil
                end
                alPhase = 1
                if autoLeftSetVisual then
                    autoLeftSetVisual(false)
                end
                if autoPlaySetVisual then
                    autoPlaySetVisual(false)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then
                    mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
                    mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false)
                end
                return
            end
            local d = AP_L2 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
        end
        if autoMoveSwingEnabled and not _alSwingDebounce then
            _alSwingDebounce = true
            local bat = findBat()
            if bat then
                if bat.Parent ~= char then
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:EquipTool(bat)
                    end)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    bat:Activate()
                end)
            end
            task.delay(autoMoveSwingInterval, function()
                _alSwingDebounce = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end
startAutoRight = function()
    if arConn then
        arConn:Disconnect()
    end
    arPhase = 1
    arConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoRightEnabled then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then
            return
        end
        if isRagdollState(hum) then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            return
        end
        local spd = getAutoPathSpeed()
        if arPhase == 1 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP_R1.X, hrp.Position.Y, AP_R1.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                arPhase = 2
                local d = AP_R2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                hum:Move(mv, false)
                applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
                return
            end
            local d = AP_R1 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
        elseif arPhase == 2 then
            local tgt = Vector3.new(AP_R2.X, hrp.Position.Y, AP_R2.Z)
            if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                applyVel(hrp, Vector3.zero)
                if not carrySpeedActive then
                    setNormalCarry()
                end
                autoRightEnabled = false
                autoPlayEnabled = false
                if arConn then
                    arConn:Disconnect()
                    arConn = nil
                end
                arPhase = 1
                if autoRightSetVisual then
                    autoRightSetVisual(false)
                end
                if autoPlaySetVisual then
                    autoPlaySetVisual(false)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then
                    mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
                    mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false)
                end
                return
            end
            local d = AP_R2 - hrp.Position
            local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
            hum:Move(mv, false)
            applyVel(hrp, Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, mv.Z * spd))
        end
        if autoMoveSwingEnabled and not _arSwingDebounce then
            _arSwingDebounce = true
            local bat = findBat()
            if bat then
                if bat.Parent ~= char then
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:EquipTool(bat)
                    end)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    bat:Activate()
                end)
            end
            task.delay(autoMoveSwingInterval, function()
                _arSwingDebounce = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end
runDrop = function()
    if _G._S2DropMethod == "STAND STILL" then
        if dropActive then return end
        local c = LP.Character
        local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not r then return end
        dropActive = true
        -- Stand Still mode: briefly suppress local motion, without importing
        -- the source script's extreme fling impulses into this build.
        local began = os.clock()
        local conn
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local ch = LP.Character
            local rootNow = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not rootNow or os.clock() - began >= 0.12 then
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                dropActive = false
                return
            end
            rootNow.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            rootNow.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
        return
    end
    if dropActive then
        return
    end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return
    end
    dropActive = true
    local t0 = tick()
    local dc
    dc = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local r = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not r then
            dc:Disconnect()
            dropActive = false
            return
        end
        if tick() - t0 >= DROP_ASCEND_DURATION then
            dc:Disconnect()
            local rp = RaycastParams.new()
            rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
            rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local rr = workspace:Raycast(r.Position, Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), rp)
            if rr then
                local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local off = (hum2 and hum2.HipHeight or 2) + (r.Size.Y / 2)
                r.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position.X, rr.Position.Y + off, r.Position.Z)
                applyVel(r, Vector3.zero)
            end
            dropActive = false
            return
        end
        applyVel(r, Vector3.new(r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, DROP_ASCEND_SPEED, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z))
    end)
end
local function doAutoTPDown(force)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        autoTPLastY = nil
        autoTPCharacter = nil
        autoTPArmed = true
        return
    end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        autoTPLastY = nil
        return
    end
    local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum2 then
        autoTPLastY = hrp.Position.Y
        return
    end
    local currentY = hrp.Position.Y
    if autoTPCharacter ~= char then
        autoTPCharacter = char
        autoTPLastY = currentY
        autoTPArmed = true
        autoTPLastTrigger = 0
        return
    end
    local previousY = autoTPLastY or currentY
    local riseDelta = currentY - previousY
    local verticalVelocity = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
    autoTPLastY = currentY
    if force then
        autoTPArmed = false
    else
        if not autoTPArmed then
            if currentY <= autoTPHeight - 4 then
                autoTPArmed = true
            end
            return
        end
        if currentY < autoTPHeight then
            return
        end
        local rising = riseDelta >= AUTO_TP_MIN_RISE or verticalVelocity > 1
        if not rising then
            return
        end
        local now = tick()
        if now - (autoTPLastTrigger or 0) < AUTO_TP_COOLDOWN then
            return
        end
        autoTPLastTrigger = now
        autoTPArmed = false
    end
    local function snapToFloor(rootPart)
        if not rootPart then return end
        local yaw = select(2, rootPart.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ())
        local targetY = -7.00
        -- TP Down modes ported from S2: NORMAL remains byte-for-byte
        -- equivalent in behavior; SLOW FALL and ZIGZAG descend over time.
        local method = _G._S2TPDownMethod
        if method == "SLOW" or method == "ZIGZAG" then
            if _G._S2TPDownBusy then return end
            _G._S2TPDownBusy = true
            local zig = method == "ZIGZAG"
            _G._S2TPDownZigSide = -(_G._S2TPDownZigSide or 1)
            local side = _G._S2TPDownZigSide
            task.spawn(function()
                local deadline = os.clock() + 6
                local startPos = rootPart.Position
                while os.clock() < deadline do
                    local charNow = LP.Character
                    local r = charNow and charNow:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local h = charNow and charNow:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not r or not h or h.Health <= 0 then break end
                    local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    local pos = r.Position
                    local nextY = math.max(targetY, pos.Y - 55 * math.min(tonumber(dt) or 0.016, 0.05))
                    local right = r.CFrame.RightVector
                    local lateral = zig and (right * (side * math.min((startPos.Y-nextY)*0.045, 6))) or Vector3.zero
                    r.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, nextY, pos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
                    if zig then r.CFrame = r.CFrame + Vector3.new(lateral.X, 0, lateral.Z) end
                    r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    if nextY <= targetY + 0.05 then break end
                end
                _G._S2TPDownBusy = false
            end)
            return
        end
        rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(rootPart.Position.X, targetY, rootPart.Position.Z)
            * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
        rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    snapToFloor(hrp)
end
startAutoTP = function()
    if autoTPConn then
        task.cancel(autoTPConn)
        autoTPConn = nil
    end
    autoTPLastY = nil
    autoTPArmed = true
    autoTPCharacter = nil
    autoTPLastTrigger = 0
    autoTPConn = task.spawn(function()
        while autoTPEnabled do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not autoTPEnabled then
                break
            end
            pcall(function()
                doAutoTPDown(false)
            end)
        end
    end)
end
stopAutoTP = function()
    autoTPEnabled = false
    autoTPLastY = nil
    autoTPArmed = true
    autoTPCharacter = nil
    autoTPLastTrigger = 0
    if autoTPConn then
        task.cancel(autoTPConn)
        autoTPConn = nil
    end
end
local function pauseAutoTPForAimbot()
    if autoTPEnabled and not _autoTPPausedByAimbot then
        _autoTPWasEnabledForAimbot = true
        _autoTPPausedByAimbot = true
        stopAutoTP()
        if setAutoTPVisual then
            setAutoTPVisual(false)
        end
    end
end
local function resumeAutoTPAfterAimbot()
    if not _autoTPPausedByAimbot then
        return
    end
    local shouldResume = _autoTPWasEnabledForAimbot == true
    _autoTPWasEnabledForAimbot = false
    _autoTPPausedByAimbot = false
    if shouldResume and not autoTPEnabled then
        autoTPEnabled = true
        startAutoTP()
        if setAutoTPVisual then
            setAutoTPVisual(true)
        end
    end
end
runTPFloor = function()
    pcall(function()
        doAutoTPDown(true)
    end)
end

do
-- Look At Enemy: nearest living player inside a configurable radius.
-- It only rotates the local character; it does not move or target the Aimbot.
local function stopLookAtEnemy()
    local st = _S2LookAtState
    if st.conn then pcall(function() st.conn:Disconnect() end); st.conn = nil end
    if st.died then pcall(function() st.died:Disconnect() end); st.died = nil end
    if st.align then pcall(function() st.align:Destroy() end); st.align = nil end
    if st.anchor then pcall(function() st.anchor:Destroy() end); st.anchor = nil end
    local c = LP.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if h then h.AutoRotate = true end
end
local function startLookAtEnemy()
    stopLookAtEnemy()
    local st = _S2LookAtState
    local c = LP.Character
    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local anchor = Instance.new("Part")
    anchor.Name = "S2LookAtAnchor"; anchor.Size = Vector3.new(0.2,0.2,0.2)
    anchor.Transparency = 1; anchor.Anchored = true; anchor.CanCollide = false
    anchor.CanTouch = false; anchor.CanQuery = false; anchor.Parent = workspace
    local ext = Instance.new("Attachment"); ext.Parent = anchor
    local localAtt = root:FindFirstChild("RootAttachment") or Instance.new("Attachment")
    localAtt.Name = "RootAttachment"; localAtt.Parent = root
    local align = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
    align.Name = "S2LookAtOrientation"; align.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.TwoAttachment
    align.Attachment0 = localAtt; align.Attachment1 = ext
    align.MaxTorque = 999999; align.MaxAngularVelocity = 999999
    align.Responsiveness = 200; align.Enabled = false; align.Parent = workspace
    st.anchor, st.align = anchor, align
    st.died = hum.Died:Connect(stopLookAtEnemy)
    st.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local me = LP.Character
        local myRoot = me and me:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local myHum = me and me:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not myRoot or not myHum or myHum.Health <= 0 then stopLookAtEnemy(); return end
        local nearest, nearestDist
        for _, other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if other ~= LP then
                local oc = other.Character
                local oroot = oc and oc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local oh = oc and oc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if oroot and oh and oh.Health > 0 then
                    local d = (oroot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if d <= (tonumber(_G._S2LookAtRadius) or 20) and (not nearestDist or d < nearestDist) then
                        nearest, nearestDist = oroot, d
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if nearest then
            myHum.AutoRotate = false; align.Enabled = true
            local target = nearest.Position
            anchor.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(myRoot.Position, Vector3.new(target.X, myRoot.Position.Y, target.Z))
        else
            myHum.AutoRotate = true; align.Enabled = false
        end
    end)
    return true
end
local function setLookAtEnemy(on)
    _G._S2LookAtEnabled = on == true
    if _G._S2LookAtEnabled then startLookAtEnemy() else stopLookAtEnemy() end
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    stopLookAtEnemy()
    if _G._S2LookAtEnabled then task.delay(0.3, startLookAtEnemy) end
end)
LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(stopLookAtEnemy)

-- Float: cycles above the current position and returns via the existing TP Down.
local function stopFloat()
    local st = _S2FloatState
    st.enabled = false; _G._S2FloatEnabled = false
    if st.conn then pcall(function() st.conn:Disconnect() end); st.conn = nil end
    if st.thread then pcall(task.cancel, st.thread); st.thread = nil end
    pcall(runTPFloor)
end
local function startFloat()
    local st = _S2FloatState
    if st.enabled then return end
    st.enabled = true; _G._S2FloatEnabled = true
    st.thread = task.spawn(function()
        while st.enabled do
            local c = LP.Character
            local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local targetY = root.Position.Y + 17
                local start = os.clock()
                while st.enabled and root.Parent and os.clock()-start < 2.5 do
                    local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    local pos, vel = root.Position, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local y = math.min(pos.Y + 55 * math.min(tonumber(dt) or 0.016, 0.05), targetY)
                    local rot = root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, y, pos.Z) * rot
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
                end
                if st.enabled then
                    pcall(runTPFloor)
                    local deadline = os.clock()+3
                    while st.enabled and os.clock()<deadline do
                        local c2=LP.Character; local h2=c2 and c2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not h2 or h2.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then break end
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end
                    if st.enabled then task.wait(0.5) end
                end
            else task.wait(0.2) end
        end
    end)
end
local function toggleFloat(on)
    if on then startFloat() else stopFloat() end
end
_G._S2SetLookAtEnemy = setLookAtEnemy
_G._S2ToggleFloat = toggleFloat
end

-- Local Bat skin cycle, adapted from S2 without importing its UI.
do
    local skins = {
        {name="Original"},
        {name="Diamond Sword", mesh="rbxassetid://8827558932", tex="rbxassetid://8827558969", scale=Vector3.new(0.2,0.2,0.2), c0=CFrame.new(-0.05,-0.1,-0.12)*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90),math.rad(180),math.rad(300))},
        {name="Katana", mesh="rbxassetid://13528902482", tex="rbxassetid://13528902373", scale=Vector3.new(1.4,1.4,1.4), c0=CFrame.new(0,0.6,0)*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(270),math.rad(180),math.rad(180))},
    }
    local original = setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
    local function apply(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") or not tool.Name:lower():find("bat",1,true) then return end
        local handle=tool:FindFirstChild("Handle"); if not handle then return end
        local skin
        for _,v in ipairs(skins) do if v.name==_G._S2BatSkin then skin=v; break end end
        if not skin then skin=skins[1] end
        local replica=handle:FindFirstChild("S2BatSkinReplica")
        if skin.name=="Original" then
            if replica then replica:Destroy() end
            for _,d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                    local v=original[d]; if v~=nil then pcall(function() d.LocalTransparencyModifier=v end); original[d]=nil end
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if replica and replica:GetAttribute("SkinName")==skin.name then return end
        if replica then replica:Destroy() end
        for _,d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                if original[d]==nil then original[d]=d.LocalTransparencyModifier end
                pcall(function() d.LocalTransparencyModifier=1 end)
            end
        end
        local model=Instance.new("Model"); model.Name="S2BatSkinReplica"
        local part=Instance.new("Part"); part.Name="MainPart"; part.Size=Vector3.new(1,1,1)
        part.CanCollide=false; part.CanTouch=false; part.CanQuery=false; part.Massless=true; part.Transparency=0
        part.Parent=model
        local mesh=Instance.new("SpecialMesh"); mesh.MeshType=Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId=skin.mesh; mesh.TextureId=skin.tex; mesh.Scale=skin.scale; mesh.Parent=part
        local motor=Instance.new("Motor6D"); motor.Part0=handle; motor.Part1=part; motor.C0=skin.c0; motor.Parent=part
        model:SetAttribute("SkinName", skin.name)
        model.Parent=handle
    end
    _G._S2SetBatSkin=function(name)
        for _,v in ipairs(skins) do if v.name==name then _G._S2BatSkin=name; break end end
        local c=LP.Character; local bp=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        for _,container in ipairs({c,bp}) do
            if container then for _,tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do pcall(apply,tool) end end
        end
        return _G._S2BatSkin
    end
    _G._S2CycleBatSkin=function()
        local idx=1; for i,v in ipairs(skins) do if v.name==_G._S2BatSkin then idx=i; break end end
        idx=idx%#skins+1
        return _G._S2SetBatSkin(skins[idx].name)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.6)
            local c=LP.Character; local bp=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            for _,container in ipairs({c,bp}) do
                if container then for _,tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do pcall(apply,tool) end end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Rainbow Bat: identifica o bastão mesmo quando o jogo usa nome genérico e recolore partes/meshes.
do
    local rainbowWasEnabled = _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled == true
    if type(_G.S2SetRainbowBat) == "function" then
        pcall(_G.S2SetRainbowBat, false)
    end
    _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled = rainbowWasEnabled

    local renderConn = nil
    local saved = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local scanAt = 0
    local hueSeed = 0

    local function isNamedBat(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
        local name = string.lower(tool.Name)
        return string.find(name, "bat", 1, true) ~= nil
            or string.find(name, "slap", 1, true) ~= nil
            or tool:FindFirstChild("S2BatSkinReplica", true) ~= nil
    end

    local function isExcludedFallback(tool)
        local name = string.lower(tool.Name)
        return string.find(name, "carpet", 1, true)
            or string.find(name, "medusa", 1, true)
            or string.find(name, "head", 1, true)
            or string.find(name, "stone", 1, true)
    end

    local function findBatTools()
        local results, equippedFallback = {}, {}
        local character = LP.Character
        local backpack = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        for _, container in ipairs({ character, backpack }) do
            if container then
                for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        if isNamedBat(tool) then
                            results[#results + 1] = tool
                        elseif container == character and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") and not isExcludedFallback(tool) then
                            -- Alguns jogos dão ao bastão um nome genérico; o único Tool equipado
                            -- com Handle é o fallback enquanto não há um nome reconhecível.
                            equippedFallback[#equippedFallback + 1] = tool
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #results == 0 then
            for _, tool in ipairs(equippedFallback) do results[#results + 1] = tool end
        end
        return results
    end

    local function saveProperty(obj, prop)
        local row = saved[obj]
        if not row then row = {}; saved[obj] = row end
        if row[prop] == nil then pcall(function() row[prop] = obj[prop] end) end
    end

    local function scan()
        for _, tool in ipairs(findBatTools()) do
            for _, obj in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                    saveProperty(obj, "Color")
                    saveProperty(obj, "Material")
                elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                    saveProperty(obj, "VertexColor")
                elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                    saveProperty(obj, "Color3")
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function paint(now)
        local index = 0
        for obj in pairs(saved) do
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                index = index + 1
                local color = Color3.fromHSV((now * 0.28 + hueSeed + index * 0.047) % 1, 1, 1)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        obj.Color = color
                        obj.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                    elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                        obj.VertexColor = Vector3.new(color.R, color.G, color.B)
                    elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                        obj.Color3 = color
                    end
                end)
            else
                saved[obj] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function restore()
        for obj, properties in pairs(saved) do
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                for prop, value in pairs(properties) do
                    pcall(function() obj[prop] = value end)
                end
            end
            saved[obj] = nil
        end
    end

    local function stop()
        if renderConn then pcall(function() renderConn:Disconnect() end); renderConn = nil end
        restore()
    end

    local function start()
        stop()
        scan()
        scanAt = 0
        hueSeed = math.random()
        paint(os.clock())
        renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - scanAt >= 0.08 then
                scan()
                scanAt = now
            end
            paint(now)
        end)
    end

    _G.S2SetRainbowBat = function(on)
        _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled = on == true
        if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled then start() else stop() end
        return _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled
    end

    local function watchCharacter(char)
        if not char then return end
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled and child:IsA("Tool") then
                task.defer(function() scan(); paint(os.clock()) end)
            end
        end)
    end
    watchCharacter(LP.Character)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        watchCharacter(char)
        if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled then
            task.defer(function()
                if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled then start() end
            end)
        end
    end)
    local backpack = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if backpack then
        backpack.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled and child:IsA("Tool") then
                task.defer(function() scan(); paint(os.clock()) end)
            end
        end)
    end
    LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Backpack") then
            child.ChildAdded:Connect(function(tool)
                if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled and tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    task.defer(function() scan(); paint(os.clock()) end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    if _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled then start() end
end

-- TP Ragdoll: separate from the existing Anti Ragdoll toggle.
do
    local conn, was, generation = nil, false, 0
    local routes = {
        right={{x=-461.00,z=62.58},{x=-459.24,z=-11.13},{x=-484.88,z=24.08}},
        left={{x=-461.00,z=62.58},{x=-468.65,z=133.95},{x=-484.88,z=96.05}},
    }
    local function runRoute(token)
        local side = (autoPlaySide == "right" or autoPlaySide == "R") and "right" or "left"
        task.delay(1.15,function()
            if token~=generation then return end
            local old=_G._S2TPDownMethod; _G._S2TPDownMethod="NORMAL"
            pcall(doAutoTPDown,true)
            _G._S2TPDownMethod=old
        end)
        task.delay(1.25,function()
            if token~=generation then return end
            local pts=routes[side]
            for _,pt in ipairs(pts) do
                if token~=generation then return end
                local ch=LP.Character
                if not ch then break end
                pcall(function() local pos=ch:GetPivot().Position; ch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pt.x,pos.Y,pt.z)) end)
                task.wait(0.095)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function stop()
        generation=generation+1; was=false
        if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end); conn=nil end
    end
    local function start()
        stop()
        conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not _G._S2TPRagdollEnabled then return end
            local ch=LP.Character; local hum=ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health<=0 then was=false; return end
            local st=hum:GetState()
            local down=(st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll)
            if down and not was then
                was=true; generation=generation+1; runRoute(generation)
            elseif not down then was=false end
        end)
    end
    _G._S2SetTPRagdoll=function(on)
        _G._S2TPRagdollEnabled=on==true
        if _G._S2TPRagdollEnabled then start() else stop() end
    end
end


-- Event-driven player collision isolation module ported from S2.
do
  local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
-- ============================================================
-- NO PLAYER COLLISION v3 (event-driven)
-- Sostituisce il vecchio blocco a polling. Differenze:
--   1. ZERO loop. Ogni BasePart e' registrata una volta con
--      GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"): se qualcosa la riaccende
--      torna a false nello STESSO frame, non dopo 0.2s.
--   2. DescendantAdded sul character: accessori, tool equipaggiati e
--      arti che arrivano tardi al respawn vengono presi subito.
--   3. Copre anche l'assembly esterna (brainrot trasportato, hitbox
--      weldate ma parented in workspace), che prima era ignorata.
--   4. Spegne anche le TUE parti: sono le uniche di cui hai il network
--      ownership, quindi replicano davvero sul client dell'altro.
--   5. Ref-count: TP Bat e toggle utente possono tenerla accesa insieme
--      senza pestarsi i piedi (S2NoCollisionAcquire/Release).
-- Tutto dentro un do-end: non consuma slot locals nel main chunk.
-- ============================================================
_G.S2NoPlayerCollisionState = _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionState or {}

do
  local NPC = _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionState
  NPC.connections = NPC.connections or {}
  NPC.parts       = NPC.parts or {}       -- part -> {conn, destroy, cc, ct}
  NPC.charConns   = NPC.charConns or {}   -- character -> {conns}
  NPC.holders     = NPC.holders or {}     -- tag -> true
  NPC.running     = NPC.running or false

  -- Parti del corpo R6 + R15
  local BODY = {
    HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, Torso = true, UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
    ["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true, ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
    LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
    RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
    LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
    RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
  }

  local function isOn() return NPC.running == true end

  -- Rete di sicurezza per le scritture interne del motore che non emettono
  -- GetPropertyChangedSignal. Passa su tutti i giocatori, ma UNA VOLTA AL
  -- SECONDO, non ad ogni frame come faceva il vecchio TP Bat.
  -- Abbassalo a 0.25 se vedi ancora micro-spinte entrando nei nemici;
  -- mettilo a 0 per restare puramente event-driven.
  local SELF_VERIFY_INTERVAL = 1

  -- ---------- registro parti ----------
  local function lockPart(part, killTouch)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    if NPC.parts[part] then return end

    local e = {cc = part.CanCollide, ct = part.CanTouch}
    NPC.parts[part] = e

    pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
    if killTouch then pcall(function() part.CanTouch = false end) end

    -- Cuore del fix: reazione immediata, non ogni 0.2s.
    e.conn = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function()
      if not isOn() then return end
      if part.CanCollide then pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end) end
    end)

    e.destroy = part.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
      if part:IsDescendantOf(game) then return end
      if e.conn then pcall(function() e.conn:Disconnect() end) end
      if e.destroy then pcall(function() e.destroy:Disconnect() end) end
      NPC.parts[part] = nil
    end)
  end

  local function unlockPart(part)
    local e = NPC.parts[part]
    if not e then return end
    if e.conn then pcall(function() e.conn:Disconnect() end) end
    if e.destroy then pcall(function() e.destroy:Disconnect() end) end
    NPC.parts[part] = nil
    if part and part.Parent then
      pcall(function() part.CanCollide = e.cc end)
      pcall(function() part.CanTouch = e.ct end)
    end
  end

  local hookHumanoid

  -- Riscrive CanCollide = false sulle parti gia' registrate di un character.
  -- Serve quando il motore le rimette a true senza emettere il segnale.
  local function reassert(char, isSelf)
    if not char then return end
    for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
      if obj:IsA("BasePart") and NPC.parts[obj] then
        if not (isSelf and obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart") then
          if obj.CanCollide then pcall(function() obj.CanCollide = false end) end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  hookHumanoid = function(hum, char, isSelf)
    if not NPC.charConns[char] then NPC.charConns[char] = {} end
    table.insert(NPC.charConns[char], hum.StateChanged:Connect(function()
      if not isOn() then return end
      -- differita di un frame: l'Humanoid scrive dopo il cambio di stato
      task.defer(function()
        if isOn() and char.Parent then reassert(char, isSelf) end
      end)
    end))
    table.insert(NPC.charConns[char], hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FloorMaterial"):Connect(function()
      if not isOn() then return end
      task.defer(function()
        if isOn() and char.Parent then reassert(char, isSelf) end
      end)
    end))
  end

  -- ---------- aggancio character ----------
  -- isSelf: sul tuo character l'HRP resta collidibile o l'Humanoid sprofonda.
  local function hookCharacter(char, isSelf)
    if not char then return end
    if NPC.charConns[char] then return end
    NPC.charConns[char] = {}

    local function handle(obj)
      if not isOn() then return end
      if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
      if isSelf and obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then return end
      lockPart(obj, not isSelf)
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do handle(obj) end
    table.insert(NPC.charConns[char], char.DescendantAdded:Connect(handle))

    -- Salto, caduta, atterraggio, ragdoll: l'Humanoid rimette mano alle
    -- collisioni. Qui le riportiamo giu' subito dopo, senza polling.
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then
      local hc
      hc = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
        if c:IsA("Humanoid") then
          hc:Disconnect()
          hookHumanoid(c, char, isSelf)
        end
      end)
      table.insert(NPC.charConns[char], hc)
    else
      hookHumanoid(hum, char, isSelf)
    end
    table.insert(NPC.charConns[char], char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
      if parent == nil then
        for _, c in ipairs(NPC.charConns[char] or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        NPC.charConns[char] = nil
      end
    end))
  end

  -- ---------- assembly esterne (brainrot trasportato, hitbox) ----------
  local function ownedByOtherPlayer(part)
    local root = part.AssemblyRootPart
    if not root then return false end
    local model = root:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not model then return false end
    local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
    return plr ~= nil and plr ~= LP
  end

  local function hookWorkspacePart(obj)
    if not isOn() then return end
    if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
    if NPC.parts[obj] then return end
    -- il weld arriva un frame dopo il parenting
    task.defer(function()
      if not isOn() then return end
      if not obj.Parent then return end
      if ownedByOtherPlayer(obj) then lockPart(obj, true) end
    end)
  end

  -- ---------- start / stop interni ----------
  local function startCore()
    if NPC.running then return end
    NPC.running = true

    for _, c in ipairs(NPC.connections or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    NPC.connections = {}
    local function track(c) table.insert(NPC.connections, c) end

    local function watchPlayer(plr)
      if plr == LP then return end
      if plr.Character then hookCharacter(plr.Character, false) end
      track(plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if isOn() then hookCharacter(char, false) end
      end))
    end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do watchPlayer(plr) end
    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer))

    if LP.Character then hookCharacter(LP.Character, true) end
    track(LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
      if isOn() then hookCharacter(char, true) end
    end))

    for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do hookWorkspacePart(obj) end
    track(Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(hookWorkspacePart))

    if SELF_VERIFY_INTERVAL > 0 then
      local acc = 0
      track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not isOn() then return end
        acc = acc + (dt or 0)
        if acc < SELF_VERIFY_INTERVAL then return end
        acc = 0
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
          local char = plr.Character
          if char then
            local isSelf = (plr == LP)
            reassert(char, isSelf)
            -- parti mai registrate (comparse in una finestra scoperta)
            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
              if obj:IsA("BasePart") and not NPC.parts[obj] then
                if not (isSelf and obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart") then
                  lockPart(obj, not isSelf)
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end))
    end
  end

  local function stopCore()
    if not NPC.running then return end
    NPC.running = false

    for _, c in ipairs(NPC.connections or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    NPC.connections = {}

    for char, conns in pairs(NPC.charConns) do
      for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
      NPC.charConns[char] = nil
    end

    for part in pairs(NPC.parts) do unlockPart(part) end
    NPC.parts = {}
  end

  -- Ripassata completa: riaggancia character e parti gia' presenti.
  -- Serve dopo un cambio di holder, per non restare in attesa del
  -- prossimo DescendantAdded.
  local function rescan()
    if not isOn() then return end
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
      local char = plr.Character
      if char then
        if plr == LP then
          for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then lockPart(obj, false) end
          end
        else
          for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then lockPart(obj, true) end
          end
        end
        hookCharacter(char, plr == LP)
      end
    end
  end

  -- ---------- API ref-count ----------
  -- Permette a TP Bat e toggle utente di tenerla accesa insieme.
  function _G.S2NoCollisionAcquire(tag)
    NPC.holders[tag or "default"] = true
    startCore()
  end

  function _G.S2NoCollisionRelease(tag)
    NPC.holders[tag or "default"] = nil
    -- Il toggle utente vince sempre: finche' e' acceso non si spegne nulla,
    -- anche se il tag "user" si fosse perso per strada.
    if _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionEnabled == true then
      NPC.holders["user"] = true
      rescan()
      return
    end
    if next(NPC.holders) == nil then stopCore() end
  end
end

-- Toggle utente (chiamato dalla UI e da applyS2Config)
function enableNoPlayerCollision()
  _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionEnabled = true
  _G.S2NoCollisionAcquire("user")
end

function disableNoPlayerCollision()
  _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
  _G.S2NoCollisionRelease("user")
end

-- Compatibilita' con il vecchio nome usato altrove nello script
function _G.S2SetOtherPlayerCollision(state)
  if state == false then
    _G.S2NoCollisionAcquire("legacy")
  else
    _G.S2NoCollisionRelease("legacy")
  end
end

end

defLightBrightness, defLightClock, defLightAmbient = nil, nil, nil
function optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(root, isActive, processObject, seen, batchSize)
    if not root or not processObject then
        return false
    end
    seen = seen or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    batchSize = math.max(1, tonumber(batchSize) or 48)
    local stack = { { node = root } }
    local processed = 0
    while #stack > 0 do
        if isActive and not isActive() then
            return false
        end
        local frame = stack[#stack]
        if not frame.children then
            local ok, children = pcall(function()
                return frame.node:GetChildren()
            end)
            frame.children = ok and children or {}
            frame.index = 1
        end
        local child = frame.children[frame.index]
        if child then
            frame.index = frame.index + 1
            if child.Parent then
                if not seen[child] then
                    seen[child] = true
                    pcall(processObject, child)
                end
                if child.Parent then
                    stack[#stack + 1] = { node = child }
                end
            end
            processed = processed + 1
            if processed >= batchSize then
                processed = 0
                task.wait()
            end
        else
            stack[#stack] = nil
        end
    end
    return true
end
