function optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processObject, seen)
    if not queueState or not obj or not obj.Parent or not isActive() or seen[obj] or queueState.pending[obj] then
        return
    end
    queueState.tail = queueState.tail + 1
    queueState.items[queueState.tail] = obj
    queueState.pending[obj] = true
    if queueState.worker then
        return
    end
    queueState.worker = true
    local worker = task.spawn(function()
        while isActive() do
            local processed = 0
            while queueState.head <= queueState.tail and processed < 48 and isActive() do
                local item = queueState.items[queueState.head]
                queueState.items[queueState.head] = nil
                queueState.head = queueState.head + 1
                queueState.pending[item] = nil
                processed = processed + 1
                if item and item.Parent and not seen[item] then
                    seen[item] = true
                    pcall(processObject, item)
                end
            end
            if queueState.head > queueState.tail then
                break
            end
            task.wait()
        end
        queueState.items = {}
        queueState.head = 1
        queueState.tail = 0
        queueState.pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        queueState.worker = nil
    end)
    if queueState.worker == true then
        queueState.worker = worker
    end
end
local function applyAntiLagDerender(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then
            obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            obj.Reflectance = 0
            obj.CastShadow = false
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif
            obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or obj:IsA("Trail")
            or obj:IsA("Beam")
            or obj:IsA("Fire")
            or obj:IsA("Smoke")
            or obj:IsA("Sparkles")
        then
            obj.Enabled = false
        end
    end)
end
enableAntiLag = function()
    if antiLagEnabled then return end
    stopOtherWorkspaceOptimizers("antiLag")
    antiLagSession = (antiLagSession or 0) + 1
    local session = antiLagSession
    local seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local queueState = { items = {}, head = 1, tail = 0, pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    local function isActive()
        return antiLagEnabled and antiLagSession == session
    end
    local function processObject(obj)
        applyAntiLagDerender(obj)
    end
    removeAccessoriesEnabled = true
    antiLagEnabled = true
    defLightBrightness = defLightBrightness or Lighting.Brightness
    defLightClock = defLightClock or Lighting.ClockTime
    defLightAmbient = defLightAmbient or Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
    Lighting.Brightness = 1
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(function()
            if effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or effect:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                or effect:IsA("BloomEffect") or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                effect.Enabled = false
            end
        end)
    end
    if antiLagDescConn then antiLagDescConn:Disconnect() end
    antiLagDescConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processObject, seen)
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
        optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(workspace, isActive, processObject, seen, 48)
    end)
end

disableAntiLag = function()
    removeAccessoriesEnabled = false
    antiLagEnabled = false
    antiLagSession = (antiLagSession or 0) + 1
    if antiLagDescConn then
        antiLagDescConn:Disconnect()
        antiLagDescConn = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        if defLightBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = defLightBrightness end
        if defLightClock then Lighting.ClockTime = defLightClock end
        if defLightAmbient then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = defLightAmbient end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
    end)
end
applyAntiLagV2Derender = function(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then
            local char = LP.Character
            if char and obj:IsDescendantOf(char) then return end
            obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            obj.Reflectance = 0
            obj.CastShadow = false
            obj.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            obj.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            obj.FrontSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            obj.BackSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            obj.LeftSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
            obj.RightSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") then
            obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif obj:IsA("Texture") then
            obj.Transparency = 0.5
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam")
            or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
            obj.Enabled = false
        elseif obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            obj.Enabled = false
        elseif obj:IsA("SelectionBox") and obj.Name ~= "VortexESP_HL" then
            obj:Destroy()
        end
    end)
end
enableAntiLagV2 = function()
    if antiLagV2Enabled then return end
    stopOtherWorkspaceOptimizers("antiLagV2")
    antiLagV2Session = (antiLagV2Session or 0) + 1
    local session = antiLagV2Session
    local seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local queueState = { items = {}, head = 1, tail = 0, pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    local function isActive()
        return antiLagV2Enabled and antiLagV2Session == session
    end
    local function processObject(obj)
        applyAntiLagV2Derender(obj)
    end
    antiLagV2Enabled = true
    defLightBrightness = defLightBrightness or Lighting.Brightness
    defLightClock = defLightClock or Lighting.ClockTime
    defLightAmbient = defLightAmbient or Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
    pcall(function()
        settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = 1
        settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Disabled
    end)
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
    Lighting.Brightness = 1
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    Lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0
    Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
    pcall(function() Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Compatibility end)
    for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(function()
            if effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or effect:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                or effect:IsA("BloomEffect") or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or effect:IsA("BrightnessEffect")
                or effect:IsA("DitheringEffect") then
                effect.Enabled = false
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
        workspace.Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
        workspace.Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
        workspace.Terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
        workspace.Terrain.Decoration = false
    end)
    if antiLagV2DescConn then antiLagV2DescConn:Disconnect() end
    antiLagV2DescConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processObject, seen)
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
        optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(workspace, isActive, processObject, seen, 48)
    end)
end

disableAntiLagV2 = function()
    antiLagV2Enabled = false
    antiLagV2Session = (antiLagV2Session or 0) + 1
    if antiLagV2DescConn then
        antiLagV2DescConn:Disconnect()
        antiLagV2DescConn = nil
    end
    pcall(function()
        if defLightBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = defLightBrightness end
        if defLightClock then Lighting.ClockTime = defLightClock end
        if defLightAmbient then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = defLightAmbient end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
    end)
end

-- Anti Lag V3: reversible version of the low-render-cost workspace pass.
do
    local saved=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
    local terrainSaved=nil
    local renderSaved=nil
    local function setSaved(obj,prop,value)
        local row=saved[obj]
        if not row then row={}; saved[obj]=row end
        if row[prop]==nil then pcall(function() row[prop]=obj[prop] end) end
        pcall(function() obj[prop]=value end)
    end
    local function optimize(obj)
        if not antiLagV3Enabled or not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            for prop,val in pairs({Material=Enum.Material.Plastic,Reflectance=0,CastShadow=false,
                TopSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,BottomSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
                FrontSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,BackSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
                LeftSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,RightSurface=Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth}) do setSaved(obj,prop,val) end
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            setSaved(obj,"Transparency",0.5)
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam")
            or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles")
            or obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            setSaved(obj,"Enabled",false)
        elseif obj:IsA("SelectionBox") then
            setSaved(obj,"Visible",false)
        end
    end
    local function restore()
        for obj,row in pairs(saved) do
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                for prop,value in pairs(row) do pcall(function() obj[prop]=value end) end
            end
            saved[obj]=nil
        end
        if terrainSaved then
            local terrain=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
            if terrain then for prop,value in pairs(terrainSaved) do pcall(function() terrain[prop]=value end) end end
            terrainSaved=nil
        end
        if renderSaved then
            pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel=renderSaved.QualityLevel end)
            pcall(function() settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel=renderSaved.MeshPartDetailLevel end)
            renderSaved=nil
        end
    end
    local function stop()
        antiLagV3Enabled=false; _G._S2AntiLagV3Enabled=false
        if antiLagV3DescConn then pcall(function() antiLagV3DescConn:Disconnect() end); antiLagV3DescConn=nil end
        restore()
    end
    local function start()
        if antiLagV3Enabled then return end
        antiLagV3Enabled=true; _G._S2AntiLagV3Enabled=true
        pcall(function()
            local r=settings().Rendering
            renderSaved={QualityLevel=r.QualityLevel,MeshPartDetailLevel=r.MeshPartDetailLevel}
            r.QualityLevel=1; r.MeshPartDetailLevel=Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Disabled
        end)
        for prop,val in pairs({GlobalShadows=false,FogEnd=1e10,FogStart=0,Brightness=1,
            EnvironmentDiffuseScale=0,EnvironmentSpecularScale=0,ShadowSoftness=0,ExposureCompensation=0}) do
            setSaved(Lighting,prop,val)
        end
        pcall(function() setSaved(Lighting,"Technology",Enum.Technology.Compatibility) end)
        for _,effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            local keep=effect.Name=="S2VividColor" or effect.Name=="S2VividBloom" or effect.Name=="S2MotionBlur"
                or effect:GetAttribute("S2Vivid")==true
            if not keep and (effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                or effect:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or effect:IsA("BloomEffect")
                or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or effect:IsA("BrightnessEffect")
                or effect:IsA("DitheringEffect")) then setSaved(effect,"Enabled",false) end
        end
        local terrain=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
        if terrain then
            terrainSaved={}
            for _,prop in ipairs({"WaterWaveSize","WaterWaveSpeed","WaterReflectance","WaterTransparency","Decoration"}) do
                pcall(function() terrainSaved[prop]=terrain[prop] end)
            end
            pcall(function() terrain.WaterWaveSize=0; terrain.WaterWaveSpeed=0; terrain.WaterReflectance=0; terrain.WaterTransparency=0; terrain.Decoration=false end)
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local batch=0
            for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if not antiLagV3Enabled then break end
                if not (LP.Character and obj:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character)) then optimize(obj) end
                batch=batch+1
                if batch>=100 then batch=0; task.wait() end
            end
        end)
        if antiLagV3DescConn then pcall(function() antiLagV3DescConn:Disconnect() end) end
        antiLagV3DescConn=workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            task.defer(function()
                if antiLagV3Enabled and not (LP.Character and obj:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character)) then optimize(obj) end
            end)
        end)
    end
    _G._S2SetAntiLagV3=function(on)
        if on then start() else stop() end
    end
end
local function findMedusa()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then
        return nil
    end
    for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
        if t:IsA("Tool") then
            local n = t.Name:lower()
            if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then
                return t
            end
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if t:IsA("Tool") then
                local n = t.Name:lower()
                if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then
                    return t
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function useMedusaCounter()
    if medusaDebounce then
        return
    end
    if MEDUSA_COOLDOWN > (tick() - medusaLastUsed) then
        return
    end
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then
        return
    end
    medusaDebounce = true
    local med = findMedusa()
    if not med then
        medusaDebounce = false
        return
    end
    if med.Parent ~= c then
        local hum2 = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum2 then
            hum2:EquipTool(med)
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        med:Activate()
    end)
    medusaLastUsed = tick()
    medusaDebounce = false
end
local function onAnchorChanged(part)
    return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
        if part.Anchored and part.Transparency == 1 then
            if medusaCounterEnabled then
                useMedusaCounter()
            end
        end
    end)
end
setupMedusa = function(char)
    for _, c in pairs(Conns.anchor) do
        pcall(function()
            c:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    Conns.anchor = {}
    if not char then
        return
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            table.insert(Conns.anchor, onAnchorChanged(part))
        end
    end
    table.insert(
        Conns.anchor,
        char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part)
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(Conns.anchor, onAnchorChanged(part))
            end
        end)
    )
end
stopMedusaCounter = function()
    for _, c in pairs(Conns.anchor) do
        pcall(function()
            c:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    Conns.anchor = {}
end
BAT_COUNTER_SLAP_LIST = {
    "Bat",
    "Slap",
    "Iron Slap",
    "Gold Slap",
    "Diamond Slap",
    "Emerald Slap",
    "Ruby Slap",
    "Dark Matter Slap",
    "Flame Slap",
    "Nuclear Slap",
    "Galaxy Slap",
    "Glitched Slap",
}
local function findBatForCounter()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then
        return nil
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    for _, name in ipairs(BAT_COUNTER_SLAP_LIST) do
        local t = c:FindFirstChild(name) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
        if t then
            return t
        end
    end
    for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then
            return ch
        end
    end
    if bp then
        for _, ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then
                return ch
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function swingBatForCounter(bat, char)
    local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if bat.Parent ~= char then
        if hum2 then
            pcall(function()
                hum2:EquipTool(bat)
            end)
        end
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    local remote = bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
    if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer()
        end)
        task.wait(0.15)
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer()
        end)
    else
        pcall(function()
            bat:Activate()
        end)
        task.wait(0.15)
        pcall(function()
            bat:Activate()
        end)
    end
end
startBatCounter = function()
    if Conns.batCounter then
        return
    end
    Conns.batCounter = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not batCounterEnabled or batCounterDebounce then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum2 then
            return
        end
        local st = hum2:GetState()
        if
            st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        then
            batCounterDebounce = true
            task.spawn(function()
                local bat = findBatForCounter()
                if bat then
                    swingBatForCounter(bat, char)
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
                batCounterDebounce = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end
stopBatCounter = function()
    if Conns.batCounter then
        Conns.batCounter:Disconnect()
        Conns.batCounter = nil
    end
    batCounterDebounce = false
end
aimbotConn = nil
aimbotV2Conn = nil
local function findBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return nil
    end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
            return tool
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
                return tool
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function getClosestTarget()
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then
                    minDist = dist
                    closest = tRoot
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end
local function swingCurrentBat()
    if not autoSwingEnabled then
        return
    end
    local bat = findBat()
    if bat and bat.Parent == LP.Character and bat:IsA("Tool") then
        pcall(function()
            bat:Activate()
        end)
    end
end
local function getClosestPlayerAim(maxDist)
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return nil, math.huge
    end
    local closest, minDist = nil, maxDist or math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then
                    minDist = dist
                    closest = plr
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest, minDist
end
_predBall = nil
findBatForAimbot = function()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return nil
    end
    for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
            return tool
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
                return tool
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function getConfiguredAimbotSpeed(defaultValue)
    local custom
    if laggerModeEnabled or laggerCarryActive then
        custom = tonumber(_G._S2_AimLaggerSpeed or aimbotLaggerSpeedValue)
    else
        custom = tonumber(_G._S2_AimSpeed or aimbotSpeedValue)
    end
    if custom and custom > 0 then
        return custom
    end
    return tonumber(defaultValue) or 58
end
local function getConfiguredAimbotHitDistance(defaultValue)
    local custom = tonumber(_G._S2_AimDist)
    if custom and custom > 0 then
        return custom
    end
    return tonumber(defaultValue) or 12
end
startBatAimbot = function()
    if aimbotConn then
        aimbotConn:Disconnect()
    end
    autoBatEnabled = true
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoLeft()
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    _autoTPWasEnabledForBat = autoTPEnabled == true
    pauseAutoTPForAimbot()
    pauseAntiBatForAimbot()
    autoBatEnabled = true
    _aimbotTarget = nil
    _aimbotLastScan = 0
    _aimbotSwingCooldown = false
    autoBatEquippedThisRun = false
    aimbotConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not autoBatEnabled then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then
            return
        end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then
            return
        end
        local bat = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or findBatForAimbot()
        if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function()
                hum:EquipTool(bat)
            end)
        end
        local now = tick()
        local target = _aimbotTarget
        if now - (_aimbotLastScan or 0) > 0.1 or not target or not target.Parent then
            _aimbotLastScan = now
            target = nil
            local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                    local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local th = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if tRoot and th and th.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < minDist then
                            minDist = dist
                            closest = tRoot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            target = closest
            _aimbotTarget = target
        else
            local th = target.Parent and target.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not th or th.Health <= 0 then
                _aimbotTarget = nil
                target = nil
            end
        end
        if not target then
            hum.AutoRotate = true
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return
        end
        hum.AutoRotate = false
        local targetVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local myPos = root.Position
        local targetPos = target.Position
        local predictPos = targetPos + targetVel * 0.14 + target.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3
        local direction = predictPos - myPos
        if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            return
        end
        local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
        if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            return
        end
        flatDir = flatDir.Unit
        local chaseSpeed = getConfiguredAimbotSpeed(58)
        local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
        local yVel = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5 + targetVel.Y * 0.8
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
            yVel = math.max(yVel, 13)
        end
        yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)
        local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed)
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)
        local speed3 = targetVel.Magnitude
        local predictTime = math.clamp(speed3 / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
        local predictedPos = targetPos + targetVel * predictTime
        local toPredict = predictedPos - myPos
        if toPredict.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, predictedPos)
            local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
            local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
            rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
            ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
            rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
        end
        if autoSwingEnabled and bat and not _aimbotSwingCooldown then
            _aimbotSwingCooldown = true
            pcall(function()
                bat:Activate()
            end)
            task.delay(0.08, function()
                _aimbotSwingCooldown = false
            end)
        end
    end)
    if autoBatSetVisual then
        autoBatSetVisual(true)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
        mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true)
    end
end
isSummerBaseName = function(name)
    if not name then
        return false
    end
    local n = tostring(name):lower()
    return n == "summerbase"
        or n == "summer_base"
        or n:find("summerbase", 1, true) ~= nil
        or n:find("summer_base", 1, true) ~= nil
end
isAnchorName = function(name)
    if not name then
        return false
    end
    local n = tostring(name):lower()
    return n == "anchor" or n == "anchors"
end
stripBlockingAnchor = function(obj)
    if not obj or not obj.Parent then
        return
    end
    local key = tostring(obj:GetFullName())
    if _antiSummerCleaned[key] then
        return
    end
    _antiSummerCleaned[key] = true
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
            obj.CanCollide = false
            obj.CanQuery = false
            obj.CanTouch = false
            obj.Transparency = 1
        end
        obj:Destroy()
    end)
end
cleanSummerBaseAnchors = function()
    if not antiSummerBaseEnabled then return end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    local session = antiSummerBaseSession
    local function isActive()
        return antiSummerBaseEnabled and antiSummerBaseSession == session
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, plotList = pcall(function() return plots:GetChildren() end)
        if not ok then return end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plotList) do
            if not isActive() then return end
            if plot and plot.Parent then
                local isSummer = isSummerBaseName(plot.Name)
                if not isSummer then
                    local searchSeen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
                    local function searchActive()
                        return isActive() and not isSummer
                    end
                    optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(plot, searchActive, function(obj)
                        if isSummerBaseName(obj.Name) then isSummer = true end
                    end, searchSeen, 48)
                end
                if isSummer and isActive() then
                    local anchorSeen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
                    optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(plot, isActive, function(obj)
                        if isAnchorName(obj.Name) then stripBlockingAnchor(obj) end
                    end, anchorSeen, 48)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
enableAntiSummerBase = function()
    antiSummerBaseSession = (antiSummerBaseSession or 0) + 1
    local session = antiSummerBaseSession
    local seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local queueState = { items = {}, head = 1, tail = 0, pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    local function isActive()
        return antiSummerBaseEnabled and antiSummerBaseSession == session
    end
    local function processAnchor(obj)
        if not obj.Parent or not isAnchorName(obj.Name) then return end
        local ancestor = obj
        local underPlots, nearSummer = false, false
        while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
            if ancestor.Name == "Plots" or (ancestor.Parent and ancestor.Parent.Name == "Plots") then
                underPlots = true
            end
            if isSummerBaseName(ancestor.Name) then
                nearSummer = true
            end
            ancestor = ancestor.Parent
        end
        if underPlots and nearSummer then stripBlockingAnchor(obj) end
    end
    antiSummerBaseEnabled = true
    _antiSummerCleaned = {}
    if antiSummerBaseConn then
        pcall(function() antiSummerBaseConn:Disconnect() end)
        antiSummerBaseConn = nil
    end
    antiSummerBaseConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if isAnchorName(obj.Name) then
            optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processAnchor, seen)
        end
    end)
    cleanSummerBaseAnchors()
end
disableAntiSummerBase = function()
    antiSummerBaseEnabled = false
    antiSummerBaseSession = (antiSummerBaseSession or 0) + 1
    if antiSummerBaseConn then
        pcall(function()
            antiSummerBaseConn:Disconnect()
        end)
        antiSummerBaseConn = nil
    end
end
local function removeFace(head)
    local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
    if face then
        face:Destroy()
    end
end
applyHeadlessToChar = function(char, enabled)
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then
        return
    end
    if enabled then
        head.Transparency = 1
        head.CanCollide = false
        removeFace(head)
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.MeshId == "rbxassetid://1095708" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://1095708"
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
        mesh.Name = "HeadlessMesh"
        mesh.Parent = head
        head:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
            if head.Transparency ~= 1 then
                head.Transparency = 1
            end
        end)
        head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "face" and child:IsA("Decal") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    else
        head.Transparency = 0
        head.CanCollide = true
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "HeadlessMesh" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        removeFace(head)
    end
end
applyKorbloxToChar = function(char, enabled)
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end
    if enabled then
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                    if rightLeg.Color ~= Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64) then
                        rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                    end
                end)
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
                mesh.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = rightLeg
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then
                    rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1
                end
                if rightFoot then
                    rightFoot.Transparency = 1
                end
                local oldKorblox = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if oldKorblox then
                    oldKorblox:Destroy()
                end
                local korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                korbloxLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                korbloxLeg.Parent = char
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
                mesh.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg
                local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
            end
        end
    else
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = LIGHT_BLUE
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then
                    rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0
                end
                if rightFoot then
                    rightFoot.Transparency = 0
                end
                local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if korbloxLeg then
                    korbloxLeg:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
applyCharterToChar = function(char)
    if not char then
        return
    end
    applyHeadlessToChar(char, headlessEnabled)
    applyKorbloxToChar(char, korbloxEnabled)
end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.15)
    applyCharterToChar(char)
end)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        applyCharterToChar(char)
    end
end)
toggleHeadless = function()
    headlessEnabled = not headlessEnabled
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        applyHeadlessToChar(char, headlessEnabled)
    end
    if headlessSetVisual then
        headlessSetVisual(headlessEnabled)
    end
    return headlessEnabled
end
toggleKorblox = function()
    korbloxEnabled = not korbloxEnabled
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        applyKorbloxToChar(char, korbloxEnabled)
    end
    if korbloxSetVisual then
        korbloxSetVisual(korbloxEnabled)
    end
    return korbloxEnabled
end
stopBatAimbot = function()
    if aimbotConn then
        aimbotConn:Disconnect()
        aimbotConn = nil
    end
    autoBatEnabled = false
    _aimbotSwingCooldown = false
    if _predBall then
        _predBall:Destroy()
        _predBall = nil
    end
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then
        applyVel(root, Vector3.zero)
        applyAngVel(root, Vector3.zero)
    end
    local hum2 = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum2 then
        hum2.AutoRotate = true
    end
    resumeAutoTPAfterAimbot()
    resumeAntiBatAfterAimbot()
    _autoTPWasEnabledForBat = false
    if autoBatSetVisual then
        autoBatSetVisual(false)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
        mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false)
    end
end
do
    -- Motor preditivo adaptado do callback AIMBOT efetivo do script de referência.
    local Z = {
        targetPlayer=nil, lastTargetPos=nil, targetVelocity=Vector3.zero,
        smoothedVelocity=Vector3.zero, velocityHistory={}, accelerationHistory={},
        aerialVelocityHistory={}, verticalVelocityHistory={}, previousDirection=nil,
        lastDirectionChangeTime=0, airborneTime=0, lastYVelocity=0,
        lastJumpTime=0, lastActivationTime=0, currentPing=0.1, realPingMs=0,
    }
    local ZCFG = {
        FOLLOW_SPEED=55, ACTIVATE_DISTANCE=13, MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE=1,
        PREDICTION_TIME=0.22, PREDICT_AHEAD=3, MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE=150,
        VELOCITY_SMOOTHING=0.2, MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY=80,
        SERVER_TICKRATE=1/60, MIN_PING_COMPENSATION=0.03,
        MAX_PING_COMPENSATION=0.25, ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT=0.3,
        DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME=0.12,
        QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER=1.5, GRAVITY=196.2,
        AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR=0.8, MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME=0.08,
    }
    local pingGeneration=0
    local function averageVector(history)
        if #history==0 then return Vector3.zero end
        local total=Vector3.zero
        for _,value in ipairs(history) do total=total+value end
        return total/#history
    end
    local function pushHistory(history,value,maximum)
        table.insert(history,value)
        if #history>maximum then table.remove(history,1) end
    end
    local function resetTarget()
        Z.targetPlayer=nil; Z.lastTargetPos=nil
        Z.targetVelocity=Vector3.zero; Z.smoothedVelocity=Vector3.zero
        Z.velocityHistory={}; Z.accelerationHistory={}; Z.aerialVelocityHistory={}; Z.verticalVelocityHistory={}
        Z.previousDirection=nil; Z.airborneTime=0; Z.lastYVelocity=0
    end
    local function rotateRoot(root,direction)
        if direction.Magnitude<0.01 then return end
        local axis=root.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(direction.Unit)
        local angle=math.asin(math.clamp(axis.Magnitude,-1,1))
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=axis.Magnitude>0.01 and axis.Unit*angle*80 or Vector3.zero
    end
    local function samplePing()
        local ok,value=pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
        end)
        if ok and type(value)=="number" then Z.realPingMs=math.floor(value) end
        Z.currentPing=math.clamp(Z.realPingMs/1000,ZCFG.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION,ZCFG.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)
    end
    local function startPingSampler()
        pingGeneration=pingGeneration+1
        local generation=pingGeneration
        task.spawn(function()
            while aimbotV2Enabled and pingGeneration==generation do
                pcall(samplePing)
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    end
    startAimbotV2 = function()
        if aimbotV2Conn then pcall(function() aimbotV2Conn:Disconnect() end); aimbotV2Conn=nil end
        aimbotV2Enabled=true
        if autoBatEnabled then
            stopBatAimbot()
            if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
            if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end
        end
        pauseAutoTPForAimbot(); pauseAntiBatForAimbot()
        resetTarget(); Z.lastActivationTime=0
        startPingSampler()
        local char=LP.Character
        local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate=false end
        aimbotV2Conn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not aimbotV2Enabled then return end
            local currentChar=LP.Character
            local root=currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local currentHumanoid=currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not root or not currentHumanoid or currentHumanoid.Health<=0 then return end
            currentHumanoid.AutoRotate=false
            local bat=currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or findBatForAimbot()
            if bat and bat.Parent~=currentChar then pcall(currentHumanoid.EquipTool,currentHumanoid,bat) end

            -- Respeita um marcador de alvo externo quando disponível; normalmente
            -- usa o jogador vivo mais próximo, como o botão AIMBOT da referência.
            local markerCFrame=nil
            if _G.__CrystalTPBatGetMarkerCFrame then
                local ok,result=pcall(_G.__CrystalTPBatGetMarkerCFrame)
                if ok then markerCFrame=result end
            end
            if markerCFrame then
                local markedDirection=markerCFrame.Position-root.Position
                if markedDirection.Magnitude>0.1 then rotateRoot(root,markedDirection) end
                if markedDirection.Magnitude>ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
                    local speed=math.clamp(getConfiguredAimbotSpeed(ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED),1,120)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=markedDirection.Unit*speed
                else
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                end
                return
            end

            local target,dist=getClosestPlayerAim()
            local targetChar=target and target.Character
            local targetRoot=targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHumanoid=targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not targetRoot or not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health<=0 then
                resetTarget()
                return
            end
            Z.targetPlayer=target
            local targetPos=targetRoot.Position
            local safeDt=math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0,1/240)
            if Z.lastTargetPos then
                local rawVelocity=(targetPos-Z.lastTargetPos)/safeDt
                local velocityDelta=rawVelocity-Z.targetVelocity
                if velocityDelta.Magnitude>ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE then
                    rawVelocity=Z.targetVelocity+velocityDelta.Unit*ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE
                end
                local horizontal=Vector3.new(rawVelocity.X,0,rawVelocity.Z)
                if horizontal.Magnitude>ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then
                    horizontal=horizontal.Unit*ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY
                    rawVelocity=Vector3.new(horizontal.X,rawVelocity.Y,horizontal.Z)
                end
                pushHistory(Z.accelerationHistory,(rawVelocity-Z.targetVelocity)/safeDt,4)
                pushHistory(Z.velocityHistory,rawVelocity,8)
                pushHistory(Z.verticalVelocityHistory,rawVelocity.Y,5)
                Z.targetVelocity=rawVelocity
                Z.smoothedVelocity=Z.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(rawVelocity,ZCFG.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)
            end
            Z.lastTargetPos=targetPos
            local airborne=targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial==Enum.Material.Air
            Z.airborneTime=airborne and (Z.airborneTime+safeDt) or 0
            if airborne and Z.airborneTime>=ZCFG.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then
                pushHistory(Z.aerialVelocityHistory,Z.targetVelocity,6)
            elseif not airborne then
                Z.aerialVelocityHistory={}
            end
            local predictionVelocity=Z.smoothedVelocity
            if airborne and #Z.aerialVelocityHistory>0 then
                local aerial=averageVector(Z.aerialVelocityHistory)
                predictionVelocity=Vector3.new(aerial.X,Z.targetVelocity.Y,aerial.Z)*ZCFG.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR
            end
            local quickTurn=false
            local horizontalVelocity=Vector3.new(Z.targetVelocity.X,0,Z.targetVelocity.Z)
            if horizontalVelocity.Magnitude>5 then
                local direction=horizontalVelocity.Unit
                if Z.previousDirection and Z.previousDirection:Dot(direction)<0.5 then
                    quickTurn=tick()-Z.lastDirectionChangeTime<ZCFG.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME
                    Z.lastDirectionChangeTime=tick()
                end
                Z.previousDirection=direction
            end
            local serverDelay=Z.currentPing+ZCFG.SERVER_TICKRATE
            if quickTurn then serverDelay=serverDelay*ZCFG.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER end
            local predicted=targetPos+predictionVelocity*serverDelay
            local acceleration=averageVector(Z.accelerationHistory)
            predicted=predicted+acceleration*ZCFG.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT*(serverDelay*serverDelay*0.5)
            local predictionTime=ZCFG.PREDICTION_TIME*1.1
            if airborne then
                predicted=predicted+predictionVelocity*predictionTime
                predicted=predicted+Vector3.new(0,-0.5*ZCFG.GRAVITY*predictionTime*predictionTime,0)
            else
                predicted=predicted+predictionVelocity*predictionTime
            end
            local flatPrediction=Vector3.new(predictionVelocity.X,0,predictionVelocity.Z)
            if flatPrediction.Magnitude>1 then predicted=predicted+flatPrediction.Unit*ZCFG.PREDICT_AHEAD end
            local toTarget=predicted-root.Position
            rotateRoot(root,toTarget)
            if (targetPos-root.Position).Magnitude<=ZCFG.ACTIVATE_DISTANCE and tick()-Z.lastActivationTime>=0.3 then
                if bat then pcall(bat.Activate,bat) end
                Z.lastActivationTime=tick()
            end
            if toTarget.Magnitude>ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
                local speed=math.clamp(getConfiguredAimbotSpeed(ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED),1,120)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=toTarget.Unit*speed
            else
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y*0.5,0)
            end
        end)
        if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(true) end
        if mobBtnRefs.batV2 then mobBtnRefs.batV2(true) end
    end
    stopAimbotV2 = function()
        if aimbotV2Conn then pcall(function() aimbotV2Conn:Disconnect() end); aimbotV2Conn=nil end
        aimbotV2Enabled=false; pingGeneration=pingGeneration+1
        resetTarget(); Z.lastActivationTime=0
        if _predBall then _predBall:Destroy(); _predBall=nil end
        local char=LP.Character
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end) end
        local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate=true end) end
        resumeAutoTPAfterAimbot(); resumeAntiBatAfterAimbot()
        if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(false) end
        if mobBtnRefs.batV2 then mobBtnRefs.batV2(false) end
    end
end
local ANTI_BAT_SPEED_THRESHOLD = 140
local function getPlayerHorizontalSpeed(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return 0 end
    local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or root.Velocity
    return Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude
end
local function getNearestAimTargetAndSpeed()
    local target = getClosestPlayerAim()
    return target, target and getPlayerHorizontalSpeed(target) or 0
end
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.12) do
        if autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatEnabled
            and _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == nil
            and (autoBatEnabled or aimbotV2Enabled)
        then
            local target, speed = getNearestAimTargetAndSpeed()
            if target then
                local wantsBypass = speed >= ANTI_BAT_SPEED_THRESHOLD
                if wantsBypass and not aimbotV2Enabled then
                    if autoBatEnabled then stopBatAimbot() end
                    startAimbotV2()
                elseif not wantsBypass and not autoBatEnabled then
                    if aimbotV2Enabled then stopAimbotV2() end
                    antiBatBypassEnabled = false
                    startBatAimbot()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
_G.S2AntiDesync = _G.S2AntiDesync or { conn = nil, hittingCooldown = false, h = nil, hrp = nil, diedConn = nil }
_G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
batTP = batTP or {}
do
local tpBatCollisionSaved=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local tpBatAntiDieConnections={}
local tpBatHumanoidSnapshot=nil
local function tpBatDisconnectAntiDie(restore)
    for _,conn in ipairs(tpBatAntiDieConnections) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    tpBatAntiDieConnections={}
    local snap=tpBatHumanoidSnapshot
    if restore and snap and snap.h and snap.h.Parent then
        pcall(function() snap.h.BreakJointsOnDeath=snap.breakJoints end)
        for state,value in pairs(snap.states or {}) do pcall(function() snap.h:SetStateEnabled(state,value) end) end
    end
    tpBatHumanoidSnapshot=nil
end
local function tpBatRestoreCollisions()
    for part,value in pairs(tpBatCollisionSaved) do
        if part and part.Parent then pcall(function() part.CanCollide=value end) end
        tpBatCollisionSaved[part]=nil
    end
end
_G.S2AntiDesync = _G.S2AntiDesync or {conn=nil,hittingCooldown=false,h=nil,hrp=nil,diedConn=nil}
_G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn=false
function _G.S2AntiDesyncGetBat()
    local char=LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local function findBat(container)
        if not container then return nil end
        for _,item in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("Tool") then
                local name=item.Name:lower()
                if name:find("bat",1,true) or name:find("slap",1,true) then return item end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local tool=findBat(char)
    if tool then return tool end
    local backpack=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    tool=findBat(backpack)
    if tool then
        local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
    end
    return tool
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncTrySwing()
    local state=_G.S2AntiDesync
    if not state or state.hittingCooldown then return end
    state.hittingCooldown=true
    pcall(function()
        local bat=_G.S2AntiDesyncGetBat()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local remote=bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if remote then remote:FireServer() end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(0.08,function() if _G.S2AntiDesync then _G.S2AntiDesync.hittingCooldown=false end end)
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
    local root=_G.S2AntiDesync and _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp
    if not root then return nil,math.huge end
    local best,bestDistance=nil,math.huge
    for _,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if other~=LP and other.Character then
            local otherRoot=other.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local otherHum=other.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if otherRoot and otherHum and otherHum.Health>0 then
                local distance=(root.Position-otherRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance<bestDistance then best,bestDistance=other,distance end
            end
        end
    end
    return best,bestDistance
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
    task.wait(0.1)
    local state=_G.S2AntiDesync
    if not state then return end
    state.h=char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid",5) or nil
    state.hrp=char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5) or nil
    batTP.h=state.h; batTP.hrp=state.hrp
    if state.diedConn then pcall(function() state.diedConn:Disconnect() end); state.diedConn=nil end
    tpBatDisconnectAntiDie(true)
    if state.h and _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
        local hum=state.h
        local snapshot={h=hum,breakJoints=hum.BreakJointsOnDeath,states={}}
        local stateTypes={Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead}
        pcall(function() table.insert(stateTypes,Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dying) end)
        for _,humState in ipairs(stateTypes) do
            pcall(function() snapshot.states[humState]=hum:GetStateEnabled(humState); hum:SetStateEnabled(humState,false) end)
        end
        pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath=false end)
        tpBatHumanoidSnapshot=snapshot
        table.insert(tpBatAntiDieConnections,hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
            if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn and hum.Parent and hum.Health<=0 then
                pcall(function() hum.Health=hum.MaxHealth; hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
        end))
        state.diedConn=hum.Died:Connect(function()
            _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
            if batTP.setVisual then batTP.setVisual(false) end
            if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false) end
        end)
    end
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) pcall(function() _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(char) end) end)
if LP.Character then task.spawn(function() pcall(function() _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character) end) end) end
function _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
    if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.S2AntiDesync and _G.S2AntiDesync.conn then return end
    pauseAutoTPForAimbot(); pauseAntiBatForAimbot()
    _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn=true; batTP.enabled=true
    if _G.S2AntiDesync.conn then pcall(function() _G.S2AntiDesync.conn:Disconnect() end); _G.S2AntiDesync.conn=nil end
    if LP.Character then pcall(function() _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character) end) end
    _G.S2AntiDesync.conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then return end
        local character=LP.Character
        local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 or not root then return end
        _G.S2AntiDesync.h=humanoid; _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp=root; batTP.h=humanoid; batTP.hrp=root
        local animator=humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if animator then pcall(function() for _,track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do track:Stop() end end) end
        local tool=_G.S2AntiDesyncGetBat()
        if tool and tool.Parent~=character then pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(tool) end) end
        local target,distance=_G.S2AntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
        local targetRoot=target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not targetRoot or distance>100 then return end
        pcall(function() if root.SetNetworkOwner then root:SetNetworkOwner(nil) end end)
        if not _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn or root.Parent~=character or not targetRoot.Parent then return end
        pcall(function()
            root.CFrame=CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position+Vector3.new(0,0.9,0))
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        end)
        pcall(function() if root.SetNetworkOwner then root:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end end)
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then pcall(function() camera.CFrame=CFrame.new(camera.CFrame.Position,targetRoot.Position) end) end
        _G.S2AntiDesyncTrySwing()
        pcall(function()
            for _,part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if tpBatCollisionSaved[part]==nil then tpBatCollisionSaved[part]=part.CanCollide end
                    part.CanCollide=false
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
    batTP.conn=_G.S2AntiDesync.conn
    if batTP.setVisual then batTP.setVisual(true) end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(true) end
    saveConfig()
end
function _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
    _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn=false; batTP.enabled=false
    local state=_G.S2AntiDesync
    if state and state.conn then pcall(function() state.conn:Disconnect() end); state.conn=nil end
    batTP.conn=nil
    if state then
        state.hittingCooldown=false
        if state.diedConn then pcall(function() state.diedConn:Disconnect() end); state.diedConn=nil end
    end
    tpBatDisconnectAntiDie(true); tpBatRestoreCollisions()
    local character=LP.Character; local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if root then pcall(function() if root.SetNetworkOwner then root:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end end) end
    if state then state.hrp=nil; state.h=nil end
    batTP.hrp=nil; batTP.h=nil
    if batTP.setVisual then batTP.setVisual(false) end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false) end
    resumeAutoTPAfterAimbot(); resumeAntiBatAfterAimbot(); saveConfig()
end
function _G.S2ToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
    if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot() else _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot() end
end
end
batTP = batTP or {}
batTP.start = function()
    _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
batTP.stop = function()
    _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
batTP.toggle = function()
    _G.S2ToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
batTP.enabled = false
batTP.setVisual = nil
startAutoPlay = function()
    autoPlayEnabled = true
    stopAutoLeft()
    stopAutoRight()
    autoLeftEnabled = false
    autoRightEnabled = false
    local side = autoPlaySide or "left"
    if side == "left" then
        autoLeftEnabled = true
        if type(startAutoLeft) == "function" then
            startAutoLeft()
        end
    else
        autoRightEnabled = true
        if type(startAutoRight) == "function" then
            startAutoRight()
        end
    end
    if autoPlaySetVisual then
        autoPlaySetVisual(true)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
        mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(true)
    end
end
stopAutoPlay = function()
    autoPlayEnabled = false
    autoLeftEnabled = false
    autoRightEnabled = false
    if type(stopAutoLeft) == "function" then
        stopAutoLeft()
    end
    if type(stopAutoRight) == "function" then
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    if autoPlaySetVisual then
        autoPlaySetVisual(false)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
        mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false)
    end
end
queueAutoBatStart = function()
    if aimbotV2Enabled then
        stopAimbotV2()
    end
    if antiKickEnabled and brainrotDetected then
        return
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoLeft()
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    startBatAimbot()
end

    carrySpeedActive = false
    laggerModeEnabled = false
    laggerCarryActive = false

    if mobBtnRefs.normalSpeed then

    if mobBtnRefs.normalCarry then

        mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(false)
    end
    if setCarryModeVisual then
        setCarryModeVisual(false)
    end
    if setLaggerModeVisual then
        setLaggerModeVisual(false)
    end
    saveConfig()
end

end

local function forceNoSplatterReset()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    pcall(function()
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then obj.Enabled = true end
            if obj:IsA("Constraint") then obj.Enabled = true end
        end
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum end
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.Sit = false
    end)
end
startAntiRagdoll = function()
    if antiRagdollConn then return end
    antiRagdollConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        local state = hum:GetState()
        local ragdolled = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        if antiRagdollMode == "No Splatter" then
            if ragdolled then
                local now = tick()
                if now - (antiRagdollNoSplatterCooldown or 0) > 0.15 then
                    antiRagdollNoSplatterCooldown = now
                    forceNoSplatterReset()
                end
            end
            return
        end
        if not root then return end
        local endTime = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
        if endTime and (endTime - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0 then ragdolled = true end
        if ragdolled then
            pcall(function() LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) end)
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
                    d:Destroy()
                end
            end
            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Enabled == false then obj.Enabled = true end
            end
            if hum.Health > 0 then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
            if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum end
            root.Anchored = false
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end
stopAntiRagdoll = function()
    if antiRagdollConn then
        antiRagdollConn:Disconnect()
        antiRagdollConn = nil
    end
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    startAntiRagdoll()
end)
startAntiRagdoll()
-- Separate V2 ragdoll recovery; mutually exclusive with the classic Splatter mode.
do
    local conn=nil
    local function stop()
        if conn then pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end); conn=nil end
    end
    local function recover()
        local char=LP.Character
        local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health<=0 then return end
        local st=hum:GetState()
        local rag=(st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st==Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown)
        local endTime=LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
        if endTime and (endTime-workspace:GetServerTimeNow())>0 then rag=true end
        if not rag then return end
        pcall(function() LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime",workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) end)
        for _,obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or (obj:IsA("Attachment") and obj.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            if hum.Health>0 then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
            root.Anchored=false; root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero; root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
            root.CFrame=CFrame.new(root.Position)*CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(root.Orientation.Y),0)
            local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera; if cam and cam.CameraSubject~=hum then cam.CameraSubject=hum end
        end)
    end
    local function start()
        if conn then return end
        conn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if _G._S2AntiRagdollV2Enabled then recover() else stop() end
        end)
    end
    _G._S2SetAntiRagdollV2=function(on)
        _G._S2AntiRagdollV2Enabled=on==true
        if on then
            if antiRagdollEnabled then antiRagdollEnabled=false; stopAntiRagdoll() end
            if setAntiRagSplatterVisual then setAntiRagSplatterVisual(false) end
            if setAntiRagNoSplatterVisual then setAntiRagNoSplatterVisual(false) end
            start()
        else stop() end
    end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if _G._S2AntiRagdollV2Enabled then task.delay(0.25,start) end
    end)
end

-- 000 DUELS ANTI DIE (física portada do EasyDuels)
antiDieEnabled = false
local antiDieConns = {}
local antiDieCharConn = nil
local antiDieHeart = nil
local function antiDieDisconnect()
    for _, c in ipairs(antiDieConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    antiDieConns = {}
    if antiDieHeart then
        pcall(function() antiDieHeart:Disconnect() end)
        antiDieHeart = nil
    end
end
local function antiDieProtect(char)
    if not antiDieEnabled or not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not hum then return end
    pcall(function()
        hum.MaxHealth = math.huge
        hum.Health = math.huge
        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
    end)
    table.insert(antiDieConns, hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
        if not antiDieEnabled then return end
        if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
            pcall(function()
                hum.Health = math.huge
                hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            end)
        end
    end))
    table.insert(antiDieConns, hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
        if not antiDieEnabled then return end
        if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
            pcall(function() hum.Health = math.huge end)
        end
    end))
    antiDieHeart = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiDieEnabled then return end
        if hum.Parent and hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
            pcall(function() hum.Health = math.huge end)
        end
    end)
end
enableAntiDie = function()
    antiDieEnabled = true
    antiDieProtect(LP.Character)
    if antiDieCharConn then
        pcall(function() antiDieCharConn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    antiDieCharConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if not antiDieEnabled then return end
        task.wait(0.1)
        antiDieDisconnect()
        antiDieProtect(char)
    end)
end
disableAntiDie = function()
    antiDieEnabled = false
    antiDieDisconnect()
    if antiDieCharConn then
        pcall(function() antiDieCharConn:Disconnect() end)
        antiDieCharConn = nil
    end
end
setAntiDieVisual = nil

-- Anti Medusa Counter (rotina integrada do script de referência, sem UI própria).
do
    local antiMedusaCounterConn = nil
    local antiMedusaResetConn = nil
    local medusaToolDetected = false
    local antiMedusaGeneration = 0
    local antiMedusaResetGeneration = 0
    local deathCoords = CFrame.new(1000003.56, 999999.69, 8.17)

    local function disconnectReset()
        antiMedusaResetGeneration = antiMedusaResetGeneration + 1
        if antiMedusaResetConn then
            pcall(function() antiMedusaResetConn:Disconnect() end)
            antiMedusaResetConn = nil
        end
    end

    local function findCarpet()
        local backpack = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if not backpack then return nil end
        for _, tool in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") and string.find(string.lower(tool.Name), "carpet", 1, true) then
                return tool
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function instaReset(token)
        local character = LP.Character
        if not character then return end
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not humanoid then return end

        local carpet = findCarpet()
        if carpet then
            pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(carpet) end)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if token ~= antiMedusaGeneration or not antiMedusaCounterEnabled then return end

        pcall(function() root.CFrame = deathCoords end)
        disconnectReset()
        local resetToken = antiMedusaResetGeneration
        antiMedusaResetConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if resetToken ~= antiMedusaResetGeneration or not antiMedusaCounterEnabled
                or not character or not character.Parent then
                disconnectReset()
                return
            end
            local currentRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not currentRoot then
                disconnectReset()
                return
            end
            pcall(function() currentRoot.CFrame = deathCoords end)
            local currentHumanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if currentHumanoid and currentHumanoid.Health <= 0 then disconnectReset() end
        end)
    end

    startAntiMedusaCounter = function()
        if antiMedusaCounterConn then return end
        antiMedusaCounterEnabled = true
        antiMedusaCounterConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not antiMedusaCounterEnabled then return end
            local character = LP.Character
            if not character then
                if medusaToolDetected then
                    medusaToolDetected = false
                    antiMedusaGeneration = antiMedusaGeneration + 1
                end
                return
            end

            local hasMedusa = false
            for _, tool in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    local name = string.lower(tool.Name)
                    if string.find(name, "medusa", 1, true) or string.find(name, "head", 1, true) then
                        hasMedusa = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if hasMedusa and not medusaToolDetected then
                medusaToolDetected = true
                antiMedusaGeneration = antiMedusaGeneration + 1
                local token = antiMedusaGeneration
                task.delay(1, function()
                    if antiMedusaCounterEnabled and medusaToolDetected and token == antiMedusaGeneration then
                        instaReset(token)
                        medusaToolDetected = false
                    end
                end)
            elseif not hasMedusa and medusaToolDetected then
                medusaToolDetected = false
                antiMedusaGeneration = antiMedusaGeneration + 1
            end
        end)
    end

    stopAntiMedusaCounter = function()
        antiMedusaCounterEnabled = false
        medusaToolDetected = false
        antiMedusaGeneration = antiMedusaGeneration + 1
        if antiMedusaCounterConn then
            pcall(function() antiMedusaCounterConn:Disconnect() end)
            antiMedusaCounterConn = nil
        end
        disconnectReset()
    end
end

-- RESTART ON START MATCH: sem polling; acompanha mudanças de texto/visibilidade no PlayerGui.
do
    local root = nil
    local rootAddedConn, rootRemovingConn, rootAncestryConn, playerGuiAddedConn = nil, nil, nil, nil
    local tracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local matchCounts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local ancestorWatchers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local totalMatches = 0
    local alreadyTriggered = false
    local forceRespawn

    local latinTerms = {}
    local latinTermString = [[ACCEPTED|ACCEPT|APPROVED|APPROVE|ACEITADO|ACEITADA|ACEITO|ACEITA|APROVADO|APROVADA|ACEPTADO|ACEPTADA|ACEPTADOS|ACEPTADAS|APROBADO|APROBADA|APPROUVE|ACCETTATO|ACCETTATA|APPROVATO|APPROVATA|AKZEPTIERT|ANGENOMMEN|GENEHMIGT|BESTATIGT|GEACCEPTEERD|GOEDGEKEURD|GODKJENT|AKSEPTERT|GODKAND|ACCEPTERAD|GODKENDT|ACCEPTERET|HYVAKSYTTY|SAMTHYKKT|ACCEPTAT|APROVAT|ACCEPTAT|APROVAT|ACEPTADO|ACEPTADA|ACEPTAT|APROBAT|ZAAKCEPTOWANO|ZAAKCEPTOWANY|PRIJATO|SCHVALENO|PRIJATE|SCHVALENE|ELFOGADVA|JOVAHAGYVA|PRIHVACENO|ODOBRENO|PRIHVACENO|ODOBRENO|PRIETO|ODOBRENO|PRIHVATENO|PRANUAR|APROVUAR|SPREJETO|ODOBRENO|VASTU VOETUD|HEAKS KIIDETUD|PIENEMTS|APSTIPRINATS|PRIIMTA|PATVIRTINTA|QABUL QILINDI|QABUL QILINDI|QABUL|DITERIMA|DISETUJUI|DILULUSKAN|TINANGGAP|NAAPRUBAHAN|IMEKUBALIWA|IMEIDHINISHWA|AANVAAR|GOEDGEKEUR|GLACHTA|DERBYNIWYD|ONARTUTA|ACEPTADO|ACEPTADA|ACEPTAT|ACEPTADA|ACEPTAT|APPROVED|CONFIRMED|CONFIRMADO|CONFIRMADA|VALIDADO|VALIDADA|VALIDEE|VALIDES|VALIDATED|GRANTED|PERMITTED|ALLOWED|GRANTED|PERMITTED|ALLOWED]]
    for term in latinTermString:gmatch("[^|]+") do latinTerms[term] = true end

    -- Equivalentes em alfabetos sem conversão de caixa confiável pela string library do Luau.
    local otherTerms = {
        "ПРИНЯТО", "принято", "ПРИНЯТ", "принят", "ОДОБРЕНО", "одобрено", "ОДОБРЕН", "одобрен",
        "ПРИЙНЯТО", "прийнято", "СХВАЛЕНО", "схвалено", "ПРИЕТО", "прието", "ПРИФАТЕНО", "прифатено",
        "ACCEPTÉ", "ACCEPTÉE", "ACCETTATO", "ACCETTATA", "GODKÄND", "GODKÄNT", "HYVÄKSYTTY",
        "ACCEPTERET", "ACCEPTERAD", "SAMÞYKKT", "PŘIJATO", "SCHVÁLENO", "PŘIJATÉ", "SCHVÁLENÉ",
        "JÓVÁHAGYVA", "PRIHVAĆENO", "ODOBRENO", "PRIHVAĆENO", "ZAAKCEPTOWANO", "ZAAKCEPTOWANY",
        "ΕΓΚΡΙΘΗΚΕ", "εγκρίθηκε", "ΑΠΟΔΕΚΤΟ", "αποδεκτο", "ΚΑΤΑΧΩΡΗΘΗΚΕ", "καταχωρηθηκε",
        "قبول", "مقبول", "مقبولة", "تم القبول", "تم قبول", "موافق عليه", "تمت الموافقة",
        "התקבל", "התקבלה", "אושר", "אושרה", "پذیرفته", "پذیرفته شد", "تایید شد", "تأیید شد",
        "स्वीकृत", "स्वीकार किया", "स्वीकार", "गृहीत", "অনুমোদিত", "গৃহীত", "منظور", "منظور شد",
        "已接受", "已批准", "接受", "批准", "承認済み", "承認", "受理", "受け入れ", "승인", "수락",
        "ยอมรับ", "อนุมัติ", "đã chấp nhận", "được chấp nhận", "đã duyệt", "được duyệt",
        "đã chấp nhận", "diterima", "disetujui", "diterima", "diluluskan", "tinanggap", "naaprubahan",
        "imekubaliwa", "imeidhinishwa", "kwamukelwe", "AANVAAR", "รับรอง", "ອະນຸມັດ", "បានទទួលយក",
        "အတည်ပြု", "ተቀባይነት", "la aqbalay", "an karɓa", "ti gba", "menerima", "qəbul edildi", "qəbul edildi",
        "qabul qilindi", "მიღებულია", "ընդունված", "ЗӨВШӨӨРӨГДСӨН", "зөвшөөрөгдсөн", "ҚАБЫЛДАНДЫ", "қабылданды",
        "accepted", "approved", "accepted", "approved",
    }

    local foldMap = {
        ["Á"]="A",["À"]="A",["Â"]="A",["Ã"]="A",["Ä"]="A",["Å"]="A",["Æ"]="AE",["Ą"]="A",
        ["Ç"]="C",["Ć"]="C",["Č"]="C",["Ð"]="D",["Ď"]="D",["É"]="E",["È"]="E",["Ê"]="E",["Ë"]="E",["Ę"]="E",["Ě"]="E",
        ["Í"]="I",["Ì"]="I",["Î"]="I",["Ï"]="I",["İ"]="I",["Ł"]="L",["Ń"]="N",["Ñ"]="N",["Ó"]="O",["Ò"]="O",["Ô"]="O",["Õ"]="O",["Ö"]="O",["Ø"]="O",["Ő"]="O",
        ["Ś"]="S",["Š"]="S",["Ş"]="S",["ß"]="SS",["Ť"]="T",["Ţ"]="T",["Ú"]="U",["Ù"]="U",["Û"]="U",["Ü"]="U",["Ů"]="U",["Ű"]="U",
        ["Ý"]="Y",["Ÿ"]="Y",["Ž"]="Z",["Ź"]="Z",["Ż"]="Z",
    }
    local lowercaseFoldMap = {
        ["á"]="A",["à"]="A",["â"]="A",["ã"]="A",["ä"]="A",["å"]="A",["æ"]="AE",["ą"]="A",
        ["ç"]="C",["ć"]="C",["č"]="C",["ď"]="D",["đ"]="D",["ð"]="D",["é"]="E",["è"]="E",["ê"]="E",["ë"]="E",["ę"]="E",["ě"]="E",
        ["í"]="I",["ì"]="I",["î"]="I",["ï"]="I",["ı"]="I",["ł"]="L",["ń"]="N",["ñ"]="N",["ó"]="O",["ò"]="O",["ô"]="O",["õ"]="O",["ö"]="O",["ø"]="O",["ő"]="O",
        ["ś"]="S",["š"]="S",["ş"]="S",["ß"]="SS",["ť"]="T",["ţ"]="T",["ú"]="U",["ù"]="U",["û"]="U",["ü"]="U",["ů"]="U",["ű"]="U",
        ["ý"]="Y",["ÿ"]="Y",["ž"]="Z",["ź"]="Z",["ż"]="Z",["œ"]="OE",["þ"]="TH",
    }
    local function foldLatin(text)
        local value = string.upper(tostring(text or ""))
        for from, to in pairs(foldMap) do value = value:gsub(from, to) end
        for from, to in pairs(lowercaseFoldMap) do value = value:gsub(from, to) end
        return value
    end

    local function hasAcceptedStatus(text)
        local raw = tostring(text or "")
        local normalized = foldLatin(raw):gsub("[^A-Z0-9]+", " ")
        for word in normalized:gmatch("%S+") do
            if latinTerms[word] then return true end
        end
        local folded = foldLatin(raw)
        for _, term in ipairs(otherTerms) do
            local foldedTerm = foldLatin(term)
            if string.find(folded, foldedTerm, 1, true) or string.find(raw, term, 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
