    end

    local function isTextObject(obj)
        return obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")
    end

    local function isVisible(obj)
        if not obj.Parent or not obj.Visible then return false end
        if obj.TextTransparency >= 1 and obj.TextStrokeTransparency >= 1 then return false end
        local parent = obj.Parent
        while parent do
            if parent:IsA("GuiObject") and not parent.Visible then return false end
            if parent:IsA("LayerCollector") and not parent.Enabled then return false end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
        return obj.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and obj.AbsoluteSize.Y > 0
    end

    local function updateObject(obj)
        if not restartOnStartMatchEnabled or not tracked[obj] then return end
        local nextCount = (isVisible(obj) and hasAcceptedStatus(obj.Text)) and 1 or 0
        local oldCount = matchCounts[obj] or 0
        if nextCount == oldCount then return end
        matchCounts[obj] = nextCount
        totalMatches = math.max(0, totalMatches + nextCount - oldCount)
        if totalMatches >= 2 then
            if not alreadyTriggered then
                alreadyTriggered = true
                forceRespawn()
            end
        elseif totalMatches < 2 then
            alreadyTriggered = false
        end
    end

    local function refreshAll()
        for obj in pairs(tracked) do updateObject(obj) end
    end

    local function watchAncestors(obj)
        local parent = obj.Parent
        while parent and parent ~= root do
            local isWatchable = (parent:IsA("GuiObject") or parent:IsA("LayerCollector"))
            if isWatchable and not ancestorWatchers[parent] then
                local connections = {}
                ancestorWatchers[parent] = connections
                if parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    table.insert(connections, parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(refreshAll))
                end
                if parent:IsA("LayerCollector") then
                    table.insert(connections, parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(refreshAll))
                end
            end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
    end

    local function untrackText(obj)
        local connections = tracked[obj]
        if not connections then return end
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
        totalMatches = math.max(0, totalMatches - (matchCounts[obj] or 0))
        matchCounts[obj] = nil
        tracked[obj] = nil
        if totalMatches < 2 then alreadyTriggered = false end
    end

    local function trackText(obj)
        if not root or not obj or not obj:IsDescendantOf(root) or not isTextObject(obj) or tracked[obj] then return end
        local connections = {}
        tracked[obj] = connections
        local function watchProperty(property)
            local ok, connection = pcall(function()
                return obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property):Connect(function()
                    watchAncestors(obj)
                    updateObject(obj)
                end)
            end)
            if ok and connection then table.insert(connections, connection) end
        end
        watchProperty("Text")
        watchProperty("Visible")
        watchProperty("TextTransparency")
        watchProperty("TextStrokeTransparency")
        table.insert(connections, obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
            if root and obj:IsDescendantOf(root) then
                watchAncestors(obj)
                updateObject(obj)
            else
                untrackText(obj)
            end
        end))
        watchAncestors(obj)
        updateObject(obj)
    end

    local function clearRoot()
        if rootAddedConn then pcall(function() rootAddedConn:Disconnect() end); rootAddedConn = nil end
        if rootRemovingConn then pcall(function() rootRemovingConn:Disconnect() end); rootRemovingConn = nil end
        if rootAncestryConn then pcall(function() rootAncestryConn:Disconnect() end); rootAncestryConn = nil end
        for obj in pairs(tracked) do untrackText(obj) end
        for ancestor, connections in pairs(ancestorWatchers) do
            for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
            ancestorWatchers[ancestor] = nil
        end
        totalMatches = 0
        root = nil
    end

    local function attachRoot(newRoot)
        if not restartOnStartMatchEnabled or not newRoot or root == newRoot then return end
        clearRoot()
        root = newRoot
        rootAddedConn = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            trackText(obj)
            if obj:IsA("GuiObject") then
                for _, descendant in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do trackText(descendant) end
            end
        end)
        rootRemovingConn = root.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
            if tracked[obj] then
                untrackText(obj)
            else
                for trackedObj in pairs(tracked) do
                    if trackedObj == obj or trackedObj:IsDescendantOf(obj) then untrackText(trackedObj) end
                end
            end
        end)
        rootAncestryConn = root.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if parent == nil then clearRoot() end
        end)
        -- Uma única leitura inicial; depois, somente eventos da interface.
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do trackText(obj) end
    end

    forceRespawn = function()
        local character = LP.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local restoreAntiDie = antiDieEnabled == true
        if restoreAntiDie then
            restartOnStartMatchRestoreAntiDie = true
            if restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn then
                pcall(function() restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn:Disconnect() end)
                restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn = nil
            end
            disableAntiDie()
            if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(false) end
            restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                local connection = restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn
                restartOnStartMatchRestoreConn = nil
                if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
                task.delay(0.15, function()
                    if restartOnStartMatchRestoreAntiDie then
                        restartOnStartMatchRestoreAntiDie = false
                        enableAntiDie()
                        if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(true) end
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
            humanoid.Health = 0
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        end)
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            if character == LP.Character and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                pcall(function() character:BreakJoints() end)
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                    humanoid.Health = 0
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    startRestartOnStartMatch = function()
        if root or playerGuiAddedConn then
            restartOnStartMatchEnabled = true
            return
        end
        restartOnStartMatchEnabled = true
        alreadyTriggered = false
        local function attachExistingPlayerGui()
            local playerGui = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if playerGui then attachRoot(playerGui) end
        end
        playerGuiAddedConn = LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA("PlayerGui") or child.Name == "PlayerGui" then attachRoot(child) end
        end)
        attachExistingPlayerGui()
    end

    stopRestartOnStartMatch = function()
        restartOnStartMatchEnabled = false
        alreadyTriggered = false
        if playerGuiAddedConn then pcall(function() playerGuiAddedConn:Disconnect() end); playerGuiAddedConn = nil end
        clearRoot()
    end
end

-- Anti Void/Filing: resgate local por último checkpoint seguro.
-- Os limites seguem o mapa definido no script KYZ de referência.
ANTI_VOID_FILING_BOUNDS = {
    MinX = -535.7, MaxX = -422.0,
    MinY = -7.9, MaxY = 115.0,
    MinZ = -72.0, MaxZ = 193.3,
}
function antiVoidFilingIsWithinBounds(position)
    local bounds = ANTI_VOID_FILING_BOUNDS
    return typeof(position) == "Vector3"
        and position.X >= bounds.MinX and position.X <= bounds.MaxX
        and position.Y >= bounds.MinY and position.Y <= bounds.MaxY
        and position.Z >= bounds.MinZ and position.Z <= bounds.MaxZ
end
function startAntiVoidFiling()
    antiVoidFilingEnabled = true
    if antiVoidFilingConn then return end
    antiVoidFilingLastSafePosition = nil
    antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin = nil
    antiVoidFilingLastUpdate = 0
    if antiVoidFilingCharacterConn then
        pcall(function() antiVoidFilingCharacterConn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    antiVoidFilingCharacterConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        antiVoidFilingLastSafePosition = nil
        antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin = nil
        antiVoidFilingLastUpdate = 0
    end)
    antiVoidFilingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiVoidFilingEnabled then return end
        local character = LP.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not root.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local position = root.Position
        local now = os.clock()
        if not antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin and antiVoidFilingIsWithinBounds(position) then
            antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin = position
        end
        if now - antiVoidFilingLastUpdate >= 0.1 then
            antiVoidFilingLastUpdate = now
            if humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and antiVoidFilingIsWithinBounds(position) then
                antiVoidFilingLastSafePosition = position
            end
        end
        if not antiVoidFilingIsWithinBounds(position) then
            local destination = antiVoidFilingLastSafePosition or antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin
            if destination and antiVoidFilingIsWithinBounds(destination) then
                pcall(function()
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end
function stopAntiVoidFiling()
    antiVoidFilingEnabled = false
    if antiVoidFilingConn then
        pcall(function() antiVoidFilingConn:Disconnect() end)
        antiVoidFilingConn = nil
    end
    if antiVoidFilingCharacterConn then
        pcall(function() antiVoidFilingCharacterConn:Disconnect() end)
        antiVoidFilingCharacterConn = nil
    end
    antiVoidFilingLastSafePosition = nil
    antiVoidFilingSpawnOrigin = nil
    antiVoidFilingLastUpdate = 0
end

-- ANTI DROP: lógica de spoof de velocidade do script ANTIDROP, sem Billboard/GUI sobre a cabeça.
local function startAntiDrop()
    if antiDropActive then return end
    local mt = getrawmetatable and getrawmetatable(game)
    if not mt or not setreadonly or not newcclosure or not checkcaller then return end
    antiDropMeta = mt
    antiDropOldIndex = mt.__index
    antiDropOldNewIndex = mt.__newindex
    setreadonly(mt, false)
    mt.__index = newcclosure(function(self, key)
        if not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity")
            and typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("BasePart")
            and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and LP.Character and self:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character) then
            return antiDropSpoofedVelocity
        end
        return antiDropOldIndex(self, key)
    end)
    mt.__newindex = newcclosure(function(self, key, value)
        if not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity")
            and typeof(self) == "Instance" and self:IsA("BasePart")
            and self.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and LP.Character and self:IsDescendantOf(LP.Character) then
            antiDropSpoofedVelocity = value
            return
        end
        return antiDropOldNewIndex(self, key, value)
    end)
    setreadonly(mt, true)
    antiDropActive = true
    if antiDropCharacterConn then antiDropCharacterConn:Disconnect() end
    antiDropCharacterConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if antiDropEnabled then
            antiDropSpoofedVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAntiDrop()
    if antiDropCharacterConn then
        antiDropCharacterConn:Disconnect()
        antiDropCharacterConn = nil
    end
    if antiDropActive and antiDropMeta and antiDropOldIndex then
        setreadonly(antiDropMeta, false)
        antiDropMeta.__index = antiDropOldIndex
        antiDropMeta.__newindex = antiDropOldNewIndex
        setreadonly(antiDropMeta, true)
    end
    antiDropMeta = nil
    antiDropOldIndex = nil
    antiDropOldNewIndex = nil
    antiDropActive = false
    antiDropSpoofedVelocity = Vector3.zero
end

-- ANTI BAT: mesma física do script ANTIDROP, sem qualquer elemento visual adicional.
local function startAntiBat()
    if antiBatConnection then return end
    antiBatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiBatEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local origXZ = Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X, 0, root.Velocity.Z)
        root.Velocity = Vector3.new(ANTI_BAT_FORCE_X, root.Velocity.Y, ANTI_BAT_FORCE_Z)
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        if root.Parent then
            root.Velocity = Vector3.new(origXZ.X, root.Velocity.Y, origXZ.Z)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAntiBat()
    if antiBatConnection then
        antiBatConnection:Disconnect()
        antiBatConnection = nil
    end
end

pauseAntiBatForAimbot = function()
    if not antiBatEnabled or _antiBatPausedByAimbot then
        return
    end
    _antiBatWasEnabledForAimbot = true
    _antiBatPausedByAimbot = true
    antiBatEnabled = false
    stopAntiBat()
    if setAntiBatVisual then
        setAntiBatVisual(false)
    end
end

resumeAntiBatAfterAimbot = function()
    if not _antiBatPausedByAimbot then
        return
    end
    -- Não retome o Anti Bat no meio de uma troca entre modos de AIMBOT.
    if autoBatEnabled or aimbotV2Enabled or _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn == true then
        return
    end
    local shouldResume = _antiBatWasEnabledForAimbot == true
    _antiBatWasEnabledForAimbot = false
    _antiBatPausedByAimbot = false
    if shouldResume then
        antiBatEnabled = true
        startAntiBat()
        if setAntiBatVisual then
            setAntiBatVisual(true)
        end
    end
end

-- ANTI TP BAT: física do Stick Anti Tp Bat, sem criar uma GUI própria.
local function antiTPBatGetCharacter()
    local char = LP.Character
    return char, char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function antiTPBatSetReplicationRoot(root, value)
    if not root then return false end
    local setter = (typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" and sethiddenproperty)
        or (syn and syn.sethiddenproperty)
    if setter then
        local ok = pcall(setter, root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", value)
        if ok then return true end
    end
    return pcall(function() root.PhysicsRepRootPart = value end)
end

local function antiTPBatConfigurePhysics()
    pcall(function()
        local setter = (typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" and sethiddenproperty)
            or (syn and syn.sethiddenproperty)
        if setter then
            setter(LP, "MaximumSimulationRadius", math.huge)
            setter(LP, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        settings().Network.InterpolationThrottling = Enum.InterpolationThrottlingMode.Disabled
    end)
    pcall(function()
        settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = Enum.EnviromentalPhysicsThrottle.Disabled
        settings().Physics.AllowSleep = false
    end)
end

local function stopAntiTPBat()
    if antiTPBatLoopConnection then
        antiTPBatLoopConnection:Disconnect()
        antiTPBatLoopConnection = nil
    end
    if antiTPBatSteppedConnection then
        antiTPBatSteppedConnection:Disconnect()
        antiTPBatSteppedConnection = nil
    end
    if antiTPBatCharacterConnection then
        antiTPBatCharacterConnection:Disconnect()
        antiTPBatCharacterConnection = nil
    end
    local char, root = antiTPBatGetCharacter()
    pcall(function()
        if char and root then
            antiTPBatSetReplicationRoot(root, root)
        end
    end)
    if antiTPBatPart then
        pcall(function() antiTPBatPart:Destroy() end)
        antiTPBatPart = nil
    end
    antiTPBatOriginalCFrame = nil
end

local function startAntiTPBat()
    if antiTPBatEnabled and antiTPBatSteppedConnection and antiTPBatLoopConnection then return end
    local char, root = antiTPBatGetCharacter()
    if not char or not root then return end
    antiTPBatConfigurePhysics()
    antiTPBatEnabled = true
    antiTPBatOriginalCFrame = root.CFrame

    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name = "S2AntiTPBatOriginalDesyncRoot"
    part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.Transparency = 1
    part.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, -2500, root.Position.Z)
    part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0)
    part.Parent = workspace
    antiTPBatPart = part
    antiTPBatSetReplicationRoot(root, part)

    -- Mantém o fake root no ponto de desync e garante que a propriedade não seja revertida.
    antiTPBatSteppedConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not antiTPBatEnabled or not part.Parent then return end
        local currentChar, currentRoot = antiTPBatGetCharacter()
        if not currentChar or not currentRoot then return end
        pcall(function()
            part.Anchored = true
            part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0)
            part.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentRoot.Position.X, -2500, currentRoot.Position.Z)
            antiTPBatSetReplicationRoot(currentRoot, part)
        end)
    end)

    -- Modo ORIGINAL: leva o fake root para base - 9999 e retorna à base após 0,2 s.
    antiTPBatLoopConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiTPBatEnabled or not part.Parent then return end
        local currentChar, currentRoot = antiTPBatGetCharacter()
        if not currentChar or not currentRoot then return end
        local base = antiTPBatOriginalCFrame or currentRoot.CFrame
        local target = base + Vector3.new(0, -9999, 0)
        pcall(function() part.CFrame = target end)
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            if not antiTPBatEnabled or not part.Parent then return end
            pcall(function() part.CFrame = base end)
        end)
    end)

    antiTPBatCharacterConnection = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if antiTPBatEnabled then
            antiTPBatEnabled = false
            stopAntiTPBat()
            if mobBtnRefs and mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat then mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat(false) end
            if setAntiTPBatVisual then setAntiTPBatVisual(false) end
            saveConfig()
        end
    end)
end

saveConfig = function()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        pcall(saveGuiPositions)
        local function ks(e)
            if type(e) ~= "table" then
                return { kb = nil, gp = nil }
            end
            return { kb = e.kb and e.kb.Name or nil, gp = e.gp and e.gp.Name or nil }
        end
        local btnPos = {}
        pcall(function()
            btnPos = loadBtnPositions() or {}
        end)
        local guiPos = {}
        pcall(function()
            guiPos = loadGuiPositions() or {}
        end)
        local cfg = {
            normalSpeed = NS,
            carrySpeed = CS,
            dropBrainrotKey = ks(KB.DropBrainrot),
            autoLeftKey = ks(KB.AutoLeft),
            autoRightKey = ks(KB.AutoRight),
            autoBatKey = ks(KB.AutoBat),
            aimbotV2Key = ks(KB.AimbotV2),
            laggerToggleKey = ks(KB.LaggerToggle),
            laggerCarryKey = ks(KB.LaggerCarry),
            tpFloorKey = ks(KB.TPFloor),
            guiHideKey = ks(KB.GuiHide),
            speedToggleKey = ks(KB.SpeedToggle),
            antiDesyncKey = ks(KB.AntiDesync),
            tpBatKey = ks(KB.TPBat),
            antiTPBatKey = ks(KB.AntiTPBat),
            autoCarryEnabled = autoCarryEnabled == true,
            grabRadius = Steal.StealRadius,
            primeRange = Steal.PrimeRange,
            stealDuration = Steal.StealDuration,
            stealMode = "V2",
            antiRagdoll = antiRagdollEnabled == true,
            antiRagdollMode = antiRagdollMode,
            antiDie = antiDieEnabled == true or restartOnStartMatchRestoreAntiDie == true,
            antiDrop = antiDropEnabled == true,
            antiVoidFiling = antiVoidFilingEnabled == true,
            antiBat = antiBatEnabled == true or _antiBatWasEnabledForAimbot == true,
            antiTPBat = antiTPBatEnabled == true,
            nukedAntiLag = nukedAntiLagOn == true and nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn ~= true,
            nukedAntiLagNoSkins = nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn == true,
            autoStealEnabled = Steal.AutoStealEnabled == true,
            infiniteJump = infJumpEnabled == true,
            infJumpMode = infJumpMode,
            medusaCounter = medusaCounterEnabled == true,
            antiMedusaCounter = antiMedusaCounterEnabled == true,
            restartOnStartMatch = restartOnStartMatchEnabled == true,
            batCounter = batCounterEnabled == true,
            carrySpeedActive = carrySpeedActive == true,
            laggerModeEnabled = laggerModeEnabled == true,
            laggerCarryActive = laggerCarryActive == true,
            laggerSpeed = LAGGER_SPEED,
            laggerCarrySpeed = LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED,
            autoBat = autoBatEnabled == true,
            aimbotV2 = aimbotV2Enabled == true,
            autoAimbotDetectedAntiBat = autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatEnabled == true,
            warningAntiBat = warningAntiBatEnabled == true,
            aimbotSpeed = tonumber(_G._S2_AimSpeed or aimbotSpeedValue),
            aimbotLaggerSpeed = tonumber(_G._S2_AimLaggerSpeed or aimbotLaggerSpeedValue),
            autoSwing = autoSwingEnabled == true,
            unwalkEnabled = unwalkEnabled == true,
            antiLag = antiLagEnabled == true,
            antiLagV2 = antiLagV2Enabled == true,
            antiLagV3 = _G._S2AntiLagV3Enabled == true,
            antiRagdollV2 = _G._S2AntiRagdollV2Enabled == true,
            stretchRezV2 = _G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled == true,
            rainbowJoystick = _G.S2RainbowJoystickEnabled == true,
            autoTPEnabled = autoTPEnabled == true
                or _autoTPWasEnabledForAimbot == true
                or _autoTPWasEnabledForBat == true,
            autoTPHeight = autoTPHeight,
            tpDownMethod = _G._S2TPDownMethod,
            dropMethod = _G._S2DropMethod,
            lookAtEnemy = _G._S2LookAtEnabled == true,
            lookAtRadius = _G._S2LookAtRadius,
            floatEnabled = _G._S2FloatEnabled == true,
            batSkin = _G._S2BatSkin,
            skinChangerOutfit = tonumber(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection) or 1,
            rainbowBat = _G.S2RainbowBatEnabled == true,
            tpRagdoll = _G._S2TPRagdollEnabled == true,
            noPlayerCollision = _G.S2NoPlayerCollisionEnabled == true,
            vividGraphics = _G._S2VividGraphicsEnabled == true,
            motionBlur = _G._S2MotionBlurEnabled == true,
            guiTransparencyEnabled = guiTransparencyEnabled == true,
            mobileButtonsEnabled = mobileButtonsEnabled == true,
            mobileButtonsLocked = mobileButtonsLocked == true,
            uiLocked = uiLocked == true,
            mobileButtonsSize = mobileButtonsSize,
            circleButtonsEnabled = circleButtonsEnabled == true,
            guiScale = guiScaleValue,
            antiKick = antiKickEnabled == true,
            fovValue = fovValue,
            perButtonDrag = true,
            antiSummerBase = antiSummerBaseEnabled == true,
            headless = headlessEnabled == true,
            korblox = korbloxEnabled == true,
            skyTheme = currentSkyTheme,
            skyThemeEnabled = skyThemeEnabled,
            animPackEnabled = animPackEnabled,
            animPackName = animPackName,
            stretchVision = stretchVisionEnabled,
            guiTheme = currentGuiTheme,
            hubFontMode = HubFonts.mode,
            autoGrabStealBarSize = stealBarSizeValue,
            menuBackground = menuBackgroundIndex,
            buttonBackground = buttonBackgroundIndex,
            hubColor = hubColorIndex,
            background2 = background2Index,
            background2Version = 2,
            mobileVisual = mobileVisualIndex,
            hubLaggerEnabled = hubLaggerEnabled == true,
            laggerHubColor = laggerHubColorIndex,
            hiddenMobileButtons = hiddenMobileButtons,
            showPosition = showPositionEnabled == true,
            autoMoveSwing = autoMoveSwingEnabled == true,
            autoMoveSwingInterval = autoMoveSwingInterval,
            ragdollGui = ragdollGuiEnabled == true,
            introEnabled = introEnabled ~= false,
            introMusicEnabled = introMusicEnabled ~= false,
            espBox = espBoxEnabled == true,
            espHighlight = espHighlightEnabled == true,
            espTracer = espTracerEnabled == true,
            espSpeed = espSpeedEnabled == true,
            espRagdollCD = espRagdollCDEnabled == true,
            espAvatar = espAvatarEnabled == true,
            optimize = optimizeEnabled == true,
            nukeOptimizer = nukeOptimiserEnabled == true,
            selectedIntroMusic = selectedIntroMusic,
            btnPositions = btnPos,
            guiPositions = guiPos,
            autoPlaySide = autoPlaySide,
        }
        local encoded = HS:JSONEncode(cfg)
        local wrote = false
        pcall(function()
            writefile("000_duels.json", encoded)
            wrote = true
        end)
        pcall(function()
            writefile("000_duels_config.json", encoded)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv then
                getgenv()._S2HubCfg = cfg
                getgenv()._S2IntroEnabled = cfg.introEnabled
            end
        end)
        if not wrote then
            warn("[S2Hub] writefile did not succeed — using getgenv session backup")
        end
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[S2Hub] saveConfig failed: ", err)
    end
end
-- Skin Changer.000: outfits portados do script de referência (sem importar a UI/anim packs).
do
    if type(_G._S2SkinChangerCleanup) == "function" then
        pcall(_G._S2SkinChangerCleanup)
    end

    local TAG = "S2SkinChanger000_"
    local selectedOutfit = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection) or 1), 1, 6)
    _G._S2SkinChangerSelection = selectedOutfit
    local running = true
    local assetCache = {}
    local snapshot, removedAccessories = nil, {}
    local followItems, needReapply = {}, false
    local currentCharacter, setupToken = nil, 0
    local busy, pending = false, nil
    local renderConnection, characterConnection

    local function cloneList(list)
        local out = {}
        for _, obj in ipairs(list) do
            local ok, clone = pcall(function() return obj:Clone() end)
            if ok and clone then out[#out + 1] = clone end
        end
        return #out > 0 and out or nil
    end

    local function loadObjects(id)
        if assetCache[id] then
            local clones = cloneList(assetCache[id])
            if clones then return clones end
        end
        for attempt = 1, 4 do
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id))
            end)
            if ok and type(result) == "table" and #result > 0 then
                assetCache[id] = result
                return cloneList(result) or result
            end
            ok, result = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(id)
            end)
            if ok and result then
                assetCache[id] = { result }
                return cloneList({ result }) or { result }
            end
            task.wait(0.35 * attempt)
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function collectParts(objects)
        local out = {}
        for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = obj end
            for _, desc in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = desc end
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function destroyAll(objects)
        for _, obj in ipairs(objects or {}) do pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
    end

    local function findAttachment(char, name)
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local attachment = part:FindFirstChild(name)
                if attachment and attachment:IsA("Attachment") then return attachment end
            end
        end
    end

    local function buildWeld(item, target)
        if item.weld then pcall(function() item.weld:Destroy() end) end
        local handle = item.H
        handle.Anchored = false
        handle.CFrame = target.CFrame * item.c0 * item.c1:Inverse()
        local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
        weld.Name = TAG .. "weld"
        weld.Part0, weld.Part1 = target, handle
        weld.C0, weld.C1 = item.c0, item.c1
        weld.Parent = handle
        item.weld = weld
    end

    local function addFollow(char, handle, targetName, c0, c1)
        local target = char:FindFirstChild(targetName)
        if not target then pcall(function() handle:Destroy() end); return false end
        handle.CanCollide = false
        handle.Massless = true
        handle.Anchored = false
        handle.Parent = char
        local item = {
            H = handle, targetName = targetName, c0 = c0 or CFrame.new(),
            c1 = c1 or CFrame.new(), bad = 0, mode = "weld",
        }
        buildWeld(item, target)
        followItems[#followItems + 1] = item
        return true
    end

    local function waitReady(char, timeout)
        local started = tick()
        while tick() - started < (timeout or 10) do
            local ready = char.Parent and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                and char:FindFirstChild("Head") and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                and (char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
            local ok, loaded = pcall(function() return player:HasAppearanceLoaded() end)
            if ready and ((not ok) or loaded) then return true end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        return false
    end

    local function takeSnapshot(char)
        snapshot = { char = char, charMeshes = {} }
        local shirt = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt")
        local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
        snapshot.shirt = shirt and shirt.ShirtTemplate or nil
        snapshot.pants = pants and pants.PantsTemplate or nil
        local bodyColors = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors")
        if bodyColors then
            snapshot.colors = {
                bodyColors.HeadColor3, bodyColors.TorsoColor3, bodyColors.LeftArmColor3,
                bodyColors.RightArmColor3, bodyColors.LeftLegColor3, bodyColors.RightLegColor3,
            }
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            if head:IsA("MeshPart") then
                snapshot.headMeshId, snapshot.headTexId = head.MeshId, head.TextureID
            end
            local specialMesh = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
            if specialMesh then
                snapshot.specialMesh = {
                    MeshId = specialMesh.MeshId, TextureId = specialMesh.TextureId,
                    MeshType = specialMesh.MeshType, Scale = specialMesh.Scale,
                }
            end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("CharacterMesh") and obj.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then
                snapshot.charMeshes[#snapshot.charMeshes + 1] = obj:Clone()
            end
        end
        removedAccessories = {}
    end

    local function hidePart(part)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part:SetAttribute("S2SkinChangerHidden", true)
            part.Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    local function restoreDefault(char)
        if not char then return end
        followItems, needReapply = {}, false
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if obj.Name:sub(1, #TAG) == TAG then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end
        end
        for _, desc in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc:GetAttribute("S2SkinChangerHidden") then
                desc.Transparency = 0
                desc:SetAttribute("S2SkinChangerHidden", nil)
            end
        end
        for _, accessory in ipairs(removedAccessories) do
            if accessory and not accessory.Parent then pcall(function() accessory.Parent = char end) end
        end
        removedAccessories = {}
        if not snapshot or snapshot.char ~= char then return end

        local shirt = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt")
        if snapshot.shirt then
            shirt = shirt or Instance.new("Shirt")
            shirt.ShirtTemplate = snapshot.shirt
            shirt.Parent = char
        elseif shirt then shirt:Destroy() end
        local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
        if snapshot.pants then
            pants = pants or Instance.new("Pants")
            pants.PantsTemplate = snapshot.pants
            pants.Parent = char
        elseif pants then pants:Destroy() end
        if snapshot.colors then
            local bodyColors = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors") or Instance.new("BodyColors")
            bodyColors.HeadColor3, bodyColors.TorsoColor3, bodyColors.LeftArmColor3 =
                snapshot.colors[1], snapshot.colors[2], snapshot.colors[3]
            bodyColors.RightArmColor3, bodyColors.LeftLegColor3, bodyColors.RightLegColor3 =
                snapshot.colors[4], snapshot.colors[5], snapshot.colors[6]
            bodyColors.Parent = char
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            head.Transparency, head.CanCollide, head.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0, true, 0
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
            if face then face.Transparency = 0 end
            if head:IsA("MeshPart") and snapshot.headMeshId then
                pcall(function() head.MeshId, head.TextureID = snapshot.headMeshId, snapshot.headTexId end)
            end
            local specialMesh = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh")
            if snapshot.specialMesh then
                specialMesh = specialMesh or Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                specialMesh.MeshType = snapshot.specialMesh.MeshType
                specialMesh.MeshId, specialMesh.TextureId, specialMesh.Scale =
                    snapshot.specialMesh.MeshId, snapshot.specialMesh.TextureId, snapshot.specialMesh.Scale
                specialMesh.Parent = head
            elseif specialMesh then specialMesh:Destroy() end
        end
        pcall(function()
            local neck = char:FindFirstChild("Neck", true)
            if neck then neck.Enabled = true end
        end)
        local hasHeadMesh = false
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("CharacterMesh") and obj.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then hasHeadMesh = true end
        end
        if not hasHeadMesh then
            for _, mesh in ipairs(snapshot.charMeshes or {}) do pcall(function() mesh:Clone().Parent = char end) end
        end
    end

    local HOMERO = {
        body = { 10725826963, 86500008, 86500054, 86500036, 86500064, 86500078 },
        items = {
            { id = 103227869700418, offset = CFrame.new() },
            { id = 84952305140948, offset = CFrame.new() },
            { id = 122465238537030, offset = CFrame.new() },
        },
        pants = "rbxassetid://78591690208112",
        skinColor = Color3.fromRGB(234, 184, 146),
        headColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
    }

    local function applyBody(char)
        for _, id in ipairs(HOMERO.body) do
            local objects = loadObjects(id)
            if objects then
                for _, meshPart in ipairs(collectParts(objects)) do
                    local original = char:FindFirstChild(meshPart.Name)
                    if original and original:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local clone = meshPart:Clone()
                        clone.Name = TAG .. "body_" .. meshPart.Name
                        clone.CanCollide, clone.Anchored, clone.Massless = false, false, true
                        clone.Size = original.Size
                        addFollow(char, clone, original.Name, CFrame.new(), CFrame.new())
                        hidePart(original)
                    end
                end
                destroyAll(objects)
            end
        end
    end

    local function attachItem(char, entry)
        local objects = loadObjects(entry.id)
        if not objects then return false end
        local handle
        for _, part in ipairs(collectParts(objects)) do
            if part.Name == "Handle" then handle = part; break end
            if not handle then handle = part end
        end
        if not handle then destroyAll(objects); return false end
        local clone = handle:Clone()
        destroyAll(objects)
        clone.Name = TAG .. "item_" .. tostring(entry.id)
        local wrap = clone:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("WrapLayer")
        local target, c0, c1
        if wrap then
            target = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            c0, c1 = entry.offset or CFrame.new(), CFrame.new()
        else
            local handleAttachment = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
            local bodyAttachment = handleAttachment and findAttachment(char, handleAttachment.Name)
            if bodyAttachment then
                target = bodyAttachment.Parent
                c0 = bodyAttachment.CFrame * (entry.offset or CFrame.new())
                c1 = handleAttachment.CFrame
            else
                target = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                c0, c1 = entry.offset or CFrame.new(0, 1.4, 0), CFrame.new()
            end
        end
        if not target then clone:Destroy(); return false end
        return addFollow(char, clone, target.Name, c0, c1)
    end

    local function applyHomero(char)
        local bodyColors = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyColors") or Instance.new("BodyColors")
        bodyColors.HeadColor3 = HOMERO.headColor or HOMERO.skinColor
        bodyColors.TorsoColor3 = HOMERO.skinColor
        bodyColors.LeftArmColor3, bodyColors.RightArmColor3 = HOMERO.skinColor, HOMERO.skinColor
        bodyColors.LeftLegColor3, bodyColors.RightLegColor3 = HOMERO.skinColor, HOMERO.skinColor
        bodyColors.Parent = char
        for _, accessory in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if accessory:IsA("Accessory") then hidePart(accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")) end
        end
        local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
        pants.PantsTemplate, pants.Parent = HOMERO.pants, char
        applyBody(char)
        for _, entry in ipairs(HOMERO.items) do attachItem(char, entry) end
    end

    local function applyHeadlessKorblox(char)
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            hidePart(head)
            head.CanCollide, head.LocalTransparencyModifier = false, 1
            local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
            if face then face.Transparency = 1 end
        end
        for _, accessory in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if accessory:IsA("Accessory") then
                local weld = accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
                    or accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("WeldConstraint")
                    or accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D")
                if weld then
                    local p0, p1 = weld.Part0, weld.Part1
                    if (p0 and p0.Name == "Head") or (p1 and p1.Name == "Head") then
                        removedAccessories[#removedAccessories + 1] = accessory
                        accessory.Parent = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg") then
            for _, name in ipairs({ "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" }) do
                hidePart(char:FindFirstChild(name))
            end
            local objects = loadObjects(139607718)
            if objects then
                local part = collectParts(objects)[1]
                if part then
                    local leg = part:Clone()
                    leg.Name = TAG .. "korblox"
                    addFollow(char, leg, "RightUpperLeg", CFrame.new(), CFrame.new())
                end
                destroyAll(objects)
            end
        end
    end

    local outfits = {
        { label = "DEFAULT", default = true },
        {
            label = "SKIN 2", accessory = 10159600649, offset = Vector3.new(0, 1, -0.2),
            shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9683332638",
            pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93182020184041",
            headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402",
            headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true,
        },
        {
            label = "SKIN 3", accessory = 1744060292, offset = Vector3.new(0, 1.3, -0.2),
            shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9683332638",
            pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93182020184041",
            headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402",
            headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true,
        },
        {
            label = "SKIN 4", accessory = 8349240186, offset = Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0),
            shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11926549070",
            pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=13189494471",
            headMesh = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset/?id=16673245747", headless = true,
        },
        {
            label = "SKIN 5", accessory = 121097973925756, offset = Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0),
            shirt = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=123181702116947",
            pants = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=93330291631062",
            headMesh = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=134079402",
            headTexture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=133940918 ", headless = true,
        },
        { label = "SIMPSONS + KATSUKE", custom = true },
    }

    local function applyStandardOutfit(char, cfg)
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("CharacterMesh") and obj.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.Head then
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        local done = false
        if head:IsA("MeshPart") then
            done = pcall(function()
                head.MeshId = cfg.headMesh
                if cfg.headTexture then head.TextureID = cfg.headTexture end
            end)
        end
        if not done then
            local specialMesh = head:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpecialMesh") or Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            specialMesh.Parent = head
            specialMesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            specialMesh.MeshId = cfg.headMesh
            specialMesh.TextureId = cfg.headTexture or ""
        end
        if cfg.shirt then
            local shirt = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt") or Instance.new("Shirt")
            shirt.ShirtTemplate, shirt.Parent = cfg.shirt, char
        end
        if cfg.pants then
            local pants = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants") or Instance.new("Pants")
            pants.PantsTemplate, pants.Parent = cfg.pants, char
        end
        if cfg.accessory then
            local objects = loadObjects(cfg.accessory)
            if objects then
                local handle
                for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then handle = obj; break end
                    local part = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if part then handle = part; break end
                end
                if handle then
                    local clone = handle:Clone()
                    clone.Name = TAG .. "accessory"
                    addFollow(char, clone, "Head", CFrame.new(cfg.offset), CFrame.new())
                end
                destroyAll(objects)
            end
        end
        if cfg.headless then applyHeadlessKorblox(char) end
    end

    local function applyOutfit(index)
        local cfg = outfits[index]
        local char = player.Character
        if not (cfg and char) then return end
        waitReady(char, 10)
        if player.Character ~= char then return end
        local started = tick()
        while (not snapshot or snapshot.char ~= char) and player.Character == char and tick() - started < 12 do
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if player.Character ~= char then return end
        if not snapshot or snapshot.char ~= char then takeSnapshot(char) end
        task.wait(0.2)
        if player.Character ~= char then return end
        restoreDefault(char)
        if cfg.default then return end
        if cfg.custom then
            applyHomero(char)
        else
            applyStandardOutfit(char, cfg)
        end
    end

    local function requestOutfit(index)
        selectedOutfit = index
        _G._S2SkinChangerSelection = index
        if busy then pending = index; return end
        busy = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local target = index
            while target do
                pending = nil
                local ok, err = pcall(applyOutfit, target)
                if not ok then warn("[S2Hub Skin Changer] " .. tostring(err)) end
                target = pending
            end
            busy = false
        end)
    end

    _G._S2SetSkinChangerOutfit = function(index, persist)
        index = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1), 1, #outfits)
        requestOutfit(index)
        if persist ~= false and type(saveConfig) == "function" then pcall(saveConfig) end
        return index
    end

    local function setupCharacter(char)
        setupToken = setupToken + 1
        local token = setupToken
        currentCharacter = char
        snapshot, removedAccessories, followItems = nil, {}, {}
        needReapply = false
        char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 15)
        waitReady(char, 12)
        task.wait(0.3)
        if token ~= setupToken or player.Character ~= char then return end
        takeSnapshot(char)
        if selectedOutfit ~= 1 then requestOutfit(selectedOutfit) end
    end

    characterConnection = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.spawn(setupCharacter, char)
    end)
    if player.Character then task.spawn(setupCharacter, player.Character) end

    renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        for i = #followItems, 1, -1 do
            local item = followItems[i]
            local handle, alive = item.H, true
            if handle.Parent ~= char then alive = pcall(function() handle.Parent = char end) end
            if not alive then
                table.remove(followItems, i)
                needReapply = true
            else
                local target = char:FindFirstChild(item.targetName)
                if target then
                    local wanted = target.CFrame * item.c0 * item.c1:Inverse()
                    if item.mode == "weld" then
                        local weld = item.weld
                        if (not weld) or weld.Parent ~= handle or weld.Part0 ~= target or weld.Part1 ~= handle then
                            buildWeld(item, target)
                        elseif (handle.Position - wanted.Position).Magnitude > 1.5 then
                            item.bad = item.bad + 1
                            if item.bad > 15 then
                                item.mode = "anchor"
                                pcall(function() weld:Destroy() end)
                                handle.Anchored = true
                            end
                        else
                            item.bad = 0
                        end
                    else
                        handle.CFrame = wanted
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while running do
            task.wait(1.5)
            if needReapply and selectedOutfit ~= 1 then
                needReapply = false
                requestOutfit(selectedOutfit)
            end
        end
    end)

    _G._S2SkinChangerCleanup = function()
        running = false
        pcall(function() if renderConnection then renderConnection:Disconnect() end end)
        pcall(function() if characterConnection then characterConnection:Disconnect() end end)
        if currentCharacter and player.Character == currentCharacter then
            pcall(restoreDefault, currentCharacter)
        end
    end
end


task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(5) do
        saveConfig()
    end
end)
setAutoGrab, setInfJumpVisual, setAntiRagSplatterVisual, setAntiRagNoSplatterVisual, setMedusaVisual, setUnwalkVisual, setAntiLagVisual, setAutoSwingVisual =
    nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
setAntiLagV2Visual = nil
setTranspVisual, setLockVisual, setMobVisual, setCircleBtnsVisual = nil, nil, nil, nil
normalBox, carryBox, laggerBox, laggerCarryBox, radInput, autoTPHeightBox, durationBox, primeRangeBox =
    nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
mainFrame = nil
mainGuiRef = nil
_persistentConns = {}
local function trackConn(conn)
    table.insert(_persistentConns, conn)
    return conn
end
local function clearPersistentConns()
    for _, c in ipairs(_persistentConns) do
        pcall(function()
            c:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    _persistentConns = {}
end
refreshSpeedModeLabel = function()
    local modeText, configuredSpeed = getConfiguredModeSpeed()
    local modeDisplay = modeText .. ": " .. string.format("%.1f", configuredSpeed):gsub("%.", ",")
    if modeValLbl then
        if laggerCarryActive then
            modeValLbl.Text = "Lagger Carry"
        elseif laggerModeEnabled then
            modeValLbl.Text = "Lagger"
        elseif carrySpeedActive then
            modeValLbl.Text = "Carry"
        else
            modeValLbl.Text = "Normal"
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP and LP.Character
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local bb = head and head:FindFirstChild("MoveeSpeedBB")
        local lbl = bb and bb:FindFirstChild("DiscordLbl")
        local speedLbl = bb and bb:FindFirstChild("SpeedLbl")
        if lbl then
            lbl.Text = ".gg/HN24Xkqf8"
        end
        if speedLbl then
            speedLbl.Text = modeDisplay
        end
    end)
    if mobBtnRefs.normalSpeed then
        mobBtnRefs.normalSpeed(not carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.normalCarry then
        mobBtnRefs.normalCarry(carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerNormal then
        mobBtnRefs.laggerNormal(laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then
        mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if setCarryModeVisual then
        setCarryModeVisual(carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if setLaggerModeVisual then
        setLaggerModeVisual(laggerModeEnabled or laggerCarryActive)
    end
end
toggleCarryMode = function()
    if laggerModeEnabled or laggerCarryActive then
        if laggerCarryActive then
            setNormalCarry()
        else
            setNormalSpeed()
        end
    elseif carrySpeedActive then
        setNormalSpeed()
    else
        setNormalCarry()
    end
end
toggleLaggerMode = function()
    if laggerModeEnabled or laggerCarryActive then
        if laggerCarryActive then
            setLaggerNormal()
        else
            setLaggerCarry()
        end
    else
        if carrySpeedActive then
            setLaggerCarry()
        else
            setLaggerNormal()
        end
    end
end
toggleLaggerCarryMode = function()
    toggleLaggerMode()
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and workspace.CurrentCamera then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
    end
    if animPackEnabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.25)
            applyAnimPack(animPackName)
        end)
    end
end)
startUnwalk = function()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then
        return
    end
    local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            t:Stop()
        end
    end
    local anim = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if anim then
        unwalkSavedAnimate = anim:Clone()
        anim:Destroy()
    end
end
stopUnwalk = function()
    local c = LP.Character
    if c and unwalkSavedAnimate then
        unwalkSavedAnimate:Clone().Parent = c
        unwalkSavedAnimate = nil
    end
end
local stealPctLbl = nil
local stealFpsLbl = nil
local stealPingLbl = nil
local stealGuiRef = nil
local stealFill = nil
local stealTrack = nil
local stealTrackStroke = nil
stealBarSizeValue = 50
stealBarUIScale = nil
stealBarSizeValueLabel = nil
STEAL_BAR_BASE_WIDTH = 250
STEAL_BAR_BASE_HEIGHT = 38
function applyStealBarUISize(value, preserveCenter)
    local numericValue = tonumber(value)
    if not numericValue then numericValue = 50 end
    numericValue = math.clamp(math.floor(numericValue / 5 + 0.5) * 5, 20, 150)
    local oldScale = stealBarUIScale and tonumber(stealBarUIScale.Scale) or (stealBarSizeValue / 50)
    local newScale = numericValue / 50
    if stealBarUIScale and stealBarUIScale.Parent and math.abs(newScale - oldScale) > 0.0001 then
        if preserveCenter and stealBarFrame then
            local position = stealBarFrame.Position
            local shiftX = STEAL_BAR_BASE_WIDTH * (newScale - oldScale) / 2
            local shiftY = STEAL_BAR_BASE_HEIGHT * (newScale - oldScale) / 2
            stealBarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(position.X.Scale, position.X.Offset - shiftX,
                position.Y.Scale, position.Y.Offset - shiftY)
            if mergeGuiPosition then
                local updated = stealBarFrame.Position
                pcall(function()
                    mergeGuiPosition("_stealBar", updated.X.Scale, updated.X.Offset, updated.Y.Scale, updated.Y.Offset)
                end)
            end
        end
        stealBarUIScale.Scale = newScale
    end
    stealBarSizeValue = numericValue
    if stealBarSizeValueLabel and stealBarSizeValueLabel.Parent then
        stealBarSizeValueLabel.Text = tostring(numericValue)
    end
    return numericValue
end
applyStealBarBackground = function(index)
    if not stealBarFrame then return end
    local image = stealBarFrame:FindFirstChild("S2StealBarBackground")
    if image then
        pcall(function() image:Destroy() end)
    end
    local bg = ({
        [1] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
        [2] = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 242),
    })[background2Index] or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    if stealPctLbl then
        stealPctLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = (currentGuiTheme == "White" or background2Index == 2) and 1 or 0.35
    end
    stealBarFrame.BackgroundColor3 = bg
    local stroke = stealBarFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if stroke then
        stroke.Color = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12) or palette.primary
        stroke.Transparency = 1
    end
    if stealFill then
        stealFill.BackgroundColor3 = palette.primary
    end
    if stealTrack then
        stealTrack.BackgroundColor3 = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 238) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
    end
    if stealTrackStroke then
        stealTrackStroke.Color = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 238) or palette.primary
        stealTrackStroke.Transparency = 1
    end
end
local function createStealBarUI()
    for _, n in ipairs({ "MoveeStealBar", "S2InfoBar" }) do
        local old = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(n)
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
        local pgui = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pgui then
            local o = pgui:FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    local VIOLET = Color3.fromRGB(232, 48, 58)
    local BAR_TEXT = LIGHT_BLUE
    local BARBG = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    if type(_savedCfg) == "table" and _savedCfg.autoGrabStealBarSize ~= nil then
        local savedSize = tonumber(_savedCfg.autoGrabStealBarSize)
        if savedSize then
            stealBarSizeValue = math.clamp(math.floor(savedSize / 5 + 0.5) * 5, 20, 150)
        end
    end
    local SB_W, SB_H = STEAL_BAR_BASE_WIDTH, STEAL_BAR_BASE_HEIGHT
    local SB_SCALE = math.clamp((tonumber(stealBarSizeValue) or 50) / 50, 0.4, 3)
    local SB_RENDER_W, SB_RENDER_H = SB_W * SB_SCALE, SB_H * SB_SCALE
    local stealGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    stealGui.Name = "S2InfoBar"
    stealGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    stealGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    stealGui.DisplayOrder = 12
    if parentGui then
        parentGui(stealGui)
    else
        stealGui.Parent = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end
    stealGuiRef = stealGui
    stealBarFrame = Instance.new("Frame", stealGui)
    stealBarFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, SB_W, 0, SB_H)
    stealBarUIScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    stealBarUIScale.Name = "S2StealBarScale"
    stealBarUIScale.Scale = SB_SCALE
    stealBarUIScale.Parent = stealBarFrame
    do
        local _sbPos = nil
        pcall(function()
            _sbPos = loadGuiPositions()["_stealBar"]
        end)
        if _sbPos and tonumber(_sbPos.xo) ~= nil and tonumber(_sbPos.yo) ~= nil then
            local svpX = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X) or 800
            local svpY = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
            local sx = (_sbPos.xs or 0.5) * svpX + (_sbPos.xo or 0)
            local sy = (_sbPos.ys or 1) * svpY + (_sbPos.yo or 0)
            if sx + SB_RENDER_W >= 0 and sx - SB_RENDER_W <= svpX and sy + SB_RENDER_H >= 0 and sy - SB_RENDER_H <= svpY then
                stealBarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(_sbPos.xs or 0.5, _sbPos.xo or 0, _sbPos.ys or 1, _sbPos.yo or 0)
            else
                stealBarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -SB_RENDER_W / 2, 1, -(52 + SB_RENDER_H))
            end
        else
            stealBarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -SB_RENDER_W / 2, 1, -(52 + SB_RENDER_H))
        end
    end
    stealBarFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ({
        [1] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
        [2] = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 242),
    })[background2Index] or BARBG
    stealBarFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    stealBarFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    stealBarFrame.Active = true
    stealBarFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    applyStealBarBackground(buttonBackgroundIndex)
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner", stealBarFrame)
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local sbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", stealBarFrame)
    sbStroke.Color = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12) or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
    sbStroke.Thickness = 1
    sbStroke.Transparency = 1
    local barGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", stealBarFrame)
    barGrad.Enabled = false
    local pctLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", stealBarFrame)
    pctLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 16)
    pctLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
    pctLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pctLbl.Text = "0%"
    pctLbl.TextColor3 = BAR_TEXT
    pctLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    pctLbl.TextSize = 12
    pctLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    pctLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    pctLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = (currentGuiTheme == "White" or background2Index == 2) and 1 or 0.35
    pctLbl.ZIndex = 5
    stealPctLbl = pctLbl
    local fpsLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", stealBarFrame)
    fpsLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 82, 0, 16)
    fpsLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 44)
    fpsLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    fpsLbl.Text = "FPS: --"
    fpsLbl.TextColor3 = BAR_TEXT
    fpsLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    fpsLbl.TextSize = 10
    fpsLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    fpsLbl.Visible = false
    fpsLbl.ZIndex = 5
    stealFpsLbl = fpsLbl
    local pingLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", stealBarFrame)
    pingLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 82, 0, 16)
    pingLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -41, 0, 44)
    pingLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pingLbl.Text = "PING: --"
    pingLbl.TextColor3 = BAR_TEXT
    pingLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    pingLbl.TextSize = 10
    pingLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    pingLbl.Visible = false
    pingLbl.ZIndex = 5
    stealPingLbl = pingLbl
    local pTrack = Instance.new("Frame", stealBarFrame)
    pTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 9)
    pTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 22)
    pTrack.BackgroundColor3 = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 238) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 50)
    pTrack.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    pTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pTrack.ClipsDescendants = true
    local trackCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", pTrack)
    trackCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local trackStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pTrack)
    trackStroke.Color = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 238) or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
    trackStroke.Thickness = 1
    trackStroke.Transparency = 1
    stealTrack = pTrack
    stealTrackStroke = trackStroke
    stealFill = Instance.new("Frame", pTrack)
    stealFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    stealFill.BackgroundColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
    stealFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    local fillCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", stealFill)
    fillCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 20)
    local dragStart, dragStartPos, isDragging = nil, nil, false
    stealBarFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if uiLocked then return end
        if
            input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            isDragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            dragStartPos = stealBarFrame.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    isDragging = false
                    if stealBarFrame and writefile then
                        local p = stealBarFrame.Position
                        pcall(function()
                            mergeGuiPosition("_stealBar", p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if
            isDragging
            and not uiLocked
            and (
                input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            )
        then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            stealBarFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                dragStartPos.X.Scale,
                dragStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                dragStartPos.Y.Scale,
                dragStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    return stealGui
end
local function updateStealPct(pct)
    if stealPctLbl then
        stealPctLbl.Text = tostring(math.floor(pct * 100 + 0.5)) .. "%"
    end
    if stealFill then
        stealFill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(pct, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
        stealFill.BackgroundColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
    end
end
local function updateStealFps(fps)
    if stealFpsLbl then
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        stealFpsLbl.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(fps)
        stealFpsLbl.TextColor3 = palette.primary
    end
end
local function updateStealPing(ping)
    if stealPingLbl then
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        stealPingLbl.Text = "PING: " .. tostring(ping)
        stealPingLbl.TextColor3 = palette.primary
    end
end
local function createStealBar()
    createStealBarUI()
end
setStealStatusText = function(status, extra)
    if status == "STEALING" and extra then
        updateStealPct(extra)
    elseif status == "SEARCHING" then
    elseif status == "STOLE" then
        updateStealPct(1)
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    local frames = 0
    local t0 = tick()
    while true do
        frames = frames + 1
        local now = tick()
        if now - t0 >= 1.0 then
            local fps = math.floor(frames / (now - t0) + 0.5)
            updateStealFps(fps)
            local ping = 0
            pcall(function()
                local ok, v = pcall(function()
                    return math.floor(LP:GetNetworkPing() * 1000 + 0.5)
                end)
                if ok and type(v) == "number" then
                    ping = v
                end
            end)
            updateStealPing(ping)
            frames = 0
            t0 = now
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end)
local function updateStealProgress()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.05)
        if isStealing and stealStartTime then
            local elapsed = tick() - stealStartTime
            local pct = math.clamp(elapsed / math.max(Steal.HalfHoldMin, 0.01), 0, 1)
            updateStealPct(pct)
        elseif Steal.AutoStealEnabled then
            updateStealPct(0)
        else
            updateStealPct(0)
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(updateStealProgress)
-- Rainbow touch joystick, with original colors restored when disabled.
do
    local connections,renderConn,targets,watched={},nil,{},{}
    local function playerGui() return LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") end
    local function restore()
        for obj,info in pairs(targets) do if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj[info.prop]=info.color end) end end
        targets={}
    end
    local function track(obj)
        if targets[obj] then return end
        if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then targets[obj]={prop="ImageColor3",color=obj.ImageColor3}
        elseif obj:IsA("Frame") then targets[obj]={prop="BackgroundColor3",color=obj.BackgroundColor3} end
    end
    local function rescan()
        local pg=playerGui(); local touch=pg and pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui"); if not touch then return end
        for _,obj in ipairs(touch:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") or obj:IsA("Frame") then
                local name=obj.Name:lower(); if name:find("thumbstick") or name:find("stick") then track(obj) end
            end
        end
        local frame=touch:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
        if frame then
            for _,obj in ipairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("ImageButton") then track(obj); for _,d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("ImageLabel") then track(d) end end; break end
            end
        end
    end
    local function watchTouch(touch)
        if not touch or watched[touch] then return end
        watched[touch]=true
        table.insert(connections,touch.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
            task.delay(0.15,function() if _G.S2RainbowJoystickEnabled then rescan() end end)
        end))
    end
    local function stop()
        for _,c in ipairs(connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; connections={}; watched={}
        if renderConn then pcall(function() renderConn:Disconnect() end); renderConn=nil end
        restore()
    end
    local function start()
        stop(); rescan()
        local pg=playerGui(); if pg then
            table.insert(connections,pg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                if obj.Name=="TouchGui" and _G.S2RainbowJoystickEnabled then
                    task.delay(0.2,function() if _G.S2RainbowJoystickEnabled then rescan(); watchTouch(obj) end end)
                end
            end))
            watchTouch(pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui"))
        end
        renderConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local t=os.clock()*2
            local col=Color3.new(math.sin(t)*0.5+0.5,math.sin(t+2)*0.5+0.5,math.sin(t+4)*0.5+0.5)
            for obj,info in pairs(targets) do
                if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj[info.prop]=col end) else targets[obj]=nil end
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.S2SetRainbowJoystick=function(on)
        _G.S2RainbowJoystickEnabled=on==true
        if on then start() else stop() end
    end
end

local function destroyMobileButtons()
    pcall(saveBtnPositions)
    if mobGuiRef then
        pcall(function()
            mobGuiRef:Destroy()
        end)
        mobGuiRef = nil
    end
    for _, n in ipairs({ "MoveeMobileButtons" }) do
        local old = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(n)
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
        local pgui = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pgui then
            local o = pgui:FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    mobBtnRefs = {}
end
local function applyMobileActionButtonBackground(index)
    buttonBackgroundIndex = index
    if not mobGuiRef then return end
    for _, obj in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Frame") and obj:GetAttribute("BtnKey") then
            local image = obj:FindFirstChild("S2ActionBackground")
            if image then
                image:Destroy()
            end
            local key = obj:GetAttribute("BtnKey")
            obj.BackgroundColor3 = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or (ACTION_BTN_KEYS[key] and ACTION_BTN_IDLE or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
            obj.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        end
    end
end
local function buildMobileButtons()
    destroyMobileButtons()
    if not mobileButtonsEnabled then
        return
    end
    perButtonDragEnabled = true
    local savedPositions = loadBtnPositions()
    local BTN_SIZE = math.floor(mobileButtonsSize * 0.60)
    local BTN_W = math.floor(mobileButtonsSize * 0.72)
    local BTN_GAP = 9
    local CORNER_R = circleButtonsEnabled and BTN_SIZE or math.max(8, math.floor(BTN_SIZE * 0.22))
    local fontSize = math.max(10, math.floor(mobileButtonsSize * 0.11))
    local buttons = {
        { key = "drop", label = "DROP", toggle = false, exclusive = false },
        { key = "jump", label = "JUMP", toggle = false, exclusive = false },
        { key = "autoLeft", label = "AUTO\nLEFT", toggle = true, exclusive = true },
        { key = "autoRight", label = "AUTO\nRIGHT", toggle = true, exclusive = true },
        { key = "autoBat", label = "AIMBOT", toggle = true, exclusive = true },
        { key = "batV2", label = "BAT\nV2", toggle = true, exclusive = true },
        { key = "tpBat", label = "TP\nBAT", toggle = true, exclusive = true },
        { key = "antiTPBat", label = "ANTI\nTP", toggle = true, exclusive = false },
        { key = "tpDown", label = "TP\nDOWN", toggle = false, exclusive = false },
        { key = "normalCarry", label = "CARRY\nMODE", toggle = true, exclusive = false },
        { key = "laggerNormal", label = "LAGGER\nNORMAL", toggle = true, exclusive = false },
        { key = "laggerCarry", label = "LAGGER\nCARRY", toggle = true, exclusive = false },
    }
    local visibleButtons = {}
    for _, def in ipairs(buttons) do
        if not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] then
            table.insert(visibleButtons, def)
        end
    end
    buttons = visibleButtons
    local COLS = 2
    local totalW = (COLS * (BTN_W + BTN_GAP)) - BTN_GAP
    local totalH = #buttons > 0 and ((math.ceil(#buttons / COLS) * (BTN_SIZE + BTN_GAP)) - BTN_GAP) or BTN_SIZE
    local PAD = 10
    local vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
    local savedPanel = savedPositions["_panel"]
    local defPanelX = vp.X - totalW - PAD * 2 - 18
    local defPanelY = math.max(10, vp.Y / 2 - (totalH + PAD * 2) / 2)
    local panelXO = savedPanel and savedPanel.xo or defPanelX
    local panelYO = savedPanel and savedPanel.yo or defPanelY
    local mobGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    mobGui.Name = "S2MobileButtons"
    mobGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    mobGui.DisplayOrder = 15
    mobGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    parentGui(mobGui)
    mobGuiRef = mobGui
    local th = getTheme()
    local PINK_ON = th.MOB_ON
    local MOBILE_BG = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local BTN_BG_OFF = MOBILE_BG
    local ACTION_IDLE = MOBILE_BG
    local PANEL_BG = th.BG
    local BORDER_OFF = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 61, 68)
    local BORDER_ON = th.MOB_ON
    local mobilePalette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    local TEXT_OFF = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and mobilePalette.primary or Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246)
    local TEXT_ON = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and mobilePalette.primary or th.MOB_TEXT_ON
    local STROKE_W = 1
    local panel
    if not perButtonDragEnabled then
        panel = Instance.new("Frame", mobGui)
        panel.Name = "MobPanel"
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, totalW + PAD * 2, 0, totalH + PAD * 2)
        panel.Position = UDim2.new(0, panelXO, 0, panelYO)
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL_BG
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        panel.Active = true
        panel.ZIndex = 99
        local panelStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", panel)
        panelStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
        panelStroke.Thickness = 1
        panelStroke.Transparency = 0.7
        local gDragStart, gDragStartPos, gDragDown = nil, nil, false
        panel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if uiLocked then
                return
            end
            if
                input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                gDragDown = true
                gDragStart = input.Position
                gDragStartPos = panel.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        gDragDown = false
                        if writefile then
                            local posData = loadBtnPositions()
                            posData["_panel"] = { xo = panel.Position.X.Offset, yo = panel.Position.Y.Offset }
                            pcall(function()
                                writefile(MOB_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(posData))
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if
                gDragDown
                and not uiLocked
                and (
                    input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                )
            then
                local delta = input.Position - gDragStart
                panel.Position = UDim2.new(0, gDragStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X, 0, gDragStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)
    end
    for i, def in ipairs(buttons) do
        local col = (i - 1) % COLS
        local rowN = math.floor((i - 1) / COLS)
        local localX = PAD + col * (BTN_SIZE + BTN_GAP)
        local localY = PAD + rowN * (BTN_SIZE + BTN_GAP)
        local frameParent = perButtonDragEnabled and mobGui or panel
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame", frameParent)
        frame.Name = "MobBtn_" .. def.key
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, BTN_W, 0, BTN_SIZE)
        local sp = (not forceDefaultBtnPos) and savedPositions[def.key] or nil
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vpX = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 800
        local vpY = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
        if sp and tonumber(sp.xo) ~= nil and tonumber(sp.yo) ~= nil and tonumber(sp.xo) > vpX * 0.35 then
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, tonumber(sp.xo), 0, tonumber(sp.yo))
        else
            local xo = vpX - (totalW + PAD + 18) + localX
            local yo = math.max(10, vpY / 2 - (totalH + PAD * 2) / 2) + localY
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, xo, 0, yo)
        end
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = BTN_BG_OFF
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true
        frame.ZIndex = 102
        frame:SetAttribute("BtnKey", def.key)
        frame:SetAttribute("S2MobileButton", true)
        frame:SetAttribute("S2MobileButtonOn", false)
        local uic = Instance.new("UICorner", frame)
        uic.CornerRadius = circleButtonsEnabled and UDim.new(1, 0) or UDim.new(0, CORNER_R)
        local noBgGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", frame)
        noBgGradient.Enabled = mobileVisualIndex == 1
        noBgGradient.Name = "S2NoBackgroundGradient"
        noBgGradient.Rotation = 90
        noBgGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(58, 59, 65)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(43, 44, 49)),
