    pingFpsLbl:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
    pingFpsLbl.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
    pingFpsLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    pingFpsLbl.TextSize = 8
    pingFpsLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    pingFpsLbl.ZIndex = 8
    pingFpsLbl.Visible = false
    local perfFrames = 0
    local perfFpsStart = tick()
    local currentPerfFps, currentPerfPing = "--", "--"
    local function updatePerformanceReadouts()
        fpsOverlay.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(currentPerfFps)
        pingOverlay.Text = "Ping: " .. tostring(currentPerfPing) .. " ms"
        discSmall.Text = "FPS: " .. tostring(currentPerfFps)
        headerPingLbl.Text = "PING: " .. tostring(currentPerfPing)
        pingFpsLbl.Text = tostring(currentPerfPing) .. "ms - " .. tostring(currentPerfFps) .. "fps"
        local performanceColor = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
        discSmall.TextColor3 = performanceColor
        discSmall.TextStrokeColor3 = performanceColor
        headerPingLbl.TextColor3 = performanceColor
        headerPingLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = performanceColor
    end
    trackConn(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not pingFpsLbl or not pingFpsLbl.Parent then
            return
        end
        perfFrames = perfFrames + 1
        local now = tick()
        local elapsed = now - perfFpsStart
        if elapsed >= 1 then
            currentPerfFps = math.floor(perfFrames / elapsed)
            perfFrames = 0
            perfFpsStart = now
            updatePerformanceReadouts()
        end
    end))
    task.spawn(function()
        while pingFpsLbl and pingFpsLbl.Parent do
            task.wait(1)
            if not pingFpsLbl or not pingFpsLbl.Parent then
                break
            end
            pcall(function()
                local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
                local performanceStats = stats.PerformanceStats
                local pingStat = performanceStats and performanceStats:FindFirstChild("Ping")
                if pingStat then
                    currentPerfPing = math.floor(pingStat:GetValue() or 0)
                    updatePerformanceReadouts()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", header)
    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0.5, -13)
    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 11)
    closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeBtn.Text = "−"
    closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 225)
    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    closeBtn.TextSize = 16
    closeBtn.ZIndex = 10
    Instance.new("UICorner", closeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    local miniBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", gui)
    local miniBtnSize = math.floor(mobileButtonsSize * 0.55)
    miniBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, miniBtnSize, 0, miniBtnSize)
    miniBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -miniBtnSize / 2)
    miniBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    miniBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    miniBtn.Text = "+"
    miniBtn.TextColor3 = PINK
    miniBtn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
    miniBtn.TextSize = math.max(7, math.floor(mobileButtonsSize * 0.10))
    miniBtn.ZIndex = 20
    miniBtn.Visible = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", miniBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, math.max(8, math.floor(miniBtnSize * 0.22)))
    local miniStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", miniBtn)
    miniStroke.Color = PINK
    miniStroke.Thickness = 1
    miniStroke.Transparency = 0.7
    do
        local _savedMiniPos = _savedGuiPos["_mini"]
        if _savedMiniPos and tonumber(_savedMiniPos.xo) ~= nil and tonumber(_savedMiniPos.yo) ~= nil then
            local mvpX = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X) or 800
            local mvpY = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
            local mx = _savedMiniPos.xo + ((_savedMiniPos.xs or 0) * mvpX)
            local my = _savedMiniPos.yo + ((_savedMiniPos.ys or 0) * mvpY)
            if mx >= 0 and mx <= mvpX and my >= 0 and my <= mvpY then
                miniBtn.Position = UDim2.new(
                    _savedMiniPos.xs or 0,
                    _savedMiniPos.xo or 0,
                    _savedMiniPos.ys or 0,
                    _savedMiniPos.yo or 0
                )
            end
        end
        local mdn, mds, msp = false, nil, nil
        miniBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if uiLocked then
                return
            end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                mdn = true
                mds = i.Position
                msp = miniBtn.Position
                i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        mdn = false
                        if writefile then
                            local p = miniBtn.Position
                            pcall(function()
                                mergeGuiPosition("_mini", p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        trackConn(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if
                mdn
                and not uiLocked
                and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
            then
                miniBtn.Position = UDim2.new(
                    msp.X.Scale,
                    msp.X.Offset + (i.Position.X - mds.X),
                    msp.Y.Scale,
                    msp.Y.Offset + (i.Position.Y - mds.Y)
                )
            end
        end))
    end
    local collapsedUI = false
    local collapseVisibilityCache = {}
    local FULL_OUTER_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, H)
    local COLLAPSED_OUTER_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, headerH + 8)
    local COLLAPSE_Y_SHIFT = (H - (headerH + 8)) / 2
    local collapseTweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local collapseTweenToken = 0
    local function setMainCollapsed(state)
        collapsedUI = state == true
        collapseTweenToken = collapseTweenToken + 1
        local currentCollapseTweenToken = collapseTweenToken
        outer:SetAttribute("S2HubCollapseTweening", true)
        closeBtn.Text = collapsedUI and "+" or "−"
        closeBtn.TextSize = collapsedUI and 18 or 16
        miniBtn.Visible = false
        if collapsedUI then
            -- O cabeçalho e seus controles são a própria UI minimizada:
            -- mantenha-os explicitamente visíveis.
            header.Visible = true
            closeBtn.Visible = true
            titleBadge.Visible = true
            headerPerfBox.Visible = true
            collapseVisibilityCache = {}
            for _, child in ipairs(mainFrame:GetChildren()) do
                if child ~= header and child ~= headerFix and child ~= hDivider and child:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    collapseVisibilityCache[child] = child.Visible
                    child.Visible = false
                end
            end
            collapseVisibilityCache[headerFix] = headerFix.Visible
            collapseVisibilityCache[hDivider] = hDivider.Visible
            headerFix.Visible = false
            hDivider.Visible = false
            if bg then
                collapseVisibilityCache[bg] = bg.Visible
                bg.Visible = false
            end
            if texBg then
                collapseVisibilityCache[texBg] = texBg.Visible
                texBg.Visible = false
            end
            local p = outer.Position
            local viewportY = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y) or 600
            -- Use dimensões efetivamente renderizadas; H em pixels lógicos
            -- ignora UIScale e causava deslocamento vertical em escalas menores.
            local renderedFullHeight = outer.AbsoluteSize.Y
            local renderedScale = H > 0 and (renderedFullHeight / H) or 1
            local collapsedHalfHeight = ((headerH + 8) * renderedScale) / 2
            local currentTopY = outer.AbsolutePosition.Y
            local currentCenterY = currentTopY + (renderedFullHeight / 2)
            -- Mantém o topo/cabeçalho no mesmo ponto, exceto quando o limite
            -- da tela exige trazer a janela minimizada para dentro do viewport.
            -- Preserva o topo quando há espaço; se a posição salva já estiver
            -- fora da tela, limita o centro para que o cabeçalho nunca suma.
            local targetCenterY = math.clamp(
                currentTopY + collapsedHalfHeight,
                collapsedHalfHeight,
                math.max(collapsedHalfHeight, viewportY - collapsedHalfHeight)
            )
            local actualCollapseShift = currentCenterY - targetCenterY
            outer:SetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection", actualCollapseShift)
            TweenService:Create(outer, collapseTweenInfo, {
                Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset - actualCollapseShift),
                Size = COLLAPSED_OUTER_SIZE,
            }):Play()
        else
            for child, wasVisible in pairs(collapseVisibilityCache) do
                pcall(function()
                    if child and child.Parent ~= nil then
                        child.Visible = wasVisible
                    end
                end)
            end
            collapseVisibilityCache = {}
            local restoreCollapseShift = tonumber(outer:GetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection")) or COLLAPSE_Y_SHIFT
            outer:SetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection", restoreCollapseShift)
            local p = outer.Position
            TweenService:Create(outer, collapseTweenInfo, {
                Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + restoreCollapseShift),
                Size = FULL_OUTER_SIZE,
            }):Play()
            applyMenuBackground(menuBackgroundIndex)
        end
        -- Posição durante o tween é transitória. Salvar só a coordenada final
        -- impede que cada execução some a correção de recolhimento novamente.
        task.delay(collapseTweenInfo.Time + 0.03, function()
            if outer and outer.Parent and collapseTweenToken == currentCollapseTweenToken then
                outer:SetAttribute("S2HubCollapseTweening", false)
                if not collapsedUI then
                    outer:SetAttribute("S2HubCollapseYCorrection", 0)
                end
                pcall(saveGuiPositions)
            end
        end)
        pcall(saveGuiPositions)
    end
    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setMainCollapsed(not collapsedUI)
    end)
    miniBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setMainCollapsed(false)
    end)
    local tabBarH = 0
    local contentFrameY = headerH + 8
    local contentH = H - contentFrameY
    local tabBar = Instance.new("Frame", mainFrame)
    tabBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, tabBarH)
    tabBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 105)
    tabBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tabBar.ZIndex = 12
    local tabBarStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", tabBar)
    tabBarStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 72)
    tabBarStroke.Thickness = 1
    tabBarStroke.Transparency = 1
    Instance.new("UICorner", tabBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    tabBar.Visible = false
    local tabLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", tabBar)
    tabLay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    tabLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    tabLay.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
    tabLay.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    local tabNames = { "Speed", "Combat", "Auto", "Steal Mode", "Visual", "Keybind", "Settings" }
    local tabPages = {}
    local tabButtons = {}
    local activeTabName = "Speed"
    local function makePage(name)
        local sf = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", mainFrame)
        sf.Name = name .. "Page"
        sf.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, contentH)
        sf.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, contentFrameY)
        sf.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sf.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sf.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        sf.ScrollBarImageColor3 = PINK
        sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        sf.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        sf.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        sf.Visible = (name == "Speed")
        sf.ZIndex = 10
        sf.Active = true
        local ll = Instance.new("UIListLayout", sf)
        ll.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ll.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
        local pd = Instance.new("UIPadding", sf)
        pd.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
        pd.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
        pd.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
        pd.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12)
        return sf
    end
    local singlePage = makePage("All")
    singlePage.Visible = true
    for _, name in ipairs(tabNames) do
        tabPages[name] = singlePage
    end
    tabPages["Speed Config"] = tabPages["Speed"]
    tabPages["Steal"] = tabPages["Steal Mode"]
    tabPages["Visuals"] = tabPages["Visual"]
    local function switchTab(name)
        activeTabName = name
        _G._S2_ActiveTab = name
        local showPage = tabPages[name]
        local unique = {}
        for _, tn in ipairs(tabNames) do
            local pg = tabPages[tn]
            if pg then
                unique[pg] = (pg == showPage)
            end
        end
        for pg, show in pairs(unique) do
            pg.Visible = show
        end
        for n, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
            local on = (n == name)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(3, 3, 5)
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.05 or 0.28
            btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240) or Color3.fromRGB(145, 145, 155)
            local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if st then
                st.Transparency = on and 0.12 or 0.68
                st.Color = on and Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 220) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 62)
            end
        end
    end
    for i, name in ipairs(tabNames) do

        bst.Color = (name == "Speed") and Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 220) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 62)
        bst.Thickness = 1.25
        bst.Transparency = (name == "Speed") and 0.12 or 0.68
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            switchTab(name)
        end)
        tabButtons[name] = b
    end
    _G._S2_SwitchTab = switchTab
    if _G._S2_ActiveTab and tabPages[_G._S2_ActiveTab] then
        switchTab(_G._S2_ActiveTab)
    end

    do

    local categoryMarkers = {}
    local function getCategoryOutlineColor()
        return _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1])
    end
    local function mkSectionLbl(parent, txt)
        local spacer = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
        spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        spacer.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, 0)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt:upper()
        l.TextColor3 = SECT_LBL
        l.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        l.TextSize = 15
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 9
        local div = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        div.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        div:SetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline", true)
        div.BackgroundColor3 = getCategoryOutlineColor()
        div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        div.BorderSizePixel = 0
        div.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local sp2 = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        sp2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5)
        sp2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sp2.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        table.insert(categoryMarkers, { page = parent, title = row, marker = nil })
    end
    local IS_PC = (UIS.MouseEnabled and not UIS.TouchEnabled)
    local function addHoverFX(frame, stroke)
        if not IS_PC or not frame then
            return
        end
        local scale = frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", frame)
        scale.Scale = 1
        local baseStrokeT = stroke and stroke.Transparency or 0.6
        local baseStrokeC = stroke and stroke.Color or ROW_BORDER
        local baseStrokeThickness = stroke and stroke.Thickness or 1
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService
                :Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1.03 })
                :Play()
            if stroke then
                local isHubPrimary = stroke:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true
                local hoverColor = isHubPrimary
                    and _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1])
                    or PINK
                TweenService:Create(stroke, ti, {
                    Transparency = isHubPrimary and baseStrokeT or 0.05,
                    Color = hoverColor,
                    Thickness = baseStrokeThickness,
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(scale, ti, { Scale = 1 }):Play()
            if stroke then
                local restoreColor = stroke:GetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline") == true
                    and _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1])
                    or baseStrokeC
                TweenService:Create(stroke, ti, {
                    Transparency = baseStrokeT,
                    Color = restoreColor,
                    Thickness = baseStrokeThickness,
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
    end
    local function addHoverPop(guiObj, pop)
        if not IS_PC or not guiObj then
            return
        end
        local scale = guiObj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", guiObj)
        scale.Scale = 1
        guiObj.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                scale,
                TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Scale = pop or 1.12 }
            ):Play()
        end)
        guiObj.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
        end)
    end
    local function addClickFX(button, visual)
        if not button then
            return
        end
        local target = visual or button
        local scale = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", target)
        local base = scale.Scale
        local function down()
            TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = base * 0.88 }):Play()
        end
        local function up()
            TweenService
                :Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = base })
                :Play()
        end
        button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(down)
        button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(up)
        button.MouseLeave:Connect(up)
        button.Activated:Connect(function()
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame", target)
            ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ring.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            ring.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.ZIndex = 20
            Instance.new("UICorner", ring).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
            st.Color = PINK
            st.Thickness = 2
            st.Transparency = 0.15
            TweenService:Create(
                ring,
                TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Size = UDim2.new(1.9, 0, 1.9, 0) }
            ):Play()
            TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Transparency = 1, Thickness = 0.5 }):Play()
            task.delay(0.4, function()
                pcall(function()
                    ring:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end
    local function mkRow(parent, h)
        h = (h or 30) + 14
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
        f:SetAttribute("S2HubCardSurface", true)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = background2Index == 2 and Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 242)
            or Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 18)
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.ZIndex = 8
        f.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
        stroke:SetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline", true)
        stroke.Color = _G._S2GetAdaptiveHubOutlineColor(HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1])
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.12
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11)
        addHoverFX(f, stroke)
        local spacer = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
        spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        spacer.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren() + 1
        return f
    end
    local function mkLabel(row, txt)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextColor3 = WHITE
        l.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        l.TextSize = #tostring(txt) > 23 and 12 or 14
        l.TextWrapped = true
        l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 9
        return l
    end
    local function styleToggleStrokes(pill, dot)
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        local trackStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        trackStroke.Color = _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
        trackStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        trackStroke.Transparency = 0
        trackStroke:SetAttribute("S2HubToggleTrackStroke", true)
        trackStroke.Parent = pill
    end
    local function mkPill(row)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 28)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0.5, -14)
        pill:SetAttribute("S2HubToggleTrack", true)
        pill:SetAttribute("S2ToggleOn", false)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = _G._S2GetToggleOffTrackColor()
        pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pill.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", pill)
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -8)
        dot:SetAttribute("S2HubToggleKnob", true)
        dot:SetAttribute("S2ToggleOn", false)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        styleToggleStrokes(pill, dot)
        addHoverPop(pill, 1.15)
        return pill, dot
    end
    local function animPill(pill, dot, on)
        pill:SetAttribute("S2ToggleOn", on == true)
        dot:SetAttribute("S2ToggleOn", on == true)
        dot:SetAttribute("S2ToggleDot", true)
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        local trackColor = on and _G._S2DarkenHubColor(palette.primary, 0.35)
            or _G._S2GetToggleOffTrackColor()
        local knobColor = on and _G._S2GetToggleOnKnobColor(palette) or _G._S2GetToggleOffColor(palette)
        local outlineColor = knobColor
        TweenService
            :Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundColor3 = trackColor })
            :Play()
        TweenService
            :Create(
                dot,
                TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back),
                {
                    Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -8),
                    BackgroundColor3 = knobColor,
                }
            )
            :Play()
        local trackStroke = pill:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if trackStroke then
            TweenService:Create(trackStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Color = outlineColor }):Play()
        end
    end
    local function mkToggle(parent, txt, cb, labelWidthScale, labelTextSize)
        local row = mkRow(parent, 30)
        local label = mkLabel(row, txt)
        if labelWidthScale then
            label.Size = UDim2.new(labelWidthScale, 0, 1, 0)
        end
        if labelTextSize then
            label.TextSize = labelTextSize
        end
        local pill, dot = mkPill(row)
        local on = false
        local function sv(state)
            on = state
            animPill(pill, dot, state)
        end
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton", pill)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.ZIndex = 11
        addClickFX(clk, pill)
        clk.Activated:Connect(function()
            on = not on
            sv(on)
            if cb then
                local ok, err = pcall(cb, on)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[S2Hub] toggle error (" .. tostring(txt) .. "): ", err)
                end
            end
        end)
        return sv
    end
    local function touchFocus(tb)
        tb.TextEditable = true
        tb.Active = true
        tb.Selectable = true
        tb.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local isPointer = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            if isPointer and tb and tb.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    tb:CaptureFocus()
                    tb.CursorPosition = #tb.Text + 1
                    tb.SelectionStart = 1
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function mkBoxRow(parent, txt, default, cb, minVal, maxVal, step)
        minVal = minVal or 1
        maxVal = maxVal or 500
        step = step or 1
        local row = mkRow(parent, 34)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local container = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 28)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.5, -14)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.ZIndex = 9
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox", container)
        tb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        tb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = tostring(default)
        tb.TextColor3 = PINK
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        tb.TextSize = 15
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.ZIndex = 10
        tb.Active = true
        tb.TextEditable = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
        addHoverPop(tb, 1.08)
        local currentVal = default
        local function normalizeValue(value)
            value = tonumber(value)
            if not value then
                return nil
            end
            value = math.clamp(value, minVal, maxVal)
            if step and step > 0 then
                value = math.floor(value / step + 0.5) * step
            end
            if step and step < 1 then
                value = tonumber(string.format("%.3f", value)) or value
            else
                value = math.floor(value + 0.5)
            end
            return value
        end
        local function updateValue(newVal)
            newVal = normalizeValue(newVal)
            if newVal == nil then
                return false
            end
            currentVal = newVal
            tb.Text = tostring(newVal)
            if cb then
                cb(newVal)
            end
            return true
        end
        tb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local entered = tostring(tb.Text or ""):gsub(",", ".")
            local cleaned = entered:gsub("[^%d%-%.]", "")
            if cleaned ~= tb.Text then
                tb.Text = cleaned
            end
        end)
        tb.Focused:Connect(function()
            task.defer(function()
                if tb and tb.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        tb.CursorPosition = #tb.Text + 1
                        tb.SelectionStart = 1
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end)
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local n = normalizeValue(tb.Text)
            if n ~= nil and updateValue(n) then
                return
            end
            tb.Text = tostring(currentVal)
        end)
        touchFocus(tb)
        return tb
    end
    local function isGamepadInput(inp)
        return inp and inp.UserInputType and inp.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") ~= nil
    end
    local function isBindableInput(inp)
        if not inp or inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
            return false
        end
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            return true
        end
        return isGamepadInput(inp)
    end
    local function kbMatch(entry, kc)
        return kc and (kc == entry.kb or (entry.gp and kc == entry.gp))
    end
    local function mkKBButton(row, kbEntry, bindType, cb)
        local function getLabel()
            local k = kbEntry[bindType]
            return k and k.Name or "..."
        end
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 26)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0.5, -13)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = getLabel()
        btn.TextColor3 = PINK
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        btn.TextSize = 12
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        addHoverPop(btn, 1.08)
        addClickFX(btn)
        local li = false
        local lc
        local pv = btn.Text
        local ls = 0
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if li then
                li = false
                _anyKeyListening = false
                if lc then
                    lc:Disconnect()
                    lc = nil
                end
                btn.Text = pv
                btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                return
            end
            pv = btn.Text
            li = true
            _anyKeyListening = true
            ls = tick()
            btn.Text = "..."
            btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
            lc = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if not li then
                    return
                end
                if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                    li = false
                    _anyKeyListening = false
                    if lc then
                        lc:Disconnect()
                        lc = nil
                    end
                    btn.Text = pv
                    btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                    return
                end
                local isGp = isGamepadInput(inp)
                if isGp and not (tick() - ls >= 0.15) then
                    return
                end
                if not isBindableInput(inp) then
                    return
                end
                if (bindType == "gp" and not isGp) or (bindType == "kb" and isGp) then
                    return
                end
                btn.Text = inp.KeyCode.Name
                pv = inp.KeyCode.Name
                li = false
                _anyKeyListening = false
                if lc then
                    lc:Disconnect()
                    lc = nil
                end
                btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                kbEntry[bindType] = inp.KeyCode
                if cb then
                    cb(inp.KeyCode, isGp)
                end
            end)
        end)
        return btn
    end
    local function mkToggleKB(parent, txt, kbEntry, bindType, onToggle, onKB)
        local row = mkRow(parent, 36)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        mkKBButton(row, kbEntry, bindType or "kb", function(k, isGp)
            if onKB then
                onKB(k, isGp)
            end
        end)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 24)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -12)
        pill:Destroy()
        local pill2 = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        pill2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 24)
        pill2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0.5, -12)
        pill2.BackgroundColor3 = OFF
        pill2.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pill2.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local dot2 = Instance.new("Frame", pill2)
        dot2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        dot2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -8)
        dot2.BackgroundColor3 = GRAY
        dot2.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot2.ZIndex = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        styleToggleStrokes(pill2, dot2)
        addHoverPop(pill2, 1.15)
        local kbb = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
        if kbb then
            kbb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0.5, -12)
            kbb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 24)
        end
        local on = false
        local function sv(s)
            on = s
            animPill(pill2, dot2, s)
        end
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton", pill2)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.ZIndex = 11
        addClickFX(clk, pill2)
        clk.Activated:Connect(function()
            if _anyKeyListening then
                return
            end
            on = not on
            sv(on)
            if onToggle then
                onToggle(on)
            end
        end)
        return sv
    end
    local function mkKBRow(parent, txt, kbEntry, bindType, onKB)
        local row = mkRow(parent, 36)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        mkKBButton(row, kbEntry, bindType or "kb", function(k, isGp)
            if onKB then
                onKB(k, isGp)
            end
        end)
        return row
    end
    local function mkActionRow(parent, txt, onActivate)
        local row = mkRow(parent, 36)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 26)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, -13)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = PINK3
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = "▶"
        btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        btn.TextSize = 12
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        addHoverPop(btn, 1.1)
        addClickFX(btn)
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.06), { BackgroundColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary }):Play()
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), { BackgroundColor3 = PINK3 }):Play()
            end)
            if onActivate then

            end
        end)
        return row, btn

        local cards, cardStrokes, cardTextLayers = {}, {}, {}
        local function refreshSkinSelection()
            local selected = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection) or 1), 1, #skinNames)
            local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
            for i, card in ipairs(cards) do
                local isSelected = i == selected
                local stroke = cardStrokes[i]
                if stroke then
                    stroke:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", isSelected)
                    stroke.Color = isSelected and palette.primary or Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)
                end
                card.TextColor3 = palette.primary
                local labelText = skinNames[i] .. (isSelected and "\nSELECTED" or "")
                for _, layer in ipairs(cardTextLayers[i] or {}) do
                    layer.Text = labelText
                    layer.TextColor3 = palette.primary
                end
            end
        end
        for i, name in ipairs(skinNames) do
            local card = Instance.new("TextButton", grid)

            card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 17)

            stroke.Name = "CardBorder"
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)
            stroke.Thickness = 1
            cards[i], cardStrokes[i] = card, stroke

            local blurOffsets = { {-1, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, -1}, {0, 1}, {0, 0} }
            for layerIndex, offset in ipairs(blurOffsets) do
                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
                label.Name = "SoftTextLayer" .. tostring(layerIndex)
                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                label.BorderSizePixel = 0
                label.Position = UDim2.new(0, offset[1], 0, offset[2])
                label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                label.ZIndex = 114
                label.Text = name
                label.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
                label.TextTransparency = layerIndex == #blurOffsets and 0.28 or 0.62
                label.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                label.TextWrapped = true
                label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
                label.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
                label.TextSize = name == "SIMPSONS + KATSUKE" and 12 or 15
                label:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
                blurLayers[#blurLayers + 1] = label
            end
            cardTextLayers[i] = blurLayers

                if type(_G._S2SetSkinChangerOutfit) == "function" then
                    _G._S2SetSkinChangerOutfit(i, true)
                    refreshSkinSelection()
                end
            end)
        end

                    sizeBox:CaptureFocus()
                    sizeBox.CursorPosition = #sizeBox.Text + 1
                    sizeBox.SelectionStart = 1
                end)
            end
        end
        sizeBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local cleaned = sizeBox.Text:gsub("[^%d%-%.]", "")
            if cleaned ~= sizeBox.Text then
                sizeBox.Text = cleaned
            end
        end)
        sizeBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            task.defer(function()
                if sizeBox and sizeBox.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        sizeBox.CursorPosition = #sizeBox.Text + 1
                        sizeBox.SelectionStart = 1
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end)
        sizeBox.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local isPointer = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            if isPointer then
                task.defer(focusBtnSizeBox)
            end
        end)
        sizeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local n = tonumber(sizeBox.Text)
            if n then
                n = math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 40, 150)
                mobileButtonsSize = n
                sizeBox.Text = tostring(mobileButtonsSize)
                if mobileButtonsEnabled then
                    buildMobileButtons()
                end
                saveConfig()
            else
                sizeBox.Text = tostring(mobileButtonsSize)
            end
        end)
        touchFocus(sizeBox)
    end
    local buttonsAddRemovePanel = nil
    local buttonsAddRemoveOpen = false
    local buttonsAddRemoveAnimId = 0
    local buttonManageDefs = {
        { key = "drop", label = "DROP BR" },

    local function refreshButtonsAddRemovePanel()
        if not buttonsAddRemovePanel then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(buttonsAddRemovePanel:GetChildren()) do
            if child:GetAttribute("ButtonManageRow") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local y = 38
        for _, def in ipairs(buttonManageDefs) do
            local rowFrame = Instance.new("Frame", buttonsAddRemovePanel)
            rowFrame:SetAttribute("ButtonManageRow", true)
            rowFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 28)
            rowFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, y)
            rowFrame.BackgroundColor3 = INP
            rowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
            rowFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            rowFrame.ZIndex = 30
            Instance.new("UICorner", rowFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
            local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", rowFrame)
            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, 0)
            nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
            nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            nameLbl.Text = def.label
            nameLbl:SetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary", true)
            nameLbl.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).secondary
            nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
            nameLbl.TextSize = 13
            nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            nameLbl.ZIndex = 31
            local stateBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", rowFrame)
            stateBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 27)
            stateBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0.5, -12)
            stateBtn:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key])
            stateBtn.BackgroundColor3 = hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] and Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 78) or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
            stateBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            stateBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            stateBtn.Text = hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] and "OFF" or "ON"
            stateBtn:SetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary", not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key])
            stateBtn.TextColor3 = not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] and (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).secondary or WHITE
            stateBtn.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
            stateBtn.TextSize = 13
            stateBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            stateBtn.ZIndex = 32
            Instance.new("UICorner", stateBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            local stateStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", stateBtn)
            stateStroke:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key])
            stateStroke.Color = hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] and GRAY or (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
            stateStroke.Thickness = 1
            stateStroke.Transparency = 0.2
            stateBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
                hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] = not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key]
                if not hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] then
                    hiddenMobileButtons[def.key] = nil
                end
                buildMobileButtons()
                refreshButtonsAddRemovePanel()
                saveConfig()
            end)
            y = y + 32
        end
    end
    local function toggleButtonsAddRemovePanel()
        if not buttonsAddRemovePanel then
            buttonsAddRemovePanel = Instance.new("Frame", outer)
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.Name = "ButtonsAddRemovePanel"
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, math.max(310, 42 + #buttonManageDefs * 32))
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.Position = UDim2.new(1, 34, 0, 70)
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.BackgroundColor3 = BG
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.ZIndex = 25
            buttonsAddRemovePanel:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPanel", true)
            Instance.new("UICorner", buttonsAddRemovePanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
            local panelStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", buttonsAddRemovePanel)
            panelStroke:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
            panelStroke.Color = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
            panelStroke.Thickness = 1
            panelStroke.Transparency = 0.25
            local panelTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", buttonsAddRemovePanel)
            panelTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 30)
            panelTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4)
            panelTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            panelTitle.Text = "BUTTONS  ADD / REMOVE"
            panelTitle:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
            panelTitle.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
            panelTitle.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
            panelTitle.TextSize = 13
            panelTitle.ZIndex = 30
            refreshButtonsAddRemovePanel()
        end
        buttonsAddRemoveOpen = not buttonsAddRemoveOpen
        buttonsAddRemoveAnimId = buttonsAddRemoveAnimId + 1
        local animId = buttonsAddRemoveAnimId
        local targetPos = UDim2.new(1, 12, 0, 70)
        local hiddenPos = UDim2.new(1, 34, 0, 70)
        if buttonsAddRemoveOpen then
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.Visible = true
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.Position = hiddenPos
            buttonsAddRemovePanel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            local openTween = TweenService:Create(buttonsAddRemovePanel, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                Position = targetPos,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
            })
            openTween:Play()
        else
            local closeTween = TweenService:Create(buttonsAddRemovePanel, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                Position = hiddenPos,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            })
            closeTween:Play()
            closeTween.Completed:Connect(function()
                if buttonsAddRemovePanel and animId == buttonsAddRemoveAnimId and not buttonsAddRemoveOpen then
                    buttonsAddRemovePanel.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end

        local function cycleHubFontMode(direction)
            local currentIndex = 1
            for index, modeName in ipairs(fontModes) do
                if modeName == HubFonts.mode then
                    currentIndex = index
                    break
                end
            end
            local nextIndex = ((currentIndex - 1 + direction) % #fontModes) + 1
            HubFonts.mode = fontModes[nextIndex]
            HubFonts.apply()
            saveConfig()
        end
        leftButton.Activated:Connect(function()
            cycleHubFontMode(-1)
        end)
        rightButton.Activated:Connect(function()
            cycleHubFontMode(1)
        end)
