    applyButtonBackground(buttonBackgroundIndex)
    local function toggleAutoDirection(direction)
        local isLeft = direction == "left"
        local enabled = isLeft and autoLeftEnabled or autoRightEnabled
        if enabled then
            if isLeft then
                autoLeftEnabled = false
                stopAutoLeft()
            else
                autoRightEnabled = false
                stopAutoRight()
            end
            saveConfig()
            return
        end
        if autoPlayEnabled then stopAutoPlay() end
        if autoLeftEnabled then autoLeftEnabled = false; stopAutoLeft() end
        if autoRightEnabled then autoRightEnabled = false; stopAutoRight() end
        if autoBatEnabled then
            stopBatAimbot()
            if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
        end
        if aimbotV2Enabled then stopAimbotV2() end
        if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot() end
        if isLeft then
            autoLeftEnabled = true
            startAutoLeft()
            if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(true) end
            if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(true) end
        else
            autoRightEnabled = true
            startAutoRight()
            if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(true) end
            if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(true) end
        end
        saveConfig()
    end
    trackConn(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if _anyKeyListening then
            return
        end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            if gpe or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return
            end
        elseif not isGamepadInput(input) then
            return
        end
        if not isBindableInput(input) then
            return
        end
        local kc = input.KeyCode
        if kbMatch(KB.SpeedToggle, kc) then
            toggleCarryMode()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.LaggerToggle, kc) then
            toggleLaggerMode()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.AntiTPBat, kc) then
            antiTPBatEnabled = not antiTPBatEnabled
            if antiTPBatEnabled then startAntiTPBat() else stopAntiTPBat() end
            if setAntiTPBatVisual then setAntiTPBatVisual(antiTPBatEnabled) end
            if mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat then mobBtnRefs.antiTPBat(antiTPBatEnabled) end
            saveConfig()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.TPBat, kc) then
            if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
                _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
                if batTP.setVisual then
                    batTP.setVisual(false)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
                    mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false)
                end
            else
                if autoBatEnabled then
                    stopBatAimbot()
                    if autoBatSetVisual then
                        autoBatSetVisual(false)
                    end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false)
                    end
                end
                if aimbotV2Enabled then
                    stopAimbotV2()
                    if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then
                        mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(false)
                    end
                end
                if autoPlayEnabled then
                    stopAutoPlay()
                    if autoPlaySetVisual then
                        autoPlaySetVisual(false)
                    end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then
                        mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false)
                    end
                end
                _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
                if batTP.setVisual then
                    batTP.setVisual(true)
                end
                if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
                    mobBtnRefs.tpBat(true)
                end
            end
            saveConfig()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.DropBrainrot, kc) then
            runDrop()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.TPFloor, kc) then
            runTPFloor()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.AutoLeft, kc) then
            toggleAutoDirection("left")
        elseif kbMatch(KB.AutoRight, kc) then
            toggleAutoDirection("right")
        elseif kbMatch(KB.AutoBat, kc) then
            if not autoBatEnabled then
                _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = "classic"
                if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
                    _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
                    if batTP.setVisual then batTP.setVisual(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false) end
                end
                if autoLeftEnabled then
                    autoLeftEnabled = false
                    stopAutoLeft()
                    if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoLeft then mobBtnRefs.autoLeft(false) end
                end
                if autoRightEnabled then
                    autoRightEnabled = false
                    stopAutoRight()
                    if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoRight then mobBtnRefs.autoRight(false) end
                end
                antiBatBypassEnabled = false
                queueAutoBatStart()
                if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(true) end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true) end
            else
                if _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == "classic" then
                    _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = nil
                end
                stopBatAimbot()
                if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
                if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then mobBtnRefs.autoBat(false) end
            end
            saveConfig()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.AimbotV2, kc) then
            if not aimbotV2Enabled then
                _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = "v2"
                if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
                    _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
                    if batTP.setVisual then batTP.setVisual(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false) end
                end
                if autoPlayEnabled then
                    stopAutoPlay()
                    if autoPlaySetVisual then autoPlaySetVisual(false) end
                    if mobBtnRefs.autoPlay then mobBtnRefs.autoPlay(false) end
                end
                startAimbotV2()
            else
                if _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection == "v2" then
                    _G._S2ManualAimbotSelection = nil
                end
                stopAimbotV2()
            end
            saveConfig()
        elseif kbMatch(KB.GuiHide, kc) then
            setMainCollapsed(not collapsedUI)
        end
    end))
    local function updateCategoryOutlines()
        if not mainFrame or not mainFrame.Parent then return end
        local parentAbs = mainFrame.AbsolutePosition
        local scale = (mainGuiScaleRef and mainGuiScaleRef.Scale) or 1
        for i, info in ipairs(categoryMarkers) do
            local marker = info.marker
            if not marker then
                marker = Instance.new("Frame", mainFrame)
                marker.Name = "CategoryOutline_" .. tostring(i)
                marker.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                marker.BorderSizePixel = 0
                marker.ZIndex = 9
                marker.Active = false
                local corner = Instance.new("UICorner", marker)
                corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
                local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", marker)
                stroke.Thickness = 1
                stroke.Transparency = 0.12
                stroke:SetAttribute("S2AdaptiveHubOutline", true)
                info.marker = marker
            end
            local titleOrder = info.title.LayoutOrder
            local nextOrder = math.huge
            for _, other in ipairs(categoryMarkers) do
                if other.page == info.page and other.title.LayoutOrder > titleOrder then
                    nextOrder = math.min(nextOrder, other.title.LayoutOrder)
                end
            end
            local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
            for _, child in ipairs(info.page:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child ~= info.title and child.LayoutOrder > titleOrder and child.LayoutOrder < nextOrder
                    and child.BackgroundTransparency < 1 and child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") then
                    local pos, size = child.AbsolutePosition, child.AbsoluteSize
                    minX, minY = math.min(minX, pos.X), math.min(minY, pos.Y)
                    maxX, maxY = math.max(maxX, pos.X + size.X), math.max(maxY, pos.Y + size.Y)
                end
            end
            local valid = minX < maxX and minY < maxY and info.page.Visible
            marker.Visible = valid
            if valid then
                local gap = 5
                marker.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((minX - parentAbs.X - gap) / scale, (minY - parentAbs.Y - gap) / scale)
                marker.Size = UDim2.fromOffset((maxX - minX + gap * 2) / scale, (maxY - minY + gap * 2) / scale)
                local stroke = marker:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then stroke.Color = getCategoryOutlineColor() end
            end
        end
    end
    local categoryOutlineLayout = singlePage:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    if categoryOutlineLayout then
        trackConn(categoryOutlineLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
            task.defer(updateCategoryOutlines)
        end))
    end
    trackConn(singlePage:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
        task.defer(updateCategoryOutlines)
    end))
    trackConn(mainFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
        task.defer(updateCategoryOutlines)
    end))
    trackConn(mainGuiScaleRef:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Scale"):Connect(function()
        task.defer(updateCategoryOutlines)
    end))
    -- Os contornos continuam como filhos da janela; ao arrastar, seguem-na
    -- com offsets estáveis e só recalculam quando conteúdo, escala ou scroll mudam.
    task.defer(updateCategoryOutlines)
    task.delay(0.2, updateCategoryOutlines)
    gui.Enabled = true
    outer.Visible = true
    mainFrame.Visible = true
    header.Visible = true
    closeBtn.Visible = true
    setMainCollapsed(true)
end
_savedCfg = nil
local function loadConfigKeys()
    local cfg = nil
    pcall(function()
        if getgenv and type(getgenv()._S2HubCfg) == "table" then
            cfg = getgenv()._S2HubCfg
        end
    end)
    if not cfg then
        local raw = nil
        pcall(function()
            if isfile("000_duels.json") then
                raw = readfile("000_duels.json")
            end
        end)
        if not raw then
            pcall(function()
                if isfile("000_duels_config.json") then
                    raw = readfile("000_duels_config.json")
                end
            end)
        end
        if not raw then
            pcall(function()
                raw = readfile("000_duels.json")
            end)
        end
        if raw then
            local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
                return HS:JSONDecode(raw)
            end)
            if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
                cfg = decoded
            end
        end
    end
    if not cfg then
        return
    end
    _savedCfg = cfg
    introEnabled = true
    local function lk(e, d)
        if type(d) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        if d.kb and Enum.KeyCode[d.kb] then
            e.kb = Enum.KeyCode[d.kb]
        else
            e.kb = nil
        end
        if d.gp and Enum.KeyCode[d.gp] then
            e.gp = Enum.KeyCode[d.gp]
        else
            e.gp = nil
        end
    end
    lk(KB.DropBrainrot, cfg.dropBrainrotKey)
    lk(KB.AutoLeft, cfg.autoLeftKey)
    lk(KB.AutoRight, cfg.autoRightKey)
    lk(KB.AutoBat, cfg.autoBatKey)
    lk(KB.AimbotV2, cfg.aimbotV2Key)
    lk(KB.LaggerToggle, cfg.laggerToggleKey)
    lk(KB.LaggerCarry, cfg.laggerCarryKey)
    lk(KB.TPFloor, cfg.tpFloorKey)
    lk(KB.GuiHide, cfg.guiHideKey)
    lk(KB.SpeedToggle, cfg.speedToggleKey)
    lk(KB.AntiDesync, cfg.antiDesyncKey)
    lk(KB.TPBat, cfg.tpBatKey)
    lk(KB.AntiTPBat, cfg.antiTPBatKey)
    if cfg.autoCarryEnabled ~= nil then
        autoCarryEnabled = cfg.autoCarryEnabled == true
    end
    if cfg.normalSpeed ~= nil then
        local loadedNormalSpeed = tonumber(cfg.normalSpeed)
        if loadedNormalSpeed and loadedNormalSpeed > 0 and loadedNormalSpeed <= 500 then
            NS = tonumber(string.format("%.3f", loadedNormalSpeed)) or loadedNormalSpeed
        end
    end
    if cfg.carrySpeed ~= nil then
        local loadedCarrySpeed = tonumber(cfg.carrySpeed)
        if loadedCarrySpeed and loadedCarrySpeed > 0 and loadedCarrySpeed <= 500 then
            CS = tonumber(string.format("%.3f", loadedCarrySpeed)) or loadedCarrySpeed
        end
    end
    if cfg.aimbotSpeed ~= nil then
        local aimSpeed = tonumber(cfg.aimbotSpeed)
        if aimSpeed and aimSpeed > 0 and aimSpeed <= 500 then
            _G._S2_AimSpeed = aimSpeed
            aimbotSpeedValue = aimSpeed
        end
    end
    if cfg.aimbotLaggerSpeed ~= nil then
        local aimLaggerSpeed = tonumber(cfg.aimbotLaggerSpeed)
        if aimLaggerSpeed and aimLaggerSpeed > 0 and aimLaggerSpeed <= 500 then
            _G._S2_AimLaggerSpeed = aimLaggerSpeed
            aimbotLaggerSpeedValue = aimLaggerSpeed
        end
    end
    if cfg.laggerSpeed then
        LAGGER_SPEED = cfg.laggerSpeed
    end
    if cfg.laggerCarrySpeed then
        LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = cfg.laggerCarrySpeed
    end
    if cfg.grabRadius ~= nil then
        local radius = tonumber(cfg.grabRadius)
        if radius then
            Steal.StealRadius = math.clamp(math.floor(radius + 0.5), 1, 300)
            Steal.HalfFireRange = Steal.StealRadius
        end
    end
    if cfg.primeRange ~= nil then
        local primeRange = tonumber(cfg.primeRange)
        if primeRange then
            Steal.PrimeRange = math.clamp(math.floor(primeRange + 0.5), 1, 300)
        end
    end
    if cfg.stealDuration ~= nil then
        local duration = tonumber(cfg.stealDuration)
        if duration then
            Steal.StealDuration = math.clamp(duration, 0.05, 10)
        end
    end
    Steal.Mode = "V2"
    if cfg.autoTPHeight ~= nil then
        local height = tonumber(cfg.autoTPHeight)
        if height then autoTPHeight = math.clamp(math.floor(height + 0.5), 0, 500) end
    end
    if cfg.tpDownMethod == "SLOW" or cfg.tpDownMethod == "ZIGZAG" then
        _G._S2TPDownMethod = cfg.tpDownMethod
    else
        _G._S2TPDownMethod = "NORMAL"
    end
    if cfg.dropMethod == "STAND STILL" then
        _G._S2DropMethod = "STAND STILL"
    else
        _G._S2DropMethod = "TP UP"
    end
    if cfg.lookAtRadius ~= nil then _G._S2LookAtRadius = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.lookAtRadius) or 20, 1, 200) end
    _G._S2LookAtEnabled = cfg.lookAtEnemy == true
    _G._S2FloatEnabled = cfg.floatEnabled == true
    _G._S2TPRagdollEnabled = cfg.tpRagdoll == true
    _G._S2VividGraphicsEnabled = cfg.vividGraphics == true
    _G._S2MotionBlurEnabled = cfg.motionBlur == true
    if cfg.autoSwing ~= nil then
        autoSwingEnabled = cfg.autoSwing == true
    end
    if cfg.guiTransparencyEnabled ~= nil then
        guiTransparencyEnabled = cfg.guiTransparencyEnabled
    end
    if cfg.background2 ~= nil then
        local savedBackground2 = math.floor(tonumber(cfg.background2) or 1)
        if cfg.background2Version == 2 then
            background2Index = math.clamp(savedBackground2, 1, 2)
        elseif savedBackground2 == 3 then
            background2Index = 2
        else
            background2Index = 1
        end
    end
    if cfg.mobileVisual ~= nil then
        mobileVisualIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.mobileVisual) or 1), 1, 2)
    end
    if cfg.hubLaggerEnabled ~= nil then
        hubLaggerEnabled = cfg.hubLaggerEnabled == true
    end
    if cfg.laggerHubColor ~= nil then
        laggerHubColorIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.laggerHubColor) or 1), 1, #HUB_COLOR_PALETTES)
    end
    if cfg.hubColor ~= nil then
        if type(cfg.hubColor) == "string" then
            for i, name in ipairs(HUB_COLOR_NAMES) do
                if name == cfg.hubColor:upper() then
                    hubColorIndex = i
                    break
                end
            end
        else
            hubColorIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.hubColor) or 1), 1, #HUB_COLOR_PALETTES)
        end
    end
    if cfg.menuBackground ~= nil then
        menuBackgroundIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.menuBackground) or 1), 0, 16)
    end
    if cfg.buttonBackground ~= nil then
        buttonBackgroundIndex = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.buttonBackground) or 1), 0, 16)
    end
    if cfg.showPosition ~= nil then
        showPositionEnabled = cfg.showPosition == true
    end
    if type(cfg.hiddenMobileButtons) == "table" then
        hiddenMobileButtons = {}
        for key, value in pairs(cfg.hiddenMobileButtons) do
            if value == true then
                hiddenMobileButtons[tostring(key)] = true
            end
        end
    end
    if cfg.mobileButtonsEnabled ~= nil then
        mobileButtonsEnabled = cfg.mobileButtonsEnabled
    end
    if cfg.mobileButtonsSize ~= nil then
        mobileButtonsSize = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.mobileButtonsSize) or DEFAULT_MOBILE_BUTTONS_SIZE, 40, 150)
    else
        mobileButtonsSize = DEFAULT_MOBILE_BUTTONS_SIZE
    end
    if cfg.circleButtonsEnabled ~= nil then
        circleButtonsEnabled = cfg.circleButtonsEnabled == true
    end
    if cfg.antiKick ~= nil then
        antiKickEnabled = cfg.antiKick == true
    end
    if cfg.carrySpeedActive ~= nil then
        carrySpeedActive = cfg.carrySpeedActive
    end
    if cfg.laggerModeEnabled ~= nil then
        laggerModeEnabled = cfg.laggerModeEnabled
    end
    if cfg.laggerCarryActive ~= nil then
        laggerCarryActive = cfg.laggerCarryActive
    end
    if cfg.infJumpMode then
        infJumpMode = cfg.infJumpMode
    end
    if cfg.fovValue then
        fovValue = cfg.fovValue
        for idx, v in ipairs(fovOptions) do
            if v == fovValue then
                fovIndex = idx
            end
        end
    end
    if cfg.chromeImageVisible ~= nil then
        _G._MoveeDuels_bgImageVisible = cfg.chromeImageVisible == true
    end
    perButtonDragEnabled = true
    if cfg.autoMoveSwing ~= nil then
        autoMoveSwingEnabled = cfg.autoMoveSwing == true
    end
    if cfg.autoMoveSwingInterval then
        autoMoveSwingInterval = cfg.autoMoveSwingInterval
    end
    if cfg.skyTheme then
        currentSkyTheme = CANDY_SKY_PRESETS[cfg.skyTheme] and cfg.skyTheme or currentSkyTheme
    end
    skyThemeEnabled = false -- Sky Theme removido
    if cfg.animPackName and animPacks[cfg.animPackName] then animPackName = cfg.animPackName end
    if cfg.animPackEnabled ~= nil then animPackEnabled = cfg.animPackEnabled == true end
    if cfg.stretchVision ~= nil then stretchVisionEnabled = cfg.stretchVision == true end
    _G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled = cfg.stretchRezV2 == true
    if _G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled then stretchVisionEnabled=false end
    if cfg.autoAimbotDetectedAntiBat ~= nil then
        autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatEnabled = cfg.autoAimbotDetectedAntiBat == true
    end
    if cfg.warningAntiBat ~= nil then
        warningAntiBatEnabled = cfg.warningAntiBat == true
    end
    CandyApplyCustomSky("Off")
    if type(cfg.btnPositions) == "table" and next(cfg.btnPositions) ~= nil and writefile then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(MOB_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(cfg.btnPositions))
        end)
    end
    if type(cfg.guiPositions) == "table" and next(cfg.guiPositions) ~= nil and writefile then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(GUI_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(cfg.guiPositions))
        end)
    end
    if cfg.ragdollGui ~= nil then
        ragdollGuiEnabled = cfg.ragdollGui == true
    end
    if cfg.introEnabled ~= nil then
        introEnabled = cfg.introEnabled ~= false
    end
    if cfg.introMusicEnabled ~= nil then
        introMusicEnabled = cfg.introMusicEnabled ~= false
    end
    if cfg.selectedIntroMusic ~= nil then
        selectedIntroMusic = cfg.selectedIntroMusic
    end
end
local function loadConfigState()
    local cfg = _savedCfg
    if not cfg then
        return
    end
    if cfg.skinChangerOutfit ~= nil then
        _G._S2SkinChangerSelection = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(cfg.skinChangerOutfit) or 1), 1, 6)
    end
    if cfg.hubFontMode == "LuckyestGuys" or cfg.hubFontMode == "Gotham" or cfg.hubFontMode == "GothamBlack"
        or cfg.hubFontMode == "GOTHAM UI"
        or cfg.hubFontMode == "NORMAL FONT V1" or cfg.hubFontMode == "NORMAL FONT V2"
        or cfg.hubFontMode == "NORMAL FONT V3" then
        HubFonts.mode = cfg.hubFontMode
    end
    if cfg.autoGrabStealBarSize ~= nil then
        local loadedBarSize = tonumber(cfg.autoGrabStealBarSize)
        if loadedBarSize then applyStealBarUISize(loadedBarSize, false) end
    end
    if stealBarSizeValueLabel then stealBarSizeValueLabel.Text = tostring(stealBarSizeValue) end
    HubFonts.apply()
    if normalBox then
        normalBox.Text = tostring(NS)
    end
    if carryBox then
        carryBox.Text = tostring(CS)
    end
    if primeRangeBox then
        primeRangeBox.Text = tostring(Steal.PrimeRange)
    end
    if radInput then
        radInput.Text = tostring(Steal.StealRadius)
    end
    if durationBox then
        durationBox.Text = tostring(Steal.StealDuration)
    end
    if laggerBox then
        laggerBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED)
    end
    if laggerCarryBox then
        laggerCarryBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED)
    end
    local aimSpeedBox = mainGuiRef and mainGuiRef:FindFirstChild("AimbotSpeedBox", true)
    if aimSpeedBox and cfg.aimbotSpeed ~= nil then
        aimSpeedBox.Text = tostring(_G._S2_AimSpeed or aimbotSpeedValue)
    end
    local aimLaggerSpeedBox = mainGuiRef and mainGuiRef:FindFirstChild("AimbotLaggerSpeedBox", true)
    if aimLaggerSpeedBox and cfg.aimbotLaggerSpeed ~= nil then
        aimLaggerSpeedBox.Text = tostring(_G._S2_AimLaggerSpeed or aimbotLaggerSpeedValue)
    end
    if autoTPHeightBox then
        autoTPHeightBox.Text = tostring(autoTPHeight)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.15)
        if cfg.uiLocked then
            uiLocked = true
            if setLockVisual then
                setLockVisual(true)
            end
        end
        antiRagdollMode = "Splatter"
        if cfg.antiRagdollV2 then
            antiRagdollEnabled=false; stopAntiRagdoll()
            _G._S2SetAntiRagdollV2(true)
            if setAntiRagSplatterVisual then setAntiRagSplatterVisual(false) end
            if setAntiRagdollV2Visual then setAntiRagdollV2Visual(true) end
        else
            _G._S2SetAntiRagdollV2(false)
            if setAntiRagdollV2Visual then setAntiRagdollV2Visual(false) end
            if cfg.antiRagdoll then
                antiRagdollMode="Splatter"; antiRagdollEnabled=true
                if setAntiRagSplatterVisual then setAntiRagSplatterVisual(true) end
                startAntiRagdoll()
            else
                antiRagdollEnabled=false
                if setAntiRagSplatterVisual then setAntiRagSplatterVisual(false) end
                stopAntiRagdoll()
            end
            if setAntiRagNoSplatterVisual then setAntiRagNoSplatterVisual(false) end
        end
        if cfg.antiDie then
            antiDieEnabled = true
            if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(true) end
            enableAntiDie()
        end
        antiMedusaCounterEnabled = cfg.antiMedusaCounter == true
        if antiMedusaCounterSetVisual then antiMedusaCounterSetVisual(antiMedusaCounterEnabled) end
        if antiMedusaCounterEnabled then startAntiMedusaCounter() else stopAntiMedusaCounter() end
        restartOnStartMatchEnabled = cfg.restartOnStartMatch == true
        if restartOnStartMatchSetVisual then restartOnStartMatchSetVisual(restartOnStartMatchEnabled) end
        if restartOnStartMatchEnabled then startRestartOnStartMatch() else stopRestartOnStartMatch() end
        antiDropEnabled = cfg.antiDrop == true
        if setAntiDropVisual then setAntiDropVisual(antiDropEnabled) end
        if antiDropEnabled then startAntiDrop() else stopAntiDrop() end
        antiVoidFilingEnabled = cfg.antiVoidFiling == true
        if setAntiVoidFilingVisual then setAntiVoidFilingVisual(antiVoidFilingEnabled) end
        if antiVoidFilingEnabled then startAntiVoidFiling() else stopAntiVoidFiling() end
        antiBatEnabled = cfg.antiBat == true
        if setAntiBatVisual then setAntiBatVisual(antiBatEnabled) end
        if antiBatEnabled then startAntiBat() else stopAntiBat() end
        antiTPBatEnabled = cfg.antiTPBat == true
        if setAntiTPBatVisual then setAntiTPBatVisual(antiTPBatEnabled) end
        if antiTPBatEnabled then startAntiTPBat() else stopAntiTPBat() end
        if cfg.nukedAntiLagNoSkins then
            enableNukedAntiLag(true)
            if setNukedAntiLagNoSkinsVisual then setNukedAntiLagNoSkinsVisual(true) end
        elseif cfg.nukedAntiLag then
            enableNukedAntiLag(false)
            if setNukedAntiLagVisual then setNukedAntiLagVisual(true) end
        end
        if cfg.autoStealEnabled then
            Steal.AutoStealEnabled = true
            if setAutoGrab then
                setAutoGrab(true)
            end
            pcall(startAutoSteal)
        end
        if cfg.infiniteJump then
            infJumpEnabled = true
            if setInfJumpVisual then
                setInfJumpVisual(true)
            end
            if infJumpMode == "hold" then
                startHoldInfJump()
            end
        end
        if cfg.medusaCounter then
            medusaCounterEnabled = true
            if setMedusaVisual then
                setMedusaVisual(true)
            end
            setupMedusa(LP.Character)
        end
        if cfg.batCounter then
            batCounterEnabled = true
            if setBatCounterVisual then
                setBatCounterVisual(true)
            end
            startBatCounter()
        end
        refreshSpeedModeLabel()
        autoTPEnabled = cfg.autoTPEnabled == true
        if cfg.lookAtEnemy then _G._S2SetLookAtEnemy(true) end
        if cfg.floatEnabled then _G._S2ToggleFloat(true) end
        if cfg.tpRagdoll then _G._S2SetTPRagdoll(true) end
        if cfg.noPlayerCollision then enableNoPlayerCollision() end
        if noPlayerCollisionSetVisual then noPlayerCollisionSetVisual(cfg.noPlayerCollision == true) end
        _G.S2SetRainbowJoystick(cfg.rainbowJoystick == true)
        if rainbowJoystickSetVisual then rainbowJoystickSetVisual(cfg.rainbowJoystick == true) end
        if lookAtEnemySetVisual then lookAtEnemySetVisual(cfg.lookAtEnemy == true) end
        if floatSetVisual then floatSetVisual(cfg.floatEnabled == true) end
        if tpRagdollSetVisual then tpRagdollSetVisual(cfg.tpRagdoll == true) end
        if _S2TPMethodLabel then _S2TPMethodLabel.Text = _G._S2TPDownMethod end
        if _S2DropMethodLabel then _S2DropMethodLabel.Text = _G._S2DropMethod end
        if cfg.batSkin then _G._S2SetBatSkin(cfg.batSkin) end
        if _S2BatSkinLabel then _S2BatSkinLabel.Text = tostring(_G._S2BatSkin) end
        _G.S2SetRainbowBat(cfg.rainbowBat == true)
        if rainbowBatSetVisual then rainbowBatSetVisual(cfg.rainbowBat == true) end
        _G._S2SetVividGraphics(cfg.vividGraphics == true)
        _G._S2SetMotionBlur(cfg.motionBlur == true)
        if setVividGraphicsVisual then setVividGraphicsVisual(cfg.vividGraphics == true) end
        if setMotionBlurVisual then setMotionBlurVisual(cfg.motionBlur == true) end
        if stretchVisionSetVisual then stretchVisionSetVisual(stretchVisionEnabled) end
        if autoTPEnabled then
            if setAutoTPVisual then
                setAutoTPVisual(true)
            end
            startAutoTP()
        else
            stopAutoTP()
            if setAutoTPVisual then
                setAutoTPVisual(false)
            end
        end
        if setAutoSwingVisual then
            setAutoSwingVisual(autoSwingEnabled)
        end
        if cfg.autoBat then
            autoBatEnabled = true
            if autoBatSetVisual then
                autoBatSetVisual(true)
            end
            if mobBtnRefs.autoBat then
                mobBtnRefs.autoBat(true)
            end
            queueAutoBatStart()
        end
        if cfg.aimbotV2 then
            aimbotV2Enabled = true
            if aimbotV2SetVisual then
                aimbotV2SetVisual(true)
            end
            if mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2 then
                mobBtnRefs.aimbotV2(true)
            end
            startAimbotV2()
        end
        if cfg.unwalkEnabled then
            unwalkEnabled = true
            if setUnwalkVisual then
                setUnwalkVisual(true)
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                startUnwalk()
            end)
        end
        if cfg.antiLagV3 then
            _G._S2SetAntiLagV3(true)
            if setAntiLagV3Visual then setAntiLagV3Visual(true) end
        elseif cfg.antiLagV2 then
            enableAntiLagV2()
            if setAntiLagV2Visual then setAntiLagV2Visual(true) end
        elseif cfg.antiLag then
            enableAntiLag()
            if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(true) end
        end
        if cfg.antiSummerBase then
            enableAntiSummerBase()
            if setAntiSummerBaseVisual then
                setAntiSummerBaseVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.headless then
            headlessEnabled = true
            if headlessSetVisual then
                headlessSetVisual(true)
            end
            local _hc = LP.Character
            if _hc then
                applyHeadlessToChar(_hc, true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.korblox then
            korbloxEnabled = true
            if korbloxSetVisual then
                korbloxSetVisual(true)
            end
            local _kc = LP.Character
            if _kc then
                applyKorbloxToChar(_kc, true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espBox then
            espBoxEnabled = true
            if setEspBoxVisual then
                setEspBoxVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espHighlight then
            espHighlightEnabled = true
            if setEspHighlightVisual then
                setEspHighlightVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espTracer then
            espTracerEnabled = true
            if setEspTracerVisual then
                setEspTracerVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espSpeed then
            espSpeedEnabled = true
            if setEspSpeedVisual then
                setEspSpeedVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatEnabled and autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatSetVisual then
            autoAimbotDetectedAntiBatSetVisual(true)
        end
        if warningAntiBatEnabled and warningAntiBatSetVisual then
            warningAntiBatSetVisual(true)
        end
        if cfg.optimize then
            optimizeEnabled = true
            if setOptimizeVisual then
                setOptimizeVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.nukeOptimizer then
            enableNukeOptimizer()
            if setNukeOptimiserVisual then
                setNukeOptimiserVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espAvatar then
            espAvatarEnabled = true
            if setEspAvatarVisual then
                setEspAvatarVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if cfg.espRagdollCD then
            espRagdollCDEnabled = true
            if setEspRagdollCDVisual then
                setEspRagdollCDVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if espApply then
            espApply()
        end
        if cfg.antiKick then
            enableAntiKick()
            if antiKickSetVisual then
                antiKickSetVisual(true)
            end
        end
        if circleButtonsEnabled and setCircleBtnsVisual then
            setCircleBtnsVisual(true)
        end
        CandyApplyCustomSky("Off")
        if carrySpeedActive and not laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive then
            setNormalCarry()
        elseif laggerModeEnabled and not laggerCarryActive then
            setLaggerNormal()
        elseif laggerCarryActive then
            setLaggerCarry()
        else
            setNormalSpeed()
        end
        refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    end)
end
local _okInit, _errInit = pcall(function()
    loadConfigKeys()
    buildGui()
    if mobileButtonsEnabled then
        buildMobileButtons()
    end
    loadConfigState()
    if tonumber(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection) and tonumber(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection) > 1
        and type(_G._S2SetSkinChangerOutfit) == "function" then
        _G._S2SetSkinChangerOutfit(_G._S2SkinChangerSelection, false)
    end
    HubFonts.apply()
    if getgenv then
        local palette = HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[laggerHubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]
        getgenv()._S2LaggerHubColor = palette.primary
    end
    setHubLaggerVisual(hubLaggerEnabled)
    applyHubColor(hubColorIndex)
    applyBackground2(background2Index)
    applyMobileVisualButtons(mobileVisualIndex)
    if animPackButtonRef then animPackButtonRef.Text = animPackName end
    if skyThemeButtonRef then skyThemeButtonRef.Text = currentSkyTheme end
    applyAnimPack(animPackName)
    applyStretchVision(stretchVisionEnabled)
    _G._S2SetStretchRezV2(_G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled)
    if stretchRezV2SetVisual then stretchRezV2SetVisual(_G._S2StretchRezV2Enabled) end
    if setAntiDropVisual then setAntiDropVisual(antiDropEnabled) end
    if setAntiVoidFilingVisual then setAntiVoidFilingVisual(antiVoidFilingEnabled) end
    if antiVoidFilingEnabled then startAntiVoidFiling() end
    if setAntiBatVisual then setAntiBatVisual(antiBatEnabled) end
    if setAntiTPBatVisual then setAntiTPBatVisual(antiTPBatEnabled) end
    if antiDropEnabled then startAntiDrop() end
    if antiBatEnabled then startAntiBat() end
    if antiTPBatEnabled then startAntiTPBat() end
    if animPackToggleRef then animPackToggleRef(animPackEnabled) end
    if skyThemeToggleRef then skyThemeToggleRef(skyThemeEnabled) end
    if stretchVisionSetVisual then stretchVisionSetVisual(stretchVisionEnabled) end
    CandyApplyCustomSky("Off")
end)
if not _okInit then
    warn("[S2Hub] init error: ", _errInit)
    print("[S2Hub] init error: ", _errInit)
end
-- Crasher não é iniciado automaticamente; removido do fluxo de gameplay.
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.4)
    if introEnabled == false then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local guiParent = (LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
        for _, n in ipairs({ "S2Intro", "CalciumIntro" }) do
            local o = guiParent:FindFirstChild(n) or game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "S2Intro"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.DisplayOrder = 9999
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        pcall(function()
            sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)
        if not sg.Parent then
            sg.Parent = guiParent
        end
        HubFonts.register(sg)
        local bg = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
        bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local t1 = Instance.new("TextLabel", bg)
        t1.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
        t1.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.4, 0)
        t1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t1.Text = "VENOM DUELS"
        t1:SetAttribute("S2HubColorPrimary", true)
        t1.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).primary
        t1.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        t1.TextSize = 44
        t1.TextTransparency = 1
        local t2 = Instance.new("TextLabel", bg)
        t2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
        t2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.4, 52)
        t2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t2.Text = "VENOM.VS"
        t2:SetAttribute("S2HubColorSecondary", true)
        t2.TextColor3 = (HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[hubColorIndex] or HUB_COLOR_PALETTES[1]).secondary
        t2.Font = Enum.Font.LuckiestGuy
        t2.TextSize = 18
        t2.TextTransparency = 1
        local introSound = Instance.new("Sound")
        introSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://828172750"
        introSound.Volume = 1
        introSound.Parent = sg
        introSound:Play()
        TweenService:Create(t1, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(t2, TweenInfo.new(0.5), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
        task.wait(1.5)
        TweenService:Create(t1, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(t2, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bg, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }):Play()
        task.wait(0.4)
        sg:Destroy()
    end)
end)
;(function()
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local _acApplied = false
    local _acReturn = nil
    local _acGrace = 0
    local _acStealWas = false
    local function isCarryName(name)
        local n = tostring(name or ""):lower()
        return n:find("brainrot")
            or n:find("animal")
            or n:find("carry")
            or n:find("grab")
            or n:find("steal")
            or n:find("hold")
    end
    local function isIgnoredCarryTool(name)
        local n = tostring(name or ""):lower()
        return n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") or n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone")
    end
    local function isCarryingBrainrot(char)
        if not char then
            return false
        end
        for _, name in ipairs({ "Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab" }) do
            local v = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
            if v then
                if v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value then
                    return true
                end
                if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then
                    return true
                end
                if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Value ~= "" then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
                if child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    return true
                end
                if isCarryName(child.Name) then
                    return true
                end
            elseif child:IsA("Tool") and not isIgnoredCarryTool(child.Name) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function applyCarry()
        if _acApplied then
            return
        end
        if laggerCarryActive then
            _acReturn = "LaggerCarry"
        elseif laggerModeEnabled then
            _acReturn = "Lagger"
        elseif carrySpeedActive then
            _acReturn = "Carry"
        else
            _acReturn = "Normal"
        end
        _acApplied = true
        _acGrace = tick() + 0.75
        if _acReturn == "Lagger" or _acReturn == "LaggerCarry" then
            setLaggerCarry()
        else
            setNormalCarry()
        end
    end
    local function restore()
        if not _acApplied then
            return
        end
        _acApplied = false
        _acGrace = 0
        local r = _acReturn
        _acReturn = nil
        if r == "Lagger" then
            setLaggerNormal()
        elseif r == "LaggerCarry" then
            setLaggerCarry()
        elseif r == "Carry" then
            setNormalCarry()
        else
            setNormalSpeed()
        end
    end
    RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if autoCarryEnabled ~= true then
            if _acApplied then
                restore()
            end
            _acStealWas = false
            return
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not hum then
            if _acApplied then
                restore()
            end
            _acStealWas = false
            return
        end
        local st = hum:GetState()
        local gotHit = st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        local stealing = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
        local carrying = isCarryingBrainrot(char)
        if stealing and not _acStealWas then
            _acStealWas = true
            applyCarry()
        elseif not stealing then
            _acStealWas = false
        end
        if carrying and not _acApplied then
            applyCarry()
        end
        if _acApplied then
            local graceDone = tick() > _acGrace
            if gotHit or (graceDone and not carrying and not stealing) then
                restore()
            end
        end
    end)
end)()
Steal.AutoStealEnabled = true
pcall(startAutoSteal)
print("✅ 000 Duels loaded with Auto Steal enabled")
